### [1] [Evaluating Multi-Task Morphological Concept Learning for Pulmonary Nodule Malignancy Assessment in 3D CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38271)
*Namitha Narayanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在肺结节3D CT图像中联合学习放射科医生标注的形态学特征（如毛刺征、分叶征）与恶性风险是否能提升分类性能。使用LIDC-IDRI数据集，通过患者级划分训练、验证和测试集，比较单任务与多任务3D卷积神经网络模型。结果表明，单任务模型的平衡准确率为0.548，ROC-AUC为0.552；多任务模型分别为0.539和0.558。患者级自举分析显示两者差异不显著（ROC-AUC差值0.005，95%CI：-0.087~0.090）。辅助任务存在严重不平衡且预测性能有限，说明形态学特征的引入未明显提升恶性风险分类效果，强调了类别平衡、标签设计及读者级别注释结构在多任务肺CT分析中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估肺结节形态学特征（如毛刺征、分叶征）对恶性风险判断的重要性，探索将这些特征与恶性风险联合建模是否能提升分类性能。

Method: 采用患者级划分数据集，构建单任务与多任务3D卷积神经网络模型，分别预测恶性风险、毛刺征和分叶征，使用LIDC-IDRI数据集进行训练与测试，并通过患者级自举分析评估性能差异。

Result: 单任务模型的平衡准确率为0.548，ROC-AUC为0.552；多任务模型为0.539和0.558。两模型间性能差异不显著（ROC-AUC差值0.005，95%CI：-0.087~0.090），且辅助任务表现差，标签不平衡问题显著。

Conclusion: 在肺结节恶性风险分类中，联合学习形态学特征并未明显提升模型性能，提示类别平衡、标签设计及读者级注释结构在多任务学习中至关重要。

Abstract: Morphological characteristics such as spiculation and lobulation play an important role in assessing pulmonary nodules on computed tomography (CT), particularly in relation to malignancy risk. This study examines whether learning radiologist-annotated morphological features together with malignancy risk from lesion-centred 3D CT volumes improves classification performance. The Lung Image Database Consortium and Image Database Resource Initiative (LIDC-IDRI) dataset was used, comprising 3,918 reader-level nodule annotations from 742 patients after excluding indeterminate malignancy ratings. Patient-level splitting was used for training, validation, and testing, with 112 patients and 628 reader annotations in the held-out test set. A single-task 3D convolutional neural network was compared with a multi-task model predicting malignancy risk, spiculation, and lobulation. The single-task model achieved a balanced accuracy of 0.548 and receiver operating characteristic area under the curve (ROC-AUC) of 0.552, while the multi-task model achieved 0.539 and 0.558, respectively. Patient-level bootstrap analysis showed an ROC-AUC difference of 0.005 (95% confidence interval (CI): -0.087 to 0.090) and a balanced-accuracy difference of -0.009 (95% CI: -0.067 to 0.043). The auxiliary tasks were strongly imbalanced and showed limited predictive performance. Overall, including morphological features did not clearly improve malignancy-risk classification, showing the importance of class balance, label formulation, and reader-level annotation structure in multi-task pulmonary CT analysis.

</details>


### [2] [Masked Swingers: Harnessing Data Augmentation to Advance Autoencoders for Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38278)
*Anthony Fuller,Scott C. Lowe,Daniel G. Kyrollos,Graham W. Taylor,Evan Shelhamer,James R. Green*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Masked Swingers introduces a novel self-supervised learning method that enhances Masked Autoencoders (MAE) by processing two augmented views of an image, exchanging their global representations (CLS tokens) before decoding, promoting view-agnostic feature learning. It achieves significant performance gains on ImageNet-1K, fine-grained recognition tasks, and state-probing datasets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To improve the representational power and transferability of masked autoencoders by encouraging view-invariant feature learning through cross-view representation exchange.

Method: The method augments an image into two views, encodes each separately, exchanges their CLS tokens, and decodes the masked patches using the exchanged representations to learn a unified, view-agnostic summary.

Result: Masked Swingers outperforms MAE by +3-5% on ImageNet-1K kNN, achieves +45% relative gain on instance retrieval, +22% on animal re-ID, +76% on Omniglot, and reduces error by -64% on three new state-probing datasets.

Conclusion: Masked Swingers significantly advances self-supervised learning by enabling more robust and transferable representations through cross-view representation exchange, paving the way for improved world modeling.

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) removes the need for annotations and makes models that are capable across more domains than supervised learning. The autoencoder SSL framework learns by reconstructing its own input after information loss through a bottleneck or noise injection. Masked autoencoders (MAE) are the most successful instantiation of this framework: they encode a random subset of patches, then decode the masked-out patches. In this work, we introduce key modifications to improve MAEs. Our method augments an image in two different ways, then masks and encodes each view separately. It then exchanges the global representations (CLS tokens) between views before decoding the masked patches. By design, our Masked Swingers encourages learning a view-agnostic summary of the image to facilitate efficient transfer. We perform extensive experiments, and find Masked Swingers outperforms MAE by +3-5% on ImageNet-1K kNN and provides large gains on fine-grained tasks, e.g., relative gains of +45% on instance retrieval, +22% on animal re-ID, and +76% on Omniglot character recognition. To boot, Swingers reduces error -64% relative to MAE on three new state-probing datasets, opening the door to world modeling. Welcome to our Swingers party.

</details>


### [3] [GaugeVLM: Structuring Spatial Supervision with Measured Geometric Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38285)
*Hongbo Wang,Zihan Lin,Wenkui Yang,Shiran Ge,Yuang Ai,Jie Cao,Huaibo Huang,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaugeVLM introduces a method to improve vision-language models' consistency in spatial reasoning by using controlled 3D scene interventions, explicitly modeling geometric dependencies and error magnitudes. Its core objective, GaugeDPO, converts measured errors into preference margins and links answer variations to view-specific changes, leading to improved accuracy across multiple spatial metrics and domains.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing VLMs often contradict themselves across views of the same spatial relation and fail when relations change. Current training methods lack explicit supervision for error magnitude and geometric dependencies across observations.

Method: GaugeVLM uses controlled object and camera interventions in 3D scenes to generate linked observations with measurable differences in spatial relations and shared truths. GaugeDPO translates these measurements into learning signals by creating preference margins, enforcing canonical rankings across views, and aligning intervention-induced answer changes with geometric shifts via view-specific scaling.

Result: GaugeVLM outperforms supervised fine-tuning on 10 spatial reasoning metrics across three VLM backbones. The 7B model achieves +15.0 and +18.9 percentage points improvement on MSMU distance and QSpatial+, respectively. Gains also transfer to autonomous driving and embodied reasoning tasks, showing strong generalization.

Conclusion: By making geometric dependencies and error structures explicit through intervention-based data and GaugeDPO, GaugeVLM significantly enhances spatial reasoning consistency and generalization in vision-language models.

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can contradict themselves across views of the same spatial relation and fail to respond when that relation changes. Addressing these failures requires supervision that captures error magnitude and geometric dependencies across observations, both of which remain implicit in training on individual answers or ordinal preferences. Therefore, we introduce GaugeVLM, which makes this structure explicit through controlled object and camera interventions in explicit 3D scenes, producing linked observations with measured differences between spatial relations and shared truths across views. To translate this structure into learning signals, its core objective, GaugeDPO, converts measured errors into preference margins, directly supervises correct canonical rankings across views, and links intervention-induced answer-odds contrasts to measured relation changes with view-specific scales. Our analysis bounds canonical prediction error and establishes that the cross-view and intervention constraints can be jointly satisfied. Empirically, GaugeVLM improves all 10 established spatial metrics over supervised fine-tuning across three VLM backbones, with the main 7B model gaining 15.0 and 18.9 percentage points on MSMU distance and QSpatial+, respectively. These gains also extend to autonomous driving and embodied reasoning, demonstrating the robust generalization across domains.

</details>


### [4] [It Takes Little to Rewrite Perception: Targeted Semantic Substitution in Vision-Language Models at $ε\leq 4/255$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38298)
*Binchi Zhang,Atrisha Sarkar,Apurva Narayan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型（VLMs）在安全关键场景下的鲁棒性，发现现有基于表示对齐的攻击在小扰动（ε ≤ 4/255）下效果有限，但通过在后融合标记空间中对齐源图像与目标图像的流特征，实现了在相同扰动范围内的语义替换攻击。实验表明，在ε = 2/255时即可成功引入目标语义，ε = 4/255时完成替换率达38%；视频任务中ε = 1/255时完成替换率达35.9%。此外观察到‘语义融合’现象，即大语言模型将矛盾的视觉信号合理化为连贯叙述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估视觉语言模型在小扰动下的可控制性，揭示其看似鲁棒实则易受语义级攻击的本质，以提升对模型可信度的理解。

Method: 在白盒威胁模型下，将源图像与目标图像在受害者VLM的后融合标记空间中进行流对齐，实现跨模态语义替换；采用严格成功标准：模型需同时命名目标、确认其存在并否认源图像。

Result: 在ε = 2/255时目标语义已出现，ε = 4/255时图像完整替换率达38%；视频任务中ε = 1/255时完成替换率达35.9%；观察到大语言模型将矛盾视觉输入整合为一致叙述的‘语义融合’现象。

Conclusion: 尽管现有攻击在小扰动下表现不佳，但本文提出的语义替换攻击在相同扰动范围内显著有效，表明VLMs在语义层面并不具备预期鲁棒性，且存在语义融合风险，亟需加强防御机制。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) are widely deployed in safety-critical scenarios, and understanding to which extent they can be controlled by adversarial perturbation is a prerequisite for evaluating their trustworthiness. Existing representation-alignment attacks, which make a VLM perceive a target image, achieve limited success at $\varepsilon \leq 4/255$. Therefore, VLMs seems robust to perturbations in this range. We show that this robustness does not hold, as targeted semantic substitution succeeds within the same range. Specifically, we align each stream of the source image with its counterpart in the target image in the victim VLM's post-merger token space, operating under a white-box threat model. We evaluate under a strict success criterion, requiring the model to simultaneously name the target, confirm its presence, and deny the source. In images, target semantics appear at $\varepsilon = 2/255$ and complete replacement reaches 38\% at $\varepsilon = 4/255$. On video, complete replacement reaches 35.9\% at $\varepsilon = 1/255$. We also observe a phenomenon of \textit{semantic fusion}, where Large Language Model (LLM) rationalizes contradictory visual signals into a coherent narrative.

</details>


### [5] [Strike a Chord! Modal Kinetic Typography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38325)
*Maham Tanveer,Jiyeon Han,Nanxuan Zhao,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为模态动力字体（modal kinetic typography）的新方法，通过利用矢量字形的自然振动模式来实现语义概念的动画表达，同时保持可读性。该方法基于有限元特征值问题，从字形轮廓中提取最柔韧的模式，包括整体和各部分的变形模式，并通过闭式解处理刚体平移与旋转，使各部件可作为整体移动。使用冻结的视频扩散模型仅监督这些模式的振幅与相位，从而生成平滑、无缝循环的运动。相比现有方法，该方法在避免轮廓撕裂、减少抖动方面表现更优，且无需依赖特定类别的骨架或关键点先验，仅需一个由语言模型生成的字母部件列表作为轻量级先验。形状与运动在构造上被解耦：单一基础轮廓用于概念塑造，而模态驱动不会改变形状，因此可在不重塑字形的情况下进行动画。实验表明，该方法在概念对齐度、动画流畅性及人类偏好方面优于Dynamic Typography、AniClipart和Astra（GPT-6）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态字体方法存在两大缺陷：一是基于自由点优化的方法（如视频分数蒸馏SDS）在每帧独立更新点位置时容易导致轮廓撕裂和抖动；二是结构化方法依赖于类别特定的骨骼或关键点先验，缺乏通用性。本文旨在构建一种既保持字形可读性又具备自然流畅动画的通用方法，实现形状与运动的解耦，提升动画质量与语义表达能力。

Method: 提出基于模态分析的动态字体生成框架。首先，从矢量字形轮廓构建有限元特征值问题，求解其低阶振动模式（即柔韧变形模式），并分离零能量解（刚体平移与旋转）以支持部件整体运动。随后，利用冻结的视频扩散模型仅指导这些模式的振幅与相位，确保运动平滑且无缝循环。整个过程不修改原始字形轮廓，实现形状与运动的显式解耦。

Result: 所提方法在多个指标上优于现有方法：生成的动画更加流畅、更具表现力，显著减少轮廓撕裂现象；在概念对齐度上达到或超越对比方法；在人类评估中，无论是否允许形状变化，均获得更高偏好评分，甚至优于Astra（GPT-6）。

Conclusion: 模态动力字体通过从字形自身结构出发提取自然振动模式，实现了形状与运动的解耦，生成了更平滑、更自然且语义表达更强的动画效果。该方法具有良好的通用性与可扩展性，为动态字体设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce modal kinetic typography, which animates a vector glyph to express a semantic concept while keeping it legible. Our key idea is to build motion from the glyph's natural vibration modes. Specifically, a finite-element eigenproblem assembled from the vector outline yields the glyph's softest modes, for the whole letter and for each of its parts, allowing it to bend. The problem's zero-energy solutions, i.e., rigid translations and rotations, are applied in closed form to each part, allowing parts to also move as blocks. To animate the glyph, a frozen video diffusion model supervises only the modes' amplitudes and phases. Our modal approach addresses two weaknesses of prior work. Free-form point optimization under video score distillation (SDS) moves each point and frame independently along noisy gradients, tearing the outline and causing jitter. In contrast, our modes are smooth along the outline and driven by a few whole-cycle harmonics, which restricts these gradients to smooth, seamlessly looping motion. On the other hand, structured alternatives rely on skeletons or keypoints from category-specific priors, whereas our modes come from the glyph itself; the only prior is a list naming each letter's moving parts, generated once for the whole alphabet by a language model. In modal kinetic typography, shape and motion are disentangled by construction: a single base outline is sculpted toward the concept, and the modal drive cannot alter it, so a letter can also be animated without being reshaped. Our method produces more articulated and smoother motion than Dynamic Typography and AniClipart at comparable or better concept alignment, with less glyph tearing than Dynamic Typography, and is preferred by human raters, including in a frozen-shape setting where motion alone must carry the concept. Our results were also preferred over Astra (GPT-6) by human raters.

</details>


### [6] [ExploreNet: Learning Where to Explore in Diffusion GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38329)
*Shuyue Stella Li,Xiaochuang Han,Yulia Tsvetkov,Luke Zettlemoyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EXPLORENET提出一种自适应探索分布，通过学习每个潜在元素的噪声尺度来改进扩散模型的强化学习训练。相比传统均匀噪声，该方法能更有效地引导探索，显著提升生成质量与人类偏好得分，在多个基准上超越Flow-GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组相对强化学习方法使用各向同性高斯噪声进行探索，但这种噪声对所有通道和空间位置一视同仁，忽略了潜在表示中不同元素对图像生成的影响差异。因此需要更智能、自适应的探索策略以提高学习效率和生成质量。

Method: 引入EXPLORENET，一个基于当前潜在表示、去噪步骤和提示信息预测每个潜在元素噪声尺度的策略网络；该网络在无奖励情况下训练，仅用于学习探索分布，训练后丢弃，不影响推理过程。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium上，EXPLORENET使Hold-out GenEval2性能提升14%；在两个独立组合基准和五个偏好/图像质量模型上表现良好；达到67.2%的人类偏好胜率；实验表明探索可学习，探索分布的形状比幅度更重要，高质量滚动生成优于数量。

Conclusion: 探索在组相对扩散强化学习中是可学习的，且探索分布的结构设计至关重要，自适应探索显著优于固定均匀噪声，能有效提升生成质量和泛化能力。

Abstract: Group-relative RL methods such as Flow-GRPO post-train image generators by exploring with isotropic Gaussian noise added at every denoising step. This noise decides which rollouts the model learns from, yet it perturbs every channel and spatial position of the latent equally. In this paper, we instead show that latent elements differ in how much they change the generated image, so exploration should adapt to these differences. We introduce EXPLORENET to learn an adaptive exploration distribution. EXPLORENET is a policy that predicts a noise scale for every latent element from the current latent, the denoising step, and the prompt, before any reward is observed; it is trained on the reward spread of each rollout group and discarded after training, leaving inference unchanged. On Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, EXPLORENET improves held-out GenEval2 by 14% over Flow-GRPO, transfers to two independent compositional benchmarks and five preference and image-quality models, and reaches a 67.2% human preference win-rate. Overall, across our group-relative diffusion RL experiments, we find that exploration is learnable, the shape of the exploration distribution outweighs its magnitude, and rollout quality is more effective than rollout quantity.

</details>


### [7] [Learning Semantic Inpainting for Animatable Gaussian Head Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38343)
*Pilseo Park,Fizza Rubab,Yiying Tong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SInGA方法，通过UV空间中的语义补全实现单图驱动的可动画高斯头像生成。利用人脸对称性与UV拓扑结构，从可见区域提取特征补全不可见区域，并通过多高斯堆叠增强细节，实现高质量、身份一致且可动画的头像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖多视角数据，在单视角设置下难以有效处理未观察区域，限制了应用范围。

Method: 在UV空间中设计语义补全框架，利用人脸对称性和一致的空间对应关系，从可见区域特征推断并补全不可见区域，结合多高斯表示进行属性回归，完成高斯补全。

Result: 生成的头像在完整性、身份保真度和跨视角一致性方面表现优异，支持真实动画与渲染，且无需针对个体进行优化。

Conclusion: SInGA实现了从单张图像生成高质量、可动画的高斯头像，具备良好的泛化能力与视觉质量。

Abstract: We present SInGA, a novel method for learning Semantic Inpainting for animatable Gaussian head Avatars from a single image. Existing avatar approaches often rely on multi-view observations and lack effective handling of unobserved regions in single-view settings, limiting their applicability in such scenarios. To address this, we propose a semantic inpainting framework defined in UV space for completing unobserved facial regions. Our key insight lies in the structured topology of the UV representation, which provides consistent spatial correspondences and enables reliable completion of identity-specific features using the inherent symmetry cues of human faces. We extract features from observed regions and use them to complete unobserved regions. The completed representation is then used to regress Gaussian attributes, effectively performing Gaussian inpainting. In addition, instead of relying on a single Gaussian at each surface or pixel location, we stack multiple Gaussians to enhance detail. The resulting avatar generalizes across identities without requiring per-identity optimization and can be animated with driving inputs. Experimental results show that our method generates high-quality head avatars with improved completeness and identity preservation, while supporting realistic animation and consistent rendering from unobserved views.

</details>


### [8] [TrackFish3D: Self-Supervised 3D Tracking of Schooling Fish from Multi-view Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38347)
*Patt Phurtivilai,Zhiyang Dou,Yifan Wu,Kinfung Chu,Yuan Liu,Lei Yang,Wenping Wang,Taku Komura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrackFish3D 是一种基于几何的自监督框架，用于密集的多相机3D鱼群追踪。它利用校准的多视角几何信息作为监督信号，通过三角测量和重投影一致性生成伪关联，并结合几何编码器与全局关联变换器学习帧内所有视角间的对应关系。为使关联具有身份感知能力，引入自监督对比目标以在嵌入空间中分离共可见个体，并使用时间预测器保持身份并连接跨帧的短时遮挡。该模型仅需一次训练即可直接应用于未见测试视频，无需交叉视角身份标签、时间标注、3D真值、外观特征或测试时优化。在基准测试中，3D多目标跟踪准确率从基线的87.7%提升至95.8%；在3D-ZeF斑马鱼基准上达到81.1% MOTA，优于最佳几何基线的77.4%。该方法还可泛化至真实世界鸟类追踪任务，表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角3D鱼群行为量化需要精确轨迹，但因频繁遮挡、个体外观相似以及身份标注稀缺，多视角3D追踪仍具挑战性。现有方法依赖外观重识别或人工标注身份，限制了其在无标签数据上的应用。因此，亟需一种无需依赖外观特征或人工标注的身份感知自监督追踪方法。

Method: TrackFish3D 采用几何驱动的自监督学习框架：通过校准的多视角几何（如三角测量与重投影一致性）生成伪关联；使用几何编码器和全局关联变换器建模跨视角对应关系；引入自监督对比损失，使共可见个体在嵌入空间中可区分；设计时间预测器以维持身份连续性并处理短暂遮挡。整个模型在无标签视频上端到端训练，无需任何额外标注或测试阶段优化。

Result: 在自建基准上，TrackFish3D 将3D多目标跟踪准确率从最强基线的87.7%提升至95.8%；在3D-ZeF斑马鱼基准上实现81.1% MOTA，优于最佳几何基线的77.4%；且在真实世界鸟类追踪任务中也表现出色，证明其良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: TrackFish3D 成功构建了一个无需外观特征与人工标注的自监督3D多目标追踪框架，通过几何先验与自监督机制实现了高精度、强泛化能力的鱼群及其它动物追踪，为复杂群体行为研究提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Quantifying collective fish behavior requires accurate trajectories, yet multi-view 3D tracking remains challenging due to frequent occlusions, visually similar individuals, and the long-standing scarcity of identity annotations. We present TrackFish3D, a geometry-driven self-supervised framework for dense multi-camera 3D tracking of schooling fish. Instead of relying on appearance-based re-identification or manually annotated identities, TrackFish3D turns calibrated multi-view geometry into supervision: triangulation and reprojection consistency provide pseudo-associations, while a geometric encoder and global association transformer learn all-to-all cross-view correspondence within each frame. To make these associations identity-aware, TrackFish3D introduces a self-supervised contrastive objective that separates co-visible individuals in the embedding space, together with a temporal predictor that preserves identities and bridges short occlusions across frames. The resulting model is trained once on unlabeled footage and applied directly to unseen test videos, requiring no cross-view identity labels, temporal annotations, 3D ground truth, appearance features, or test-time optimization. On our benchmark, TrackFish3D improves 3D Multi-Object Tracking Accuracy from 87.7% for the strongest baseline to 95.8%. On the 3D-ZeF zebrafish benchmark, it achieves 81.1% MOTA, compared with 77.4% for the best geometric baseline. TrackFish3D also generalizes beyond fish, achieving strong results on real-world bird tracking.

</details>


### [9] [Inductive Visual Logic for Few-Shot Out-Of-Distribution Adaptation in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38362)
*Hung-Jen Chen,Yu-Heng Ho,Ting-Yao Huang,Po-Hsiang Hsu,Li-Yu Chen,Chun-Yi Lee,Min Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的框架Inductive Visual Logic（IVL），利用生成式视觉语言模型在远距离分布外（distant OOD）任务中虽无法分类但仍能描述视觉特征的能力，通过双模提示从少量支持图像中提取视觉属性并构建类别级属性字典，在推理时通过层次化过滤识别空间定位的属性证据，实现高准确率且可解释的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准适应方法无法解决生成式视觉语言模型在专业领域因缺乏学习判别特征而导致的性能下降问题，因为它们受限于编码器现有的特征空间；而这些模型即使在判别能力崩溃时仍保留较强的描述能力，因此可以利用这种不对称性来构建新的分类知识。

Method: IVL采用双模提示机制，结合语义描述与原始视觉观察，从少量支持图像中提取视觉属性，并构建每类的属性字典；在推理阶段，通过层级过滤策略识别空间上定位的属性证据以进行分类。

Result: 在多个远距离OOD基准测试中，IVL在两种VLM骨干网络下均实现了最高的综合准确率，并生成了可解释、可追溯属性的预测结果。

Conclusion: IVL成功利用生成式视觉语言模型的描述能力弥补其判别能力的不足，为解决远离预训练分布的任务提供了有效且可解释的新范式。

Abstract: Generative vision-language models (VLMs) such as Qwen-VL and LLaVA achieve strong zero-shot performance on tasks overlapping with their pretraining distribution, yet fail on specialized domains where the required discriminative features were never learned, a regime we term distant out-of-distribution (OOD). Standard adaptation methods cannot overcome this representational absence because they operate within the encoder's existing feature space. However, VLMs retain a robust descriptive capacity even when discrimination collapses: a model that cannot classify a medical scan can still articulate its visual patterns. Exploiting this asymmetry, we introduce Inductive Visual Logic (IVL), a training-free framework that constructs classification knowledge from the model's surviving descriptive ability. IVL extracts visual traits from few-shot support images through dual-mode prompting, combining semantic descriptions with primitive visual observations, and organizes them into per-class trait dictionaries. At inference, hierarchical filtering identifies spatially grounded trait evidence for classification. Across multiple distant-OOD benchmarks, IVL achieves the highest aggregate accuracy under two VLM backbones while producing interpretable, trait-traceable predictions.

</details>


### [10] [Composition, Not Conversation: VLMs Lose the Scene, Not the Thread](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38368)
*L. D. M. S. Sai Teja,Ufaq Khan,N. Siva Gopala Krishna,Satyajit Tourani,Ashshak Sharifdeen,Fida Mohammad Thoker,Bernard Ghanem,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Layered-VQA基准，通过将图像分解为有序的RGBA层并设计分层问题，研究视觉语言模型在信息碎片化情况下的表现。实验发现模型存在三大缺陷：组合损失（场景碎片化显著降低准确率）、归因倒置（即使选择最优证据仍表现不佳）和定位退化（随着所需证据增多，定位能力下降快于答案准确率）。结果表明，仅提供正确视觉证据不足，其呈现方式与整体场景结构同样关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VQA基准通常一次性提供完整图像和问题，但真实场景中视觉证据常被分步揭示或碎片化。本文旨在探究当信息以碎片形式呈现时，视觉语言模型如何受影响，从而揭示模型在复杂视觉推理中的局限性。

Method: 构建Layered-VQA数据集，包含93个场景和300个问题，每个图像被分解为可重构的有序RGBA层；每个问题标注支持层、最小充分层和干扰层；评估11个开源VLM（3B至32B参数）及2个专有模型，基于187,200次对话和174万次跨模型评估进行分析。

Result: 模型在场景碎片化下准确率显著下降，重组图层后性能基本恢复；即使使用最优证据，表现仍劣于完整图像；随着所需证据增多，模型的视觉定位能力退化速度远超答案准确率。

Conclusion: 仅提供正确的视觉证据不足以保证模型有效推理。证据的组织方式与原始场景的整体结构密切相关，模型需要上下文完整的视觉语境才能实现有效理解与定位。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly reason over visual evidence that is cropped, segmented, retrieved, or revealed over time. Yet most VQA benchmarks present the complete image and question at once. We ask what models lose when the same information is fragmented. We introduce Layered-VQA, with 93 scenes and 300 questions. Each image is decomposed into ordered RGBA layers that exactly recompose the original scene, and each question is annotated with supporting, minimal-sufficient, and distractor layers. We evaluate eleven open-weight VLMs from 3B to 32B parameters and two proprietary models with a scale of 187,200 conversations, graded by 1.74M open-model cross-judgments. We find three consistent failures. Loss in Composition: fragmenting the question has a small effect, but fragmenting the scene substantially reduces accuracy; recomposing the same layers largely restores performance. Oracle Inversion: even oracle-selected sufficient evidence can perform worse than the complete scene. Loss in Grounding: as more evidence is required, grounding degrades much faster than answer accuracy. Together, these results show that having the right visual evidence is not enough. How that evidence is composed and presented determines whether models can use and ground it. The right evidence is not enough: VLMs need the scene it came from.

</details>


### [11] [PhyProbe: Rethinking Physical Consistency Evaluation in Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38377)
*Max Ku,Jiaojiao Fan,Zekun Hao,Francesco Ferroni,Heng Wang,Wenhu Chen,Ming-Yu Liu,Prithvijit Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhyProbe，一种基于冻结的时空编码器和轻量级评分头的视频物理一致性评估方法，通过联合优化排序、回归和锚定校准，在多种场景下优于现有方法，与人类判断高度相关，并在无显式偏好标签的情况下表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成质量评估方法依赖于视觉-语言模型或微调评估器，前者对物理动态不敏感，后者易过拟合特定数据集且泛化能力差；同时，监督信号通常只提供相对排序或绝对分数，难以在不同设置中保持一致性和可靠性。

Method: PhyProbe利用冻结的预训练时空编码器提取特征，通过轻量级评分头映射为物理一致性违规得分；采用统一目标函数，结合成对排序、噪声标量回归以及跨异构监督源的锚定校准进行训练。

Result: PhyProbe在真实-生成和生成-生成配对的多数成对基准测试中表现更优，尤其在无对应关系和生成-生成场景下显著优于现有方法；与人类判断高度相关，排名和线性指标一致，得分稳定在[0,1]区间；即使未使用显式偏好标签，也能在人类偏好基准上表现良好。

Conclusion: PhyProbe能够有效评估视频物理一致性，具备强泛化能力、良好的可解释性与与人类感知的一致性，且无需显式偏好标注即可实现高质量评价。

Abstract: Evaluating the physical consistency of generated videos remains a fundamental challenge. Existing approaches rely on off-the-shelf vision-language models, which can often be myopic to physical dynamics, or fine-tuned evaluators trained on human annotations, which overfit to dataset-specific cues and fail to generalize. A key challenge is that existing supervision sources provide either relative ordering or absolute scores, but not both reliably and consistently across varied settings. To this end, we introduce PhyProbe, an evaluator that extracts features from a frozen pretrained spatio-temporal encoder and maps them to a scalar physical consistency violation score via a lightweight scoring head. PhyProbe is trained through a unified objective combining pairwise ranking, regression on noisy scalar annotations, and anchor-based calibration over a curated set of heterogeneous supervision sources. Experiments show that PhyProbe outperforms prior methods on most pairwise benchmarks spanning real-generated and generated-generated pairs under varying correspondence, with the largest gains in no-correspondence and generated-generated settings where existing fine-tuned evaluators degrade sharply. PhyProbe achieves strong correlation with human judgments, with close agreement between rank-based and linear metrics, indicating that scores are both well ordered and anchored to a stable [0, 1] scale. Further, despite being trained on supervision indicative of physical consistency, without explicit general-preference labels, PhyProbe also performs competitively on human preference benchmarks: consistent with the observation that physics violations are entangled with broader quality degradations.

</details>


### [12] [Team MSU GenText-Forensics Challenge 2026 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38391)
*Kirill Koltsov,Aleksandr Gushchin,Dmitriy Vatolin,Anastasia Antsiferova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分解式思维链（CoT）流水线，用于多语言文本图像的文档伪造检测与溯源。该方法结合文档篡改检测器（DTD）与两个经LoRA微调的Qwen3-VL-32B视觉语言模型，分别执行区域筛选与语义异常分析，最终生成包含证据的人类可读报告。通过蒸馏来自大模型（Qwen3-VL-235B）的思维链轨迹进行训练，系统在ACM MM 2026 GenText-Forensics挑战中获得第三名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代文档伪造已超越像素级修改，涉及内容语义层面的篡改，且针对OCR与LLM处理流程，传统方法难以有效识别此类复杂攻击，亟需能定位篡改点、识别攻击类型并生成可解释报告的系统化解决方案。

Method: 采用分解式思维链架构：首先使用DTD生成篡改概率图并提取候选区域；由第一模型（Filterer）验证区域并初步分类伪造类型；第二模型（Semantic Detective）融合并重新定位有效区域，检测肉眼不可见的语义异常，并撰写最终报告。两模型通过知识蒸馏从大型教师模型（Qwen3-VL-235B）获取带注释的思维链轨迹进行训练。

Result: 在ACM MM 2026 GenText-Forensics挑战中取得第三名成绩。实验验证了数据准备、测试时增强、区域渲染、蒸馏策略及训练配置的有效性，并进行了关于检测阈值、提示设计和流水线结构的消融研究。

Conclusion: 所提出的分解式思维链方法能够高效应对复杂文档伪造攻击，在检测精度、定位能力与报告可解释性方面表现优异，具备较强的实用潜力，尤其适用于需要高可信度输出的司法、金融等敏感场景。

Abstract: Document text forgery has evolved beyond simple pixel-level manipulation: modern attacks alter not only the appearance of a document but also its meaning, and increasingly target the OCR & LLM pipelines that consume such documents. The ACM MM 2026 GenText-Forensics challenge therefore requires systems that not only decide whether a multilingual text image is forged, but also localize the point of manipulation, identify the attack type, and produce a human-readable forensic report with supporting evidence. We present our solution, a decomposed chain-of-thought (CoT) pipeline that combines a document tampering detector (DTD) with two Qwen3-VL-32B vision-language models, each LoRA-adapted to a distinct sub-task. DTD produces tampering probability maps that are converted into numbered candidate regions; a first model (the Filterer) validates these regions and assigns a preliminary forgery type, while a second model (the Semantic Detective) merges and re-grounds the surviving regions, searches for purely semantic anomalies that are invisible to pixel-level detectors, and writes the final report. Both models are trained by distilling chain-of-thought traces from a privileged Qwen3-VL-235B teacher that has access to ground-truth masks and reports. Our approach secured third place in the ACM MM 2026 GenText-Forensics challenge. We describe the data preparation, test-time augmentation, region rendering, distillation protocol, and training configuration in detail, and report ablations over detector thresholds, prompt designs, and pipeline decompositions.

</details>


### [13] [VidHarness: Evolving Agent Harnesses for Cost-Efficient Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38413)
*Susan Liang,Jianmin Wu,Daxiang Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VidHarness 是一个自动化框架，用于设计高效处理长视频的视频代理（video agents），通过蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）和不确定性感知的多保真度验证来优化筛选，支持按帧预算动态选择最优代理，显著提升效率与性能，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频代理依赖人工设计，成本高且难以优化；处理小时级视频时逐帧分析不现实，而关键信息通常集中在少数几秒内，因此需要自动化的、高效的视频理解框架。

Method: 提出 VidHarness 框架，结合 MCTS 进行全局搜索以避免局部最优，并采用不确定性感知的多保真度验证降低评估开销；引入混合代理（mixture-of-harness）根据帧预算动态路由问题至最适配的代理。

Result: 在 LongVideoBench、Video-MME、Video-Holmes 上达到新 SOTA，相比最强手工代理提升最高达 11.2 分点；在知识密集型任务 Video-MMMU 和 MMVU 上，使用少于一半的帧即实现良好表现。

Conclusion: VidHarness 实现了自动化、低成本、高性能的长视频理解，为视频代理的设计提供了可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can answer questions about hour-long videos, but processing every frame is prohibitively expensive, even though the evidence for a question usually spans only a few seconds. Video agents, i.e., harness programs wrapped around a frozen VLM, address this by observing the video selectively, yet existing harnesses are hand-crafted by experts through slow build-and-test cycles. We propose VidHarness, a framework that automates harness design for cost-efficient long video understanding, in which a harness proposer iteratively evolves harnesses based on execution feedback from an evolution environment. To escape the local optima of greedy refinement, we organize the evolution as Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS), and to reduce the evaluation cost, we integrate uncertainty-aware multi-fidelity validation, which screens new harnesses on a few questions and promotes only the promising ones. Since the best harness varies with the frame budget, we further introduce a mixture-of-harness that routes each question to a harness specialized for its budget. VidHarness sets new state-of-the-art results on LongVideoBench, Video-MME, and Video-Holmes, outperforms the strongest hand-crafted video agent by up to $11.2$ points, and generalizes to the knowledge-intensive benchmarks Video-MMMU and MMVU with fewer than half of the frames of uniform sampling.

</details>


### [14] [LoopVL: Recurrent Visual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38426)
*Zhe Qian,Ziyang Gong,Zhongxing Xu,Hehan Li,Zhonghua Wang,Fei Luo,Mingxuan Wang,Xue Yang,Shiwei liu,Yanbiao Ma,Junchi Yan,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopVL 是一种基于循环变压器的视觉-语言模型，通过模块级和模型级的循环计算，迭代更新统一的视觉-语言状态。它在多个多模态理解与视觉推理任务上优于同类规模及更大规模的非循环模型，并观察到显著的视觉顿悟时刻（Visual Aha Moments），表明共享参数可支持更深层次的多模态计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索循环变压器在视觉-语言模型中的有效性，提升多模态理解能力并揭示共享参数在持续演化的视觉-语言状态中的作用。

Method: 结合模块级循环（Module-Loop）与模型级循环（Model-Loop）计算，通过语言预训练、多模态训练和后训练从零开始训练模型，实现对统一视觉-语言状态的迭代更新。

Result: LoopVL 在多模态理解与视觉推理基准测试中表现优于多种类似或更大规模的非循环模型，并在推理过程中观察到明显的视觉注意力跃迁现象（Visual Aha Moments）。

Conclusion: LoopVL 为循环视觉-语言建模提供了实际证据，展示了共享参数如何支持对不断演化的视觉-语言状态进行更深入的多模态计算，为未来多模态模型设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: We introduce LoopVL to study whether Loop Transformers can be effectively extended to vision- language models. LoopVL combines Module-Loop and Model-Loop computation to iteratively update a unified vision-language state through shared modules. We train LoopVL from scratch through language pre-training, multimodal training, and post-training. LoopVL outperforms a range of similarly sized and larger non-recurrent models on multimodal understanding and visual reasoning benchmarks. We also observe Visual Aha Moments in LoopVL, characterized by pronounced shifts in visual attention across loops. LoopVL provides practical evidence for recurrent vision-language modeling and offers an intuitive perspective on how shared parameters can support deeper multimodal computation over continuously evolving visual-language states.

</details>


### [15] [MOBA-VL: Event-Localized Multi-Turn Reinforcement Learning for Real-Time MOBA Commentary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38428)
*Shengyun Zhong,Xinkang Zhao,Ziyuan Chu,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MOBA-VL，一个90亿参数的视觉语言模型，通过游戏遥测数据作为监督信号，采用事件定位的多轮强化学习训练，以提升实时电竞解说中对关键事件（如击杀、目标达成）的捕捉能力。研究构建了MOBACast数据集（860场职业比赛，约460小时）和MOBACast-Bench基准，实验表明MOBA-VL在完整比赛和片段上的综合得分均显著优于现有模型（如StreamingVLM、DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash），事件召回率从34.5%提升至42.1%。代码与演示将公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时电竞解说视觉语言模型虽流畅自然，但常遗漏关键比赛事件（如击杀、目标夺取），影响解说准确性。为解决此问题，需引入更精确的事件监督信号。

Method: 利用游戏遥测数据（精确记录事件发生时间）作为监督信号，训练90亿参数的MOBA-VL模型；采用事件定位的多轮强化学习，奖励准确描述每个事件的生成回合；构建大规模标注数据集MOBACast及评估基准MOBACast-Bench。

Result: 在MOBACast-Bench上，MOBA-VL在完整比赛（63.25 vs. 55.12）和片段（63.45 vs. 56.22）的综合评分上均优于现有模型；事件召回率从34.5%提升至42.1%，显著增强对关键事件的识别能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入游戏遥测作为事件监督信号并结合事件定位的强化学习，MOBA-VL实现了更准确、更及时的实时电竞解说，在关键事件捕捉和整体表现上均超越现有方法，为未来实时赛事解说系统提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Real-time commentary for Multiplayer Online Battle Arena (MOBA) esports requires a vision-language model (VLM) to narrate a live match second by second, both fluently and accurately. Existing streaming VLMs sound natural but often miss key events such as kills and objectives. To address this limitation, we use game telemetry, which records exactly when each event occurs, as a supervision signal. We introduce MOBA-VL, a 9B-parameter model trained on this signal with event-localized multi-turn reinforcement learning, which rewards the turns that describe each event. We also collect MOBACast, 860 professional matches (about 460 hours) across three MOBA games with word-level timestamped commentary, and MOBACast-Bench, a benchmark from held-out tournaments. On MOBACast-Bench, MOBA-VL achieves the highest Overall score on full matches (63.25 vs. 55.12 for StreamingVLM) and clips (63.45 vs. 56.22 for DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash). Event-localized credit also raises event recall from 34.5 to 42.1 over supervised fine-tuning. Code and data will be released, and demos are available on an anonymous project page at https://moba-vl.github.io.

</details>


### [16] [Audible World Models: Spatially Aware Sound Generation for 3D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38444)
*Duowen Chen,Jinjin He,Gouthaman KV,Sandeep Bangalore Venkatesh,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Audible World Models，一种无需训练的框架，将声音融入文本和图像条件下的3D世界生成中。该框架通过构建全景3D代理、分割语义层、识别声源对象与背景区域，并为每个声音标签合成干音频，再基于几何声学传播渲染随听众位置变化的空间音频，实现声音与场景的精确空间对齐和动态适应。实验表明，在80个生成场景中，其空间一致性显著优于基于文本、视频和全景图的基线方法，同时保持良好的语义一致性。人类评估和VLM评价显示，该方法在音画一致性、空间合理性及运动依赖行为方面更受青睐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本和图像条件下的3D世界生成模型通常缺乏对声音空间位置的显式建模，导致音频无法随观察者移动而变化，难以实现真实的听觉体验。因此，需要一种能够将声音与几何结构和语义信息精确关联的框架，以实现空间一致且动态响应的音频渲染。

Method: 1. 从文本提示生成全景3D代理；2. 对代理进行语义分层分割；3. 识别前景中的声源物体与背景环境区域；4. 为每个声音标签合成干音频；5. 将声源锚定于重建的几何结构上；6. 利用几何声学传播模型渲染随听众视角和运动变化的空间音频。

Result: 在80个生成场景中，所提方法在空间一致性方面显著优于文本、视频和全景图条件下的基线模型；同时在语义对齐方面表现良好。人类评估和基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的评价均表明，生成的音频在音画一致性、空间合理性以及运动依赖性方面更优，用户体验更佳。

Conclusion: Audible World Models成功实现了声音与3D世界状态的显式耦合，无需训练即可生成具有空间感知能力和动态适应性的音频，显著提升虚拟世界的沉浸感与真实感，为未来多模态生成系统提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Text- and image-conditioned world generators can create visually rich 3D environments, yet these worlds often remain silent or rely on soundtracks synthesized solely from text or rendered video. Although such audio can convey what should be heard, it lacks an explicit representation of where sound sources are located and how their perceived sound should vary with listener movement. We introduce Audible World Models, a training-free framework that incorporates sound into the generated world state. Starting from a text prompt, our system constructs a panoramic 3D proxy, separates it into semantic layers, identifies sound-producing foreground objects and ambient background regions, and synthesizes dry audio for each sound label. It then anchors these sources to reconstructed geometry and renders listener-dependent spatial audio using geometric acoustic propagation. By explicitly linking semantics, geometry, and sound propagation, the framework maintains persistent source locations while adapting the rendered audio to changes in listener viewpoint and motion. Experiments across 80 generated scenes demonstrate substantial gains in spatial consistency over text-, video-, and panorama-conditioned baselines, while preserving competitive semantic alignment. VLM-based assessments and human evaluations further indicate that our soundtracks are preferred for their audio-visual consistency, spatial plausibility, and motion-dependent behavior.

</details>


### [17] [PAMI: Part Anchored Motion for Text to Human-Object Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38466)
*Chuqiao Li,Xianghui Xie,Yong Cao,Andreas Geiger,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PAMI提出一种基于身体部位锚点投票的运动框架，用于生成与文本条件匹配的全身人-物交互。通过将物体运动相对于多个身体部位锚点表达，并利用PamiVAE学习交互潜在空间，实现粗到细的交互生成。PamiGen生成粗粒度交互，PamiRefiner则结合长距离探测和短距离传感器，细化接触几何。在InterAct数据集上，PAMI相比先前方法提升了14.5%的接触召回率，验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将人和物体视为独立轨迹，隐式学习动态关系，常导致物体漂移、接触缺失和穿透问题。需要更精确的时空协调机制来提升人-物交互生成质量。

Method: 提出PAMI框架，采用身体部位锚点投票机制表达物体运动，构建结构化潜在空间；使用PamiVAE学习交互潜变量；通过PamiGen生成粗粒度交互，再由PamiRefiner利用混合表面感知表示（长程探针+短程传感器）递归优化细粒度接触几何。

Result: 在InterAct数据集上，PAMI生成的人-物交互更忠实，人类相对物体运动更准确，接触召回率比之前最优方法高出14.5%。消融实验验证了身体部位锚点投票和混合表面感知精炼的有效性。

Conclusion: PAMI通过身体部位锚点投票和混合表面感知精炼，有效解决了人-物交互生成中的物体漂移、接触缺失等问题，显著提升了生成质量和精度，为文本条件下的全身影像交互生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Text-conditioned full-body human-object interaction (HOI) generation requires synthesizing human motion and object trajectories that match the input text while remaining precisely coordinated over time. Most methods represent the human and object as separate trajectories and predict the global human-object couplings. Learning this complex, dynamically changing relationship implicitly, however, often yields object drift, missed contact, and penetration. We introduce PAMI, a Part-Anchored Motion framework for Interaction generation. Inspired by the classic Hough Transform, our key idea is to localize object motion by letting body-part anchors vote for it: we express object motion relative to multiple body-part anchors and use PamiVAE to learn an interaction latent space, decoding frame-wise weights that aggregate these part-specific votes. Building on this representation, PAMI generates interactions in a coarse-to-fine hierarchy. PamiGen first generates a coarse human-object interaction from text in this structured latent space, and PamiRefiner then recursively resolves fine-grained contact geometry using a hybrid surface-sensing representation, combining long-range probes that capture overall body-part influence with short-range sensors that resolve detailed contacts near the object surface. Experiments on InterAct show that PAMI generates more faithful interactions and more accurate human-relative object motion than previous methods, achieving 14.5% higher contact recall than the previous state of the art. Extensive ablations validate the contributions of both the part-anchored voting representation and hybrid surface-sensing refinement.

</details>


### [18] [Curating Synthetic Data for Task-Specific Visual Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38476)
*Saptarshi Neil Sinha,Paul Julius Kühn,Michael Weinmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在特定视觉任务中，如何通过精心设计的合成数据来提升模型性能，强调重点不在于生成更多数据，而在于选择和设计合适的高质量数据。文章介绍了三种合成数据的策划范式：过程化渲染、基于物理的仿真和生成式AI，并分别展示了其在工业表面缺陷检测、褪色自动彩色底片修复以及基于RGB与事件数据的6DoF姿态估计中的应用。研究表明，结合可控监督与学习到的外观特征的混合方法在实现可靠‘模拟到真实’迁移方面最具前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在特定视觉任务中，通用数据集缺乏领域先验，而人工标注成本高、不精确或不可行。因此，关键问题不是生成更多数据，而是如何选择和设计最有效的数据以支持任务需求。

Method: 提出并分析三种合成数据的策划范式：1）过程化渲染（显式控制场景参数，注释自动生成）；2）基于物理的仿真（建模真实现象机制，提供精确对齐的监督对）；3）生成式AI（从少量真实样本学习传感器特定外观，实现逼真效果但存在幻觉和标注误差风险）。结合实际案例评估不同数据组合策略。

Result: 实证表明，精心策划的合成数据作为真实观测的补充具有显著价值。尤其当结合可控监督与学习到的真实感外观时，能够有效提升模型在真实环境中的泛化能力，是实现可靠模拟到真实迁移的最佳路径。

Conclusion:  curated synthetic data 应被视作真实数据的有力补充，未来最有希望的方向是构建融合可控场景设计与生成式外观学习的混合系统，以实现高效、可靠的 sim-to-real 转移。

Abstract: Synthetic data are most valuable where general-purpose datasets cannot provide the domain-specific priors a task requires, and where manual annotation is expensive, imprecise, or infeasible. In this article we argue that the central question for specialized vision systems is not how to generate more data, but which data to generate. We therefore discuss curated synthetic data, whose scene content, appearance variations, sensing characteristics, and annotations are deliberately designed around a given task. We examine three complementary curation paradigms. Procedural rendering offers explicit control over scene parameters and the annotations follow by construction. Physically-based simulation encodes the mechanism behind an observed effect and yields exactly aligned supervision pairs. Generative AI learns sensor-specific appearance from small real seed sets and attains plausible realism, though it remains prone to hallucination and to inaccurate annotation. These paradigms are illustrated with examples from industrial surface defect detection, restoration of degraded digitized autochrome plates, and 6DoF pose estimation from RGB and event data. Using these examples, we analyze different data regimes and training strategies that combine synthetic and real data across these paradigms. We conclude that curated synthetic data are best understood as a complement to real observations, and that hybrid pipelines combining controllable supervision with learned appearance are the most promising direction for reliable sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [19] [Caption-Mediated Perceived-Safety Estimation for Pedestrian Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38479)
*Simon Parkinson,Paloma Liu,Wei Zheng,Mohammadreza Sheikhfathollahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释的行人路线规划方法，通过街景图像估算感知安全性，利用视觉-语言模型生成自然语言描述作为中间表示。该文本特征用于推导感知风险类别，确保每个路段评分对用户可检查。在相同下游流程下，对比了九种不同条件下的图像描述与直接基于CLIP图像嵌入的基线方法，结果显示描述中介表示达到与图像嵌入相当的效果。该方法在英格兰北部两个地区（曼彻斯特和赫德斯菲尔德）覆盖654,115张图像的36个选区进行了部署。独立实地验证显示，与本地收集的3,669张图像、70名参与者共3,669次评分相比，相关性虽统计显著但较弱（r=0.262），而评估者间差异的噪声上限为0.737。一次单次确认测试发现，监督基准表现提升44%的管道在实际中未带来明显改进（r=0.250，p=0.84），表明基准提升不能预测实际部署效果。行程长度对路由行为有系统性影响：低于1公里时变化不明显；3至6公里行程中，低风险路线平均延长12.78%，绕行中位数仅为2.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统行人路线规划依赖于不可解释的模型，难以提供透明性和可信度。本研究旨在通过引入可解释的自然语言中间表示，使感知安全性的计算过程对用户透明，从而提高路线推荐的可信度和可用性。

Method: 采用视觉-语言模型生成街景图像的自然语言描述，并将这些描述转化为结构化特征以估计感知风险。所有评分基于存储的文本特征进行，确保可追溯性与可解释性。在多个模型家族和条件下进行对比实验，并使用相同的下游任务流程评估性能。同时，在真实环境中进行实地验证，评估模型的实际表现。

Result: 基于图像描述的中介表示在性能上达到了与直接图像嵌入相当的水平，且具备更高的可解释性。实地验证显示，尽管相关性较低但具有统计显著性（r=0.262），噪声上限为0.737。监督基准上的性能提升并未在实际部署中体现，说明基准性能不能完全反映现实应用效果。路由行为随行程长度变化：短距离影响小，长距离（3–6公里）下低风险路线平均延长12.78%，绕行仅增加2.73%。

Conclusion: 提出的基于自然语言描述的可解释行人路由方法在保持高可解释性的同时实现了与现有图像嵌入方法相当的性能。尽管实际部署中的相关性有限，但其透明性有助于增强用户信任。结果表明，模型在长距离路径优化中能有效引导选择更安全路线，且绕行代价可控，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents an explainable approach to pedestrian routing, in which perceived safety is estimated from street-level imagery through an explicit natural-language intermediate representation. A vision--language model caption is generated and stored before any scoring is undertaken, and the perceived-risk class is derived entirely from structured features of that stored text, so that every segment score remains inspectable by the user. Nine captioning conditions across five model families are benchmarked against a direct Contrastive Language--Image Pre-training (CLIP) image-embedding baseline under an identical downstream pipeline, and the caption-mediated representation is found to reach parity with the image embedding rather than to trail it. The approach was deployed over 654,115 images covering 36 electoral wards in two locations in Northern England (Manchester and Huddersfield). Independent field validation against 3,669 locally collected ratings of 494 images across 70 participant sessions established agreement that is statistically significant but modest, at $r=0.262$, against a measured noise ceiling of 0.737 imposed by disagreement between raters. A single-use confirmatory test then found that a pipeline 44\% stronger on the supervised benchmark did not produce measurable improvement in the field ($r=0.250$, $p=0.84$), so the benchmark gains did not predict the deployment gains in this case. Routing behaviour varies systematically with journey length. There is negligible change below 1\,km, reaching a median increase of 12.78\% in low-risk route length for a median detour of 2.73\% on journeys of 3 to 6 km.

</details>


### [20] [Beyond Layers: Position-Resolved Gradient Conflict and Position-Aware Modulation for Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38485)
*Shuyang Jiang,Fucheng Deng,Yuchuan Luo,Zhenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种位置解析的干扰图方法，用于分析统一多模态模型（UMM）中图像理解与自回归图像生成之间的梯度冲突，并发现生成梯度的干扰强度随视觉标记在序列中的位置系统性变化。通过单次反向传播实现高效计算，在Show-o和Janus-Pro数据集上验证了位置解释力显著高于层级解释力（部分η²分别为0.31 vs. 0.35 和 0.15 vs. 0.30）。研究进一步提出位置感知调制（PAM），仅在高冲突位置抑制生成梯度的反向分量，无需改变架构，在参数预算相当下优于逐层分离策略，提升21 MME和2.4 GenEval得分，且与层间分离互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对统一多模态模型中理解与生成目标间的冲突分析局限于层或专家级别，无法捕捉生成梯度干扰随视觉序列位置变化的系统性差异。本文旨在揭示并利用这一隐藏维度——位置，以更精细地诊断和缓解冲突。

Method: 提出位置解析的干扰图，基于单次反向传播计算每层各视觉标记位置的生成-理解梯度冲突；设计位置感知调制（PAM）机制，仅在高冲突位置调节生成梯度方向；结合随机对照实验验证其有效性。

Result: 位置能解释大量冲突方差（如在Show-o上占31%，接近层的35%）；第一四分位的生成梯度与理解方向高度负相关（cos=-0.18），末尾四分位则接近零（cos=-0.02）；PAM在相同参数预算下超越层分离策略，提升21 MME和2.4 GenEval点，且效果可被随机位置控制部分恢复；位置与层间分离具有互补性。

Conclusion: 位置是理解统一多模态模型中生成与理解冲突的关键维度，位置感知调制（PAM）是一种高效、无需架构变更的有效缓解策略，与传统层级分离方法互补，为未来多任务模型优化提供了新视角。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) train image understanding and autoregressive image generation on shared parameters, and the two objectives are known to interfere. Existing diagnoses and remedies operate at the resolution of layers or experts, measuring conflict per layer and resolving it by separating parameters. We argue that this resolution hides an orthogonal axis. Generation in a UMM is next-token prediction over a raster sequence of visual tokens whose roles vary systematically with position, so how strongly a generation gradient interferes with understanding should depend on where in the sequence it originates. We introduce a position-resolved interference map that attributes understanding-generation gradient conflict to visual-token positions within every layer, computed from a single backward pass at $1.2\times$ the cost of a standard backward pass. On Show-o and Janus-Pro, position explains a large share of conflict variance after controlling for depth (partial $η^2=0.31$ vs. $0.35$ for layer on Show-o; $0.15$ vs. $0.30$ on Janus-Pro): the first quarter of the sequence has a mean gradient cosine of $-0.18$ against understanding, the last quarter $-0.02$. The dependence survives per-position gradient-norm normalization, retaining $80%$ of its effect size, and conflict strength tracks semantic content (Spearman $ρ=0.64$). Building on the map, we propose position-aware modulation (PAM), which removes the anti-aligned component of generation gradients only at high-conflict positions without changing the architecture. Under a matched trainable-parameter budget, PAM improves over layer-wise separation by $+21$ MME and $+2.4$ GenEval points on Show-o while matching it on POPE and overall FID; a random-position control recovers about $31%$ of the gain. Position-based and layer-based separation are complementary degrees of freedom and can be combined.

</details>


### [21] [Multidimensional Observer Model and Perceptual Dimensions of Human Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38487)
*Sheng Zhao,Weikai Lin,Yuhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种多维度观察者模型，用于理解人类图像质量判断的内在感知空间。模型基于灵长类动物腹侧流的神经表征，并通过大规模行为数据拟合，能够分析感知结构且预测性能优于现有度量方法。研究发现，人类图像质量判断所需的感知空间维度极低，且在低层次与高层次判断间结构不同，表明尽管起始于共享的视网膜编码，人类会根据任务需求选择性构建感知空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人类图像质量判断的内在感知机制，以支持机器视觉任务并揭示感知决策中的认知结构。

Method: 提出一种基于神经表征和行为数据拟合的多维度观察者模型，将图像表示为潜在感知空间中的分布，并通过噪声样本比较来模拟人类判断。

Result: 感知空间维度远低于图像空间，且在不同质量判断层次中结构不同，表明人类根据任务需求动态构建感知空间。

Conclusion: 人类图像质量判断依赖于低维、任务相关的感知空间，其结构由任务性质决定，而非单纯由输入图像决定。

Abstract: Judging image quality is not only ecologically relevant to everyday human tasks, but also underpins many machine vision tasks such as image generation. This paper proposes a framework to understand the inherent perceptual space underlying image quality judgment in humans. We propose a multi-dimensional observer model that represents images as distributions in a latent perceptual space and that models human judgment as comparing noisy samples. Being constrained by neural representations in the primate ventral stream and fit to large-scale behavioral data, the model enables analysis of perceptual structure while matching the predictive power of existing metrics. Using this model, we find that the perceptual spaces needed to account for image quality judgment in humans are extremely low-dimensional compared to the image space even when considering its sparsity. The exact structure of the space (e.g., dimensionalities, information encoded) varies between low-level and high-level quality judgments, suggesting that, despite a shared retinal encoding in the beginning, humans selectively construct task-dependent perceptual spaces in visual decision making.

</details>


### [22] [GazeFlow: From Human Gaze Behavior to Generative Egocentric Gaze Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38519)
*Sheng Zhao,Weikai Lin,Yuhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GazeFlow 是一种新型的眼动预测框架，通过联合建模时间上的眼动位置分布，结合自上而下任务信息和自下而上视觉显著性，利用条件流匹配（CFM）生成符合人类眼动动态的轨迹，在标准数据集上达到最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类眼动具有内在随机性，但受固定的时间动态（如注视与扫视交替）、任务驱动的高层认知影响以及视觉显著性等约束。现有方法难以有效建模这些复杂动态，因此需要更精确的框架来捕捉眼动的时空特性。

Method: GazeFlow 采用条件流匹配（CFM），通过学习一个速度场，将高斯噪声样本逐步转化为符合真实眼动分布的轨迹；该速度场同时依赖于视频编码器提取的视觉特征（底向上）和全局查询得到的任务信息（顶向下）。

Result: 在标准数据集上，GazeFlow 在逐帧指标上达到当前最优表现，生成的眼动轨迹在时间动态上与真实人类眼动更加一致。

Conclusion: GazeFlow 通过联合建模眼动的时空分布，有效捕捉了眼动的复杂动态特性，为未来眼动预测任务提供了新的建模范式。

Abstract: Egocentric gaze prediction enables many downstream applications but remains challenging, as human gaze is inherently stochastic. This stochasticity is constrained by structured temporal dynamics alternating between fixations and saccades, top-down influences from tasks, and bottom-up visual saliency. Based on this observation, we introduce GazeFlow, a framework that directly models gaze as a joint distribution of temporal gaze positions conditioned upon both top-down and bottom-up information. In particular, GazeFlow uses conditional flow matching (CFM): a learned velocity field iteratively transports a Gaussian noise sample into a plausible gaze trajectory drawn from this joint distribution. The velocity field is conditioned on bottom-up visual features extracted by a video encoder and on top-down task information obtained by globally querying these features. On standard datasets, GazeFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance on per-frame metrics, and the generated trajectories align better with human gaze temporal dynamics.

</details>


### [23] [ThinkV2V: Unleashing the Reasoning Capability of MLLMs for Instruction-Guided Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38541)
*Donghao Zhou,Haoyang He,Fan Zhang,Hao Yang,Guisheng Liu,Xin Gao,Zhongwei Wan,Xingyuan Bu,Jie Wang,Qiangpeng Yang,Shilei Wen,Chi-Wing Fu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ThinkV2V提出一种基于推理的视频编辑框架，通过在视觉生成前激活多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的思考过程，解决现有方法在处理隐式编辑时缺乏因果或语义推理的问题。该框架采用MLLM-to-DiT架构，将对源视频和指令的深层思考转化为精细化的条件信号，并结合渐进式课程训练与推理时思维扩展策略，提升复杂场景下的推理能力。同时构建了ThinkV2V-150K数据集和ThinkV2V-Bench评测基准，实验表明其5B模型在复杂与标准任务上均优于更大规模的10B基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的视频编辑方法仅将MLLM用作语义编码器，难以处理需要因果或语义推理的隐式编辑任务，存在推理能力不足的根本缺陷。

Method: 提出ThinkV2V框架，利用MLLM-to-DiT架构实现从思考到视觉生成的转化；采用渐进式课程训练（PCT）逐步提升模型能力；引入推理时思维扩展（Inference-Time Thinking Scaling）迭代优化提示并选择最可靠结果；构建专用数据集与评测基准支持训练与评估。

Result: ThinkV2V在复杂与标准视频编辑任务中均达到领先性能，5B规模模型显著超越10B规模基线模型，证明其高效推理与生成能力。

Conclusion: ThinkV2V通过显式激活MLLM的推理能力，有效提升了复杂指令引导视频编辑的准确性和鲁棒性，为未来智能视频编辑提供了新范式。

Abstract: Instruction-guided video editing has made significant progress, yet existing methods use multimodal large language models (MLLMs) primarily as semantic encoders, so they often fall short in working with implicit edits that require causal or semantic reasoning. To bridge this fundamental gap in video editing, we propose ThinkV2V, a reasoning-driven framework for complex instruction-guided video editing, explicitly activating MLLM thinking before visual generation. At its core, ThinkV2V builds on a practical MLLM-to-DiT architecture to turn explicit thinking over the source video and instruction into refined conditioning signals for video editing. Further, we equip it with a dedicated training and inference recipe, combining Progressive Curriculum Training, which gradually cultivates the model from basic editing to reasoning-intensive cases, with Inference-Time Thinking Scaling, which iteratively refines candidate prompts and selects the most reliable one, to better elicit reasoning in challenging editing scenarios. We also curate the ThinkV2V-150K dataset and introduce ThinkV2V-Bench to support training and evaluation of video editing with implicit intent and causal reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of ThinkV2V on both complex and standard editing scenarios, in which our 5B-scale DiT model substantially outperforms larger 10B-scale baselines.

</details>


### [24] [Detail in Context: A Dual-Scale Machine Learning Framework for Mycosis Fungoides Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38560)
*Mohamed Hazem,Tarek Waleed,Omar Khaled,Nada Omar,Mahmoud Raslan,Marwa Mohamed Fawzy,Aya Fahim,Rania M. Mogawer,Ahmed Mourad,Kariman Mansour,Muhammad Rushdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合双尺度组织病理图像分析与深度学习的自动化蕈样肉芽肿（MF）检测综合诊断框架。该框架采用10x和20x双倍率卷积神经网络的后期融合集成模型，并结合基于16个临床特征的随机森林分类器，以区分MF与其他淋巴增殖性皮肤病。在包含4,306例MF和1,961例非MF、共6,267张图像的扩展数据集上，实验结果显示：优先关注高分辨率细胞学细节（20x）并结合10x下的整体结构背景，可显著提升检测性能。图像基后期融合模型达到83.58%准确率和89.13%敏感度；临床随机森林模型则实现96.6%准确率和93.8%敏感度，表明该多模态框架具有强大的临床决策支持潜力。该系统针对两个不同临床目标：一是优化早期筛查的双尺度图像分析流程，二是用于确诊后MF分期（斑片/斑块 vs. 肿瘤）的临床元数据模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蕈样肉芽肿（MF）是一种罕见的皮肤T细胞淋巴瘤，早期常因外观类似良性炎症性皮肤病而被误诊。早期准确诊断对改善患者预后至关重要，但现有方法受限于主观判断和诊断延迟，亟需一种客观、高效的辅助诊断工具。因此，本研究旨在构建一个融合多尺度病理图像与临床信息的自动化诊断框架，以提高MF的早期识别率和分期准确性。

Method: 提出一种双尺度（10x和20x）卷积神经网络（CNN）的后期融合集成模型，分别提取低倍率图像中的组织架构信息与高倍率图像中的细胞形态细节。同时，构建一个基于16个临床特征的随机森林分类器，用于补充临床信息。通过联合图像与临床数据，实现多模态融合决策。使用6,267张图像（4,306例MF，1,961例非MF）进行训练与验证，评估模型在诊断与分期任务中的表现。

Result: 图像基后期融合模型在检测MF vs. 非MF时达到83.58%的准确率和89.13%的敏感度；临床随机森林模型在确认MF后的分期任务中表现出更高的性能，准确率达96.6%，敏感度为93.8%。结果表明，双尺度图像分析能有效捕捉关键病理特征，而临床特征可显著提升分期预测能力。整体框架展现出良好的临床应用前景。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的多模态诊断框架，通过整合双尺度病理图像与临床元数据，实现了对蕈样肉芽肿的高效早期筛查与精准分期。该系统具备成为皮肤科临床决策支持工具的潜力，有助于减少误诊漏诊，推动个体化治疗进程。

Abstract: Mycosis fungoides (MF) is a rare form of cutaneous T-cell lymphoma that is often misdiagnosed in early stages due to its visual similarity to benign inflammatory dermatoses. Early and accurate diagnosis is critical for improving patient outcomes. In this paper, we propose a comprehensive diagnostic framework for automated MF detection that combines dual- scale histopathological image analysis with deep learning. To distinguish MF from other lymphoproliferative skin conditions, the proposed approach leverages a late-fusion ensemble of dual- magnification (10x and 20x) convolutional neural networks (CNNs), complemented by a random forest classifier trained on 16 clinical features. Experimental results on an expanded dataset of 6,267 images (4,306 MF; 1,961 Non-MF) across 463 patients demonstrate that strong detection performance is obtained by prioritizing higher-resolution cytological details (20x) within broader architectural context (10x). The image-based late-fusion model achieves an accuracy of 83.58% and a sensitivity of 89.13%, while the clinical random forest model achieves an accuracy of 96.6% and sensitivity of 93.8%, highlighting the po- tential of this multimodal framework as a robust clinical decision support system in dermatology. This framework addresses two distinct clinical objectives: an image-based dual-scale pipeline optimized for the early diagnostic screening of MF versus non- MF dermatoses, and a complementary clinical metadata model designed for the subsequent staging of confirmed MF cases (patch/plaque versus tumor)

</details>


### [25] [LongTake: Learning to Sustain Dynamics in Long-Horizon Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38562)
*Byoungwoo Park,Jaemoo Choi,Juho Lee,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LongTake提出一种两阶段训练框架，通过长时序教师强制（Long-Horizon Teacher Forcing）在真实长视频上进行监督，增强自回归视频扩散模型在长时间生成中的动态持续性与视觉质量。该方法通过直接长序列监督提升初始化效果，使分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）无需中间几步蒸馏即可实现更优性能。在相同五秒训练条件下，其30秒自滚动生成的动态度显著优于短时监督初始化，且在动态度与美学质量间达到帕累托最优。混合DMD进一步利用教师模型对自滚动后期帧进行监督，实现双向联合监督，最终在30秒和60秒生成中均取得最高动态度表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型在长时间生成时易出现画面静止或视觉质量下降，原因在于短时视频监督难以有效指导长期场景演化。因此需要更强的长时序监督机制来维持动态连续性和视觉保真度。

Method: 提出LongTake两阶段训练：第一阶段采用长时序教师强制（Long-Horizon TF），以真实长视频为输入，让模型预测后续帧；第二阶段基于学生自滚动进行分布匹配蒸馏（DMD），并引入混合DMD策略，实现对初始窗口的双向联合监督和后期帧的持续监督。

Result: 在30秒和60秒自滚动生成中，LongTake在动态度和美学质量上均优于现有基线，尤其在动态度方面表现突出；混合DMD进一步提升了动态度，实现了帕累托最优性能。

Conclusion: Long-Horizon Teacher Forcing能有效增强自回归视频模型的长时序生成能力，结合高效蒸馏策略可实现高质量、高动态性的长期视频合成，为世界模型、游戏模拟和长镜头视频生成提供了有力支持。

Abstract: World models, game simulators, and long-take video creation require coherent scene evolution and sustained dynamics over extended durations. Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion provides a natural framework for long-horizon generation, yet extended rollouts often become near-static or lose visual quality. We hypothesize that these failures reflect the limited guidance provided by short-video supervision on how ongoing scene dynamics develops over longer durations. This motivates us to introduce LongTake, a two-stage training pipeline built around Long-Horizon Teacher Forcing (TF) on curated real long videos. Long-Horizon TF trains the AR model to predict later frames conditioned on long ground-truth video prefixes, extending direct supervision beyond the short training horizon. This supervision is designed to help the model sustain dynamics and preserve visual quality during long-horizon generation. Our central finding is that this training stage strengthens direct initialization for distribution matching distillation (DMD) under student self-rollout, without the intermediate few-step distillation stage used in standard pipelines. Under the same five-second DMD training setup, our initialization yields substantially higher dynamic degree than short horizon TF initialization on 30-second rollouts at comparable aesthetic quality, and surpasses the evaluated baselines in both measures. Hybrid DMD further reuses this teacher to extend supervision to later frames of the self-rollout while retaining bidirectional joint supervision over the initial window. On long-horizon self-rollouts, LongTake lies on the Pareto front of dynamic degree and aesthetic quality, and Hybrid DMD attains the highest dynamic degree among evaluated methods at both 30s and 60s.

</details>


### [26] [Retargeting Motions to Diverse Skeletons via Learnable Flattening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38578)
*Kia-Jüng Yang,Fabian H. Sinz,Paweł A. Pierzchlewicz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Transformer的自动编码器，用于跨结构运动重定向，通过可学习的骨骼图展平方法构建拓扑和位移不变的潜在空间，采用乘法集成的图位置编码，实现对不同骨骼拓扑的统一建模，并在无监督条件下训练，无需成对重定向数据。零样本评估中，该方法将全局关节位置误差降低43-47%，用户研究显示其在运动对齐和物理合理性方面表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在未见骨骼拓扑的零样本设置下可靠性差，基于Transformer的方法未能超越专用几何方法，亟需一种能统一处理多样化骨骼结构且无需配对数据的高效模型。

Method: 提出一种可学习的骨骼图展平机制，结合乘法集成的图位置编码与Transformer自动编码器架构，实现拓扑和翻译不变的潜在表示学习，支持完全无监督训练。

Result: 在零样本测试中，相比现有基准，全局关节位置误差降低43-47%；用户研究（n=37）证实其在运动对齐和物理合理性上显著优于其他方法（p<0.05）。

Conclusion: 该模型设计使Transformer架构在运动重定向任务中具备更强表达能力，是提升跨结构运动迁移性能的关键，显著超越当前主流方法。

Abstract: Cross-structural motion retargeting aims to transfer motion between different skeletal topologies. Despite recent progress, existing state-of-the-art models struggle with reliability in zero-shot settings, i.e. skeletons with different topologies which were unseen during training, and recent Transformer-based attempts have failed to outperform specialized geometric methods. We bridge this gap with a Transformer Autoencoder that learns a topology- and translation-invariant latent space. Our core contribution is a learnable flattening of skeletal graphs that captures both local dependencies and global structure. Unlike the standard transformer architecture, which adds positional information to token content, we integrate graph-based positional encodings multiplicatively, a design choice that follows directly from our flattening formulation. The resulting model handles diverse skeletal topologies within a single unified architecture and trains in a fully unsupervised manner, requiring no paired retargeting data. Ablation studies show, that the graph encodings, multiplicative formulation, and Transformer backbone is critical for the performance. In zero-shot evaluations, our method reduces global joint position error by $43-47\%$ over current benchmarks. A user study ($n = 37$), including expert animators, further ranks our approach highest in motion alignment and physical plausibility ($p < 0.05$). These results demonstrate that our model design is key to making transformer architectures effective for motion retargeting, outperforming existing approaches.

</details>


### [27] [Restoring without Forgetting: Filter-Level Continual Image Restoration via Parameter-Space Integrated Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38591)
*Xin Feng,Jin Zhao,Yizhen Zhang,Wenjie Pei,Fanglin Chen,Guangming Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RwF框架，通过滤波器级持续适应解决图像恢复模型在不重访旧数据情况下的灾难性遗忘问题。该方法利用梯度归因定位关键滤波器，通过低秩变换和跨任务注意力生成新任务专用滤波器，并在局部位置重构，实现高效且无遗忘的适应。实验表明其性能优于现有方法，参数量仅需约1/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像恢复模型在持续学习新任务时面临灾难性遗忘，而传统方法需要访问历史数据，难以满足实际场景需求。因此，亟需一种无需重访旧数据、能有效保留已有知识并适应新任务的持续学习框架。

Method: 采用参数空间集成梯度归因定位降质相关的关键滤波器；基于滤波器库通过紧凑的低秩变换生成任务特定滤波器；引入跨任务注意力与原型对比学习增强泛化能力；仅在局部位置组装新滤波器以减少干扰。

Result: 在六个图像恢复任务上验证，RwF有效避免遗忘，恢复质量媲美全数据访问的统一模型，且相比LoRA类方法使用约1/10的额外参数。

Conclusion: RwF通过滤波器级细粒度控制，实现了高效、无遗忘的持续图像恢复，为轻量级持续学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Adapting image restoration models to a stream of new tasks without revisiting past data remains challenging due to catastrophic forgetting. In this work, we propose Restoring without Forgetting (RwF), a filter-level continual adaptation framework for image restoration built upon a critical observation: task-specific knowledge is centered in a small subset of filters and can be separated from those reconstructing general content. RwF first performs parameter-space integrated gradients attribution to localize degradation-critical filters in a coarse-to-fine manner. It then adapts to new tasks by generating task-specific filters from a filter bank using compact factorized low-rank transformations, further augmented with cross-task attention and prototypical contrastive learning, and lastly assembles them back only at localized positions. Experiments on six restoration tasks show that RwF effectively avoids forgetting and achieves competitive restoration quality against all-in-one methods that have full data access, and outperforms LoRA-style adaptation with $\sim$10$\times$ fewer additional parameters. Code is available at https://github.com/funkdub/Restoring-without-Forgetting.

</details>


### [28] [StereoGaussians: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting from Stereo Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38592)
*Boyuan Tian,Huangying Zhan,Zhan Li,Shin-Fang Chng,Hanwen Yang,Zirui Wang,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StereoGaussians 提出了一种从单个校准立体图像对预测度量 3DGS 表示的方法，利用预训练立体网络的中间表示来预测高斯属性，同时通过校准的视差锚定几何结构。通过引入第二层高斯和扩展的图像画布，模型具备了处理遮挡和视场外内容的能力。在包含803个训练场景的SceneSplat-Stereo数据集上进行训练，实验表明其在真实和逼真立体基准测试中优于现有视图合成基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D高斯点阵（3DGS）虽可实现无需每场景优化的重建，但在实际立体相机应用中仍需对输入视角附近的区域进行外推。传统方法难以有效处理新暴露区域的外观建模与场景容量扩展问题。因此需要一种能够结合几何约束与外观学习的新方法。

Method: 利用冻结的预训练立体网络中间表示预测高斯属性；通过校准的视差提供几何锚定；引入第二层高斯和扩展图像画布以增强对遮挡及视场外内容的表达能力；采用基于高质量3DGS教师模型构建的SceneSplat-Stereo数据集进行训练。

Result: 在未见过的真实与逼真立体图像基准测试中，StereoGaussians 显著优于当前主流视图合成基线方法；消融实验证明了各设计组件的有效性。

Conclusion: StereoGaussians 成功实现了无需逐场景优化的高质量立体视图合成，通过结合预训练立体网络与可扩展的高斯表示，在几何精度与外观保真度之间取得良好平衡，为实际立体相机应用提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables reconstruction without per- scene optimisation, but practical stereo-camera applications require nearby-view extrapolation beyond the input views. Stereo depth anchors visible surfaces, yet rendering newly exposed regions also requires learned appearance and additional scene capacity. We introduce StereoGaussians, which predicts a metric 3DGS representation from a single calibrated stereo pair. It reuses intermediate repre- sentations from frozen pretrained stereo networks to predict Gaussian attributes, while calibrated disparity anchors the geometry. A second Gaussian layer and an expanded image canvas provide capacity for disoccluded and outside-field-of- view content. For training, we construct SceneSplat-Stereo from quality-filtered 3DGS teachers, pairing stereo inputs with nearby target views across 803 training scenes. Experiments on unseen real and photorealistic stereo benchmarks demon- strate improvements over strong view-synthesis baselines, while ablation studies support our main design choices.

</details>


### [29] [PixelUMM: Encoder-Free Unified Image and Video Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38597)
*Cong Wei,Xuanchi Ren,Bryan Chu,Weiming Ren,Huan Ling,Jiahui Huang,Laura Leal-Taixé,Sanja Fidler,Wenhu Chen,Zian Wang,Jay Zhangjie Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixelUMM 是一种无编码器的统一图像和视频理解与生成模型，直接在像素空间中操作。它将图像表示为空间块，视频表示为时空管状结构，并通过单层线性投影连接到共享的多模态主干网络。采用混合变压器架构，结合共享注意力与任务特定参数，扩展了干净像素预测至视频生成，同时支持自回归文本预测和像素空间流匹配。实验表明其在图像和视频理解与生成任务上表现优异，并对关键设计选择进行了实证研究，为未来像素空间统一多模态模型提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型通常依赖于独立的视觉表示进行理解和生成，导致视觉上下文长度增加且难以融入现有视觉-语言预训练流程。虽然像素空间建模提供了无编码器的替代方案，但将其从图像扩展到视频面临挑战，因视频的理解与生成使用不同的时间表示方式，统一视觉接口的设计仍不明确。

Method: PixelUMM 将图像表示为空间块，视频表示为时空管状结构，通过单层线性投影连接原始像素与共享多模态主干网络。采用混合变压器架构，结合共享注意力机制与任务特定参数，实现图像与视频的统一建模；同时扩展干净像素预测用于视频生成，并联合支持自回归文本生成与像素空间流匹配。

Result: PixelUMM 在图像和视频的理解与生成任务上均达到具有竞争力的性能。实证研究揭示了解码器设计、空间-时间块大小等关键设计选择的影响，为后续像素空间统一多模态模型的发展提供了重要参考。

Conclusion: PixelUMM 证明了在像素空间中实现统一图像与视频理解与生成的可行性，通过简洁的架构设计与有效建模策略，推动了无编码器多模态模型的发展方向。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) often rely on separate visual representations for understanding and generation, increasing visual context length and complicating integration with established vision-language pretraining pipelines. Recent advances in pixel-space modeling offer an encoder-free alternative, but extending this paradigm from images to videos is non-trivial: video understanding and generation adopt different temporal representations, leaving the design of a unified visual interface an open question. We present PixelUMM, an encoder-free model for unified image and video understanding and generation directly in pixel space. PixelUMM represents images as spatial patches and videos as spatiotemporal tubelets, connecting raw pixels to a shared multimodal backbone through single-layer linear projections. Its Mixture-of-Transformers architecture combines shared attention with task-specific parameters and extends clean-pixel prediction to video generation, jointly supporting autoregressive text prediction and pixel-space flow matching. Experiments show that PixelUMM achieves competitive performance across image and video understanding and generation tasks. We further conduct empirical studies of key design choices, including decoder design and spatial-temporal patch size, providing insights for future pixel-space unified multimodal models.

</details>


### [30] [Aperture: Training-Free Multiscale Concept Bottlenecks for Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38603)
*Rishabh Mondal,Nipun Batra,Utkarsh Mall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出APERTURE模型，通过多尺度概念瓶颈与预训练多模态大模型结合，在无需训练的情况下实现遥感图像的高可解释性分类。引入SiFC数据集进行评估，APERTURE在宏平均F1分数上优于现有无训练基线超过10个百分点，并超越监督型概念瓶颈模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测模型缺乏可解释性；虽有概念瓶颈模型提供可解释性与专家交互能力，但在遥感领域存在训练成本高或性能差的问题。尤其在专家领域，需同时具备图像和概念空间中的精细表示。

Method: 在图像空间采用基于贪心四叉树路由的多尺度概念瓶颈以定位小目标概念；在概念空间中使用预训练多模态大模型（MLLM）获取可靠概念得分；设计全局图像与原生概念尺度融合机制，构建APERTURE模型。

Result: 在自建的细粒度概念中心数据集SiFC上，APERTURE在宏平均F1-score上显著优于现有无训练基线（+10%以上），且表现超越部分监督型概念瓶颈模型；组件移除实验验证了概念得分对视觉证据变化的敏感性；时间实验表明描述符更新可提升对技术变迁的识别能力而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: APERTURE通过融合多尺度图像信息与可靠的预训练概念表示，实现了遥感领域训练自由、高可解释性的先进分类性能，为未来可解释地球观测模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: While earth observation models have advanced substantially, they still lack interpretability. While concept-bottleneck models provide interpretability and expert interaction, they are either too expensive to train for the remote sensing domain or perform poorly without annotation. We posit that in expert domains like remote sensing, such training-free models require both fine details in both image and concept space. In image space, we propose a multiscale concept bottleneck using greedy quadtree routing to locate small concepts. In concept space, we replace contrastive vision language models with pre-trained MLLMs and present a way to get reliable concept scores from them. We introduce APERTURE that blends concept scores at the global image and native concept-scale level to give state-ofthe-art training-free model performance. To test these models, introduce SiFC, a fine-grained concept-centric dataset across three countries, with human-reviewed class-level concept maps. On SiFC, APERTURE outperforms the best training-free baselines by more than 10 percentage points in macro F1-score, and notably also outperforms supervised concept bottleneck models. Targeted component-removal tests examine whether concept scores respond to changes in visual evidence, while temporal experiments show that descriptor updates improve recognition of technological changes without retraining.

</details>


### [31] [After a Decade: Bringing Shadow Removal into the Real World with Agentic Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38607)
*Shilin Hu,Jingyi Xu,Dimitris Samaras,Hieu Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离线代理工作流，用于构建大规模、多样化的阴影去除数据集AgenticShadow，解决真实世界中阴影去除模型训练数据稀缺的问题。该工作流结合物理驱动生成、失败检测、反馈重试、候选选择与确定性修正，显著提升图像色彩分布一致性，使现有模型在跨域测试中性能大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阴影去除方法依赖于成对的带阴影与无阴影图像训练数据，但此类数据难以获取，因需移除遮挡物同时保持场景、相机和光照不变。尽管已有大量阴影检测数据，但缺乏对应的无阴影目标图像，导致模型在真实场景下表现脆弱。

Method: 提出一种基于代理的离线工作流，包括物理动机的图像生成、失败检测、反馈驱动的重试机制、候选样本筛选及确定性修正，以从现有阴影检测数据中合成高质量的无阴影目标图像。

Result: 成功构建包含17,138个图像-掩码-目标三元组的AgenticShadow数据集，相比以往方法，色彩分布差异降低50.5%；使用该数据集训练现有模型后，跨域LAB RMSE下降19.7%-37.5%。

Conclusion: 通过引入智能代理工作流，实现了从非配对的真实世界阴影数据中高效生成高质量无阴影图像，显著推动了阴影去除技术在真实场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Shadow removal looks nearly solved on established benchmarks, yet remains brittle in the real world. Models have advanced; the paired training data they rely on have barely changed in nearly a decade. The reason is simple: obtaining a shadow-free target requires removing the occluder while keeping the scene, camera, and illumination otherwise unchanged, making diverse paired data difficult to capture. Meanwhile, large shadow detection datasets already contain diverse real-world images and masks, but no shadow-free targets. To turn this abundant but incomplete data into paired supervision, we propose an offline agentic workflow combining physics-motivated generation, failure detection, feedback-driven retry, candidate selection, and deterministic correction. Using this workflow, we construct AgenticShadow, a dataset of 17,138 image-mask-target triplets spanning general scenes, faces, and remote sensing. Our construction workflow reduces Color Distribution Difference by 50.5% over previous shadow removal work, while training existing shadow removal models on AgenticShadow reduces cross-domain LAB RMSE by 19.7-37.5%.

</details>

*Hongjia Zhai,Xiyu Zhang,Haoran Zhang,Zhichao Ye,Haomin Liu,Guofeng Zhang,Ian Reid,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Exo2EgoHOI 是一种面向交互保留的外视角到第一人称视频生成框架，通过引入统一的4D手-物体交互（HOI）先验和分解门控交叉注意力机制，有效提升在大视角变化下手-物体交互的保真度与物体一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在将外视角视频转换为第一人称视角时，难以在大视角变化下保持手-物体交互的精确性，主要受限于缺乏细粒度交互引导和弱物体中心锚定。

Method: 提出统一4D HOI先验，融合场景几何、可动手部渲染和密集手-物关系场；设计双分支残差适配器注入结构与关系线索；引入分解门控交叉注意力，分别编码物体与背景参考，自适应融合全局语义与局部外观特征作为物体中心锚点。

Result: 在ARCTIC-HOI和Ego-Exo4D数据集上显著提升物体一致性和交互保真度，尤其在ARCTIC-HOI上，物体mIoU提升32.3%，MPJPE和PA-MPJPE分别降低34.7%和50.0%。

Conclusion: Exo2EgoHOI 有效解决了外视角到第一人称视频转换中的交互保真与物体一致性难题，为具身智能提供高质量的第一人称视觉数据。

Abstract: Egocentric videos of human manipulation provide valuable visual experience for embodied intelligence, yet collecting such data at scale is costly. Exocentric-to-egocentric video generation offers a scalable alternative by transforming abundant third-person manipulation videos into first-person observations. However, existing methods often struggle to faithfully preserve demonstrated hand-object interactions (HOI) across large viewpoint changes due to insufficient fine-grained interaction guidance and weak object-centric anchoring. We present Exo2EgoHOI, an HOI-aware video generative framework for interaction-preserving exocentric-to-egocentric translation. To preserve fine-grained HOI, we introduce a unified 4D HOI prior that combines scene geometry, articulated hand renderings, and dense hand-object relation fields, together with a dual-branch residual adapter for injecting structural and relational cues into the video generation backbone. To preserve object consistency, we introduce Decomposed Gated Cross-Attention, which separately encodes object and background references and adaptively integrates global semantic and local appearance features as object-centric anchors. Experiments on ARCTIC-HOI and Ego-Exo4D demonstrate substantial improvements in object consistency and HOI preservation while maintaining competitive visual fidelity. In particular, on ARCTIC-HOI, Exo2EgoHOI improves object mIoU by 32.3% and reduces MPJPE and PA-MPJPE by 34.7% and 50.0%, respectively, relative to the respective best baseline results. Project page: https://rcl-robotics.github.io/Exo2EgoHOI/.

</details>


### [33] [HIGS: Hierarchical Implicit Grids for Joint Geometric and Semantic Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38620)
*Hanwen Cao,Wenqiang Wu,Kuang-Ting Tu,Mathias Otnes,Jeffrey Delmerico,Rui Wang,Yulun Tian,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层神经场，通过多分辨率子地图实现高效可扩展的隐式表示，并设计统一查询与解码机制，同时支持几何与语义特征。通过将场景分解为重叠子地图并进行层级优化，提升大规模环境下的计算效率；引入特征编码器预测初始网格特征，减少从零优化的时间开销；在隐式特征空间中对齐融合子地图，避免解码开销，显著加速。该方法结合几何与视觉-语言潜在特征，在SDF构建和开放词汇目标定位任务中表现优异，大幅提高计算与内存效率，保持高精度，赋予机器人在真实世界大规模场景中的空间感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经隐式表示主要关注几何重建，缺乏语义信息以支持高层空间理解与任务规划；随着环境规模与复杂度增加，传统方法面临计算效率下降的问题。因此需要一种既能保持高精度又能高效处理大规模场景的隐式表示方法。

Method: 提出分层神经场架构，采用多分辨率子地图划分场景，进行局部层级优化；设计统一的查询与解码机制，支持几何与语义特征输出；引入特征编码器预测初始层级网格特征，加速优化过程；在隐式特征空间中对齐并融合子地图，避免解码步骤，提升效率。

Result: 所提方法在大规模真实世界基准上显著提升了计算与内存效率，保持高估计精度，成功实现了开放词汇目标定位与SDF构建，增强了机器人在复杂环境中的空间感知能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分层神经场框架有效解决了大规模场景下神经隐式表示的效率与语义缺失问题，实现了高效、可扩展且具备语义理解能力的3D地图构建，适用于实际机器人应用。

Abstract: Neural implicit representations have had a significant impact on scene reconstruction by enabling robots to build continuous, differentiable, and high-fidelity 3D maps. Most existing works focus on geometric reconstruction and lack semantic information for high-level spatial understanding and task planning. Also, as the scale and complexity of the environment increase, neural representations face the challenge of maintaining computational efficiency in back-end optimization. To resolve these two challenges, we introduce a hierarchical neural field that leverages multiresolution submaps to achieve an efficient and scalable implicit representation, and a unified query and decoding mechanism to support both geometric and semantic features. More specifically, the learnable map features can be converted to the output with the query and decoding process for both training and inference. For large-scale representation, we decompose a scene into overlapping submaps and do hierarchical optimization within each local submap, thus enabling scalable computation. To further improve efficiency, we design feature encoders that predict initial hierarchical grid features to substantially reduce the time needed to optimize the submap features from scratch. To correct estimation drift among submaps, we align and fuse them entirely within the implicit feature space, leading to substantial acceleration by avoiding the need to decode the final output. Building upon this efficient hierarchical representation, we embed both geometric features and vision-language latent features into the map, and demonstrate it on both Signed Distance Field (SDF) construction and open-vocabulary object grounding. Our approach significantly improves computation and memory efficiency, maintains high estimation accuracy, and endows the robot with spatial awareness on large-scale real-world benchmarks.

</details>


### [34] [Eulerian Motion Reconstruction for Water Scenery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38622)
*Chuhan Chen,Yen-Chi Cheng,Ayush Saraf,Rajvi Shah,Tuotuo Li,Johannes Kopf,Chen Gao,Hung-Yu Tseng,Deva Ramanan,Matthew O'Toole,Changil Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从单个非循环2D视频重建并动画化水景的4D动态重建方法，通过3D静态欧拉运动场与周期性重生的高斯点表示运动，并引入时间变化的残差项以捕捉非周期性随机动态，实现从新视角交互式渲染的逼真水景动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往工作从2D视频纹理角度处理水景动画，仅生成循环视频；本文希望从3D视角出发，构建可交互渲染的4D动态重建，以更真实地再现自然水景。

Method: 采用3D静态欧拉运动场来驱动周期性重生的基准高斯点，利用渲染损失进行监督；同时引入非周期性、时间变化的残差项以建模现实场景中的非周期性和随机动态。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在定量和定性上均优于现有技术，能够生成更逼真的水景动画效果。

Conclusion: 本方法成功实现了从单个2D视频生成可交互的4D水景动态重建，显著提升了视觉真实感和灵活性。

Abstract: Reconstructing and animating water scenery from nature produces compelling and immersive visual experiences.
