TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为帧差分在线自蒸馏（FD-OPSD）的新方法，用于在视频推理中通过强化学习（RL）有效利用密集帧信息，同时保持稀疏帧采样的训练与推理效率。该方法在不依赖外部教师模型或密集自回归推演的前提下，通过比较同一响应在稀疏与密集帧视图下的词元级偏好，提取帧差分信号进行知识蒸馏，从而提升模型对时间局部事件和细粒度视觉细节的捕捉能力。实验表明，在多个视频推理基准上，该方法在16、32、64帧设置下均优于GRPO、T-GRPO和Video-KTR等先进基线模型，显著提升了整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频推理中的强化学习方法通常采用固定的稀疏帧预算，导致帧数过少可能遗漏关键的时间局部事件和视觉细节，而增加帧数又会显著提高自回归推演的成本。因此，亟需一种能够在保持稀疏帧推理效率的同时，有效利用密集帧中蕴含的有用证据的方法。

Method: 提出帧差分在线自蒸馏（FD-OPSD），通过比较策略在相同采样响应下于稀疏帧与密集帧视图中的词元级偏好，生成帧差分信号，并以无监督方式实现自蒸馏，无需外部教师模型或密集自回归推演，同时保留原始稀疏帧的推演结构和推理流程。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B和Qwen3-VL-4B模型上，于六个视频推理基准测试中，FD-OPSD在16、32、64帧评估设置下均优于最强的GRPO、T-GRPO和Video-KTR基线，实现了更高的平均性能。

Conclusion: FD-OPSD成功实现了在训练阶段从密集帧中选择性地转移有用视觉证据，同时维持了稀疏帧的推理效率与不变的推理过程，为高效且高精度的视频推理提供了一种可行的新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has substantially improved the reasoning ability of multimodal language models through verifiable rewards and increasingly fine-grainedvisual or temporal credit assignment. In video reasoning, however, current RL methods typically train with a fixed sparse frame budget: increasing the number of frames makes autoregressive rollouts expensive, while too few frames may miss temporally localized events and fine-grained visual details. We present \textbf{Frame Differential On-Policy Self-Distillation (FD-OPSD)}, which transfers the useful evidence of dense frame observations to a sparse frame policy during RL training. FD-OPSD compares the policy's token level preferences for the same sampled response under sparse and dense views, and distills the resulting frame differential signal without an external teacher or dense autoregressive rollout. The method preserves sparse-frame rollouts and leaves inference unchanged. Across Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3-VL-4B on six video reasoning benchmarks, FD-OPSD yields higher overall average performance than the strongest corresponding GRPO, T-GRPO, or Video-KTR baselines across the 16, 32, and 64 frame evaluation settings. These results show that dense visual evidence can be transferred selectively during training through token level self-distillation while retaining sparse frame rollouts and unchanged inference.

</details>


### [69] [Persistent Watermarking of Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39024)
*Dixi Yao,Kaiwen Chen,Tahseen Rabbani,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对比风格的水印目标，通过显式鼓励水印模型在触发输入上与原始模型行为不同，以增强触发数据在各种下游修改和削弱水印尝试下的持久性。实验表明，该方法在多种修改下均显著优于先前方法，检测率极高，通常达到接近100%的真正率（TPR）在假正率（FPR）低于10^-4时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成模型训练成本高昂，存在被未经授权复制和修改的风险，因此需要可靠的版权保护机制。现有方法在面对模型微调、输入预处理和输出后处理等修改时，水印易被消除或弱化，亟需更鲁棒的水印方案。

Method: 提出一种对比风格的水印目标，引入一项显式激励机制，使水印模型在触发输入上的行为与原始模型产生差异，从而增强水印的抗篡改能力。

Result: 在多种下游修改和刻意削弱水印的攻击下，触发数据的持久性显著提升，检测率大幅提高，多数情况下实现接近100%的真正率（TPR）在假正率（FPR）低于10^-4时。

Conclusion: 所提出的对比式水印方法有效提升了水印在复杂攻击场景下的鲁棒性，为文本到图像生成模型提供了强有力的版权保护手段。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generation is gaining increasing popularity with the general public, motivating the development of reliable mechanisms for copyrighting such models given their expensive training costs. An adversary may obtain and reuse a pretrained T2I model without authorization, and then serve a modified version through an API service. Such modifications may arise from ordinary downstream adaptation or deliberate attempts to erase ownership, including input-prompt preprocessing, model fine-tuning, and output post-processing. From the model owner's perspective, a key challenge is therefore to embed trigger data that remain persistent under such changes while preserving the model's normal image-generation capabilities. In this work, we propose a contrastive-style watermarking objective with a term that explicitly encourages the watermarked model to behave differently from the original model on trigger inputs. Experiments show substantially stronger trigger-data persistence than prior methods across a wide range of downstream modifications and deliberate attempts to weaken the watermark, resulting in higher detection rates, often approaching 100% TPR@FPR<$10^{-4}$.

</details>


### [70] [TED:Text-Axis Evidence Decomposition for Prompted Anomaly Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39033)
*JinYoung Kim,Geonho Kim,GiJeong Park,Geonu Lee,YoungJoon Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TED (Text-Axis Evidence Decomposition) is a post-hoc scoring method that improves fine-grained defect localization in CLIP-based anomaly detection by distinguishing between defect and hard-normal evidence. It works without retraining, preserves original models, and enhances localization accuracy—especially under strong domain shift where normal regions resemble defects. Gains are significant in high-competition settings.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP-based anomaly detectors often mislocalize defects due to ambiguous scoring when domain shifts occur; existing methods increase sensitivity but fail to ensure reliable evidence discrimination between true defects and visually complex normal regions.

Method: TED decomposes local anomaly scores by comparing support from source defect patches versus source normal patches mistaken as anomalous, using the host model's normal-versus-anomaly text response to determine which evidence type better explains each region.

Result: TED significantly improves pixel-level localization across both frozen VLM backbones and adapted CLIP-AD models. Improvements range from +5.0 in low-competition regimes to +10.9 in mid/high-competition regimes, outperforming baseline prompt similarity and most adapted methods on P-AUROC, P-PRO, and P-AP metrics.

Conclusion: Recoverable defect evidence exists in pretrained multimodal representations, but reliable localization requires explicit decoding against hard-normal competitors. TED enables this without retraining or modifying prompts, offering a powerful, train-free calibration for vision-language models.

Abstract: CLIP is a powerful vision-language model, but it was not designed for fine-grained defect localization; CLIP-based anomaly detectors therefore adapt it with prompts or lightweight modules to increase defect sensitivity. We show that stronger sensitivity does not necessarily make local evidence reliable: under domain shift, adapted CLIP-AD models often assign high anomaly scores to both true defects and visually complex normal regions. The issue is not simply missing defect information, but a local scoring rule that decodes defect and hard-normal evidence, having the same anomaly evidence. We propose TED (Text-Axis Evidence Decomposition), a post-hoc scoring method that asks whether each ambiguous response is better supported by source defect patches or by source normal patches mistaken as anomalous. TED compares these supports under the host's normal-versus-anomaly text response, leaves the backbone and prompts unchanged, and requires no target-domain training. It works as a train-free score for raw VLM backbones or as a source-calibrated residual correction for adapted CLIP-AD hosts. Across frozen VLM backbones, TED substantially improves pixel-level localization over raw prompt similarity; across adapted hosts, it improves most pixel-level settings over P-AUROC, P-PRO, and P-AP. Gains are largest under stronger hard-FP competition, with mean localization gain increasing from +5.0 in low-competition regimes to about +10.9 in mid/high-competition regimes. These results suggest that recoverable defect evidence can already exist in pretrained multimodal representations, but reliable localization requires decoding it against hard-normal competitors. Code will be released at TED GitHub repository.

</details>

### [71] [Large Language Models are Approximate Survival Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38181)
*Juan M Zambrano Chaves,Peniel Argaw,Risa Ueno,Carlo Bifulco,Kristina Young,Rom Leidner,Tristan Naumann,Hoifung Poon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出Survprompt框架，将患者结构化特征转化为临床描述文本，并使用预训练大语言模型（LLM）进行零样本生存预测。在两个多中心泛癌队列中评估了前沿LLM的表现，发现其在个体生存时间预测上表现接近最先进的生存分析模型（如随机生存森林），部分癌症类型下误差甚至优于传统模型；但跨癌种和机构的性能不一致，且风险分层能力较差（c-index较低）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型（LLM）在无专门训练的情况下能否准确进行生存预测，以探索其在临床预后信息提供中的潜力。

Method: 将患者结构化数据转换为自由文本临床案例，通过提示预训练大语言模型实现零样本生存预测；采用双队列验证（MSK-CHORD与新构建的普罗维登斯健康网络队列），对比传统生存模型，使用cMAE和c-index评估性能，并进行变量重要性分析。

Result: GPT-5.6-Sol等前沿模型在多个癌症类型中达到与专业生存模型相当的cMAE，尤其在前列腺癌中更优；但跨癌种、跨机构表现不稳定，风险区分能力弱（低c-index），显示其预测一致性不足。

Conclusion: 零样本大语言模型可生成令人惊讶的准确预后估计，无需专门训练，但其在不同癌症类型和医疗机构间表现不一致，限制了其临床应用价值。

Abstract: Survival analysis estimates time-to-event outcomes from patient covariates and is widely used for medical risk assessment. Patients seeking prognostic information after a diagnosis may turn to large language models (LLMs), now readily accessible through consumer applications. However, whether LLMs can provide accurate survival predictions has not been rigorously evaluated. We introduce Survprompt, a framework that converts structured patient covariates into free-text clinical vignettes and prompts pre-trained LLMs to predict survival zero-shot. We benchmark Survprompt against conventional survival models, including random survival forests (RSF), across two multi-institutional pan-cancer cohorts: the publicly available MSK-CHORD cohort and a newly curated cohort from the Providence St. Joseph Health Network constructed using an LLM-based medical abstraction framework. We report censored mean absolute error (cMAE) and concordance index (c-index) and conduct feature ablations to identify variables influencing LLM predictions. Frontier LLMs achieved surprisingly competitive cMAE for individual survival times. For example, GPT-5.6-Sol achieved cMAE within 10% of state-of-the-art RSF models specifically trained for survival prediction for several cancer types and lower cMAE than RSF for prostate cancer in MSK-CHORD. Feature ablations revealed that LLMs prioritized clinical variables similarly to specialized survival models. However, LLMs showed inconsistent accuracy across cancer types and institutions and poorly discriminated between high- and low-risk patients (lower c-index). Zero-shot LLMs can generate surprisingly accurate prognostic estimates without specialized training, but their variable performance across cancer types and institutions remains an important limitation for clinical use.

</details>


### [72] [TomasuLLM: Out-of-Order Speculative Execution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38201)
*Jiangnan Yu,Ceyu Xu,Mengming Li,Shiyu Huang,Yiran Xia,Jian Weng,Hui Xue,Haohui Mai,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TomasuLLM 是一种运行时系统，通过乱序执行代理工具调用，在保持任务执行正确性的前提下减少编码代理的延迟。它通过在隔离的写时复制沙箱中预执行未来操作，追踪依赖关系和影响，并仅在验证后按顺序提交结果，显著提升了效率。在多个基准测试中，其性能提升达1.27x-1.35x，且在4010条提交验证记录中零误接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时运行的工具（如编译器、测试套件、仓库命令）会显著增加编码代理的延迟，导致代理空闲等待。这种现象类似于推动乱序处理器发展的矛盾：顺序接口隐藏了可预测并提前启动的计算工作，但推测结果可能需经过验证后才可见。因此，需要一种机制来提前执行任务，同时保证正确性。

Method: TomasuLLM 采用乱序执行策略，通过预判未来动作，在独立的写时复制沙箱中提前执行；同时追踪这些操作的依赖关系与副作用，仅在验证通过后按原始轨迹顺序提交结果，确保执行正确性。

Result: 在三个不同规模的基准测试中，TomasuLLM 分别实现了1.31x、1.35x和1.27x的性能提升；在4,010条提交验证记录中未出现任何误接受，表明其高可靠性。

Conclusion: TomasuLLM 有效缓解了长时工具调用带来的延迟问题，通过乱序执行与严格验证机制，在不牺牲正确性的前提下显著提升编码代理的整体效率，具备良好的可扩展性和可靠性。

Abstract: Long-running tools can dominate coding-agent latency: compilers, test suites, and repository commands take seconds to minutes while the agent idles. This observation stall presents the same tension that drove out-of-order processors -- asequential interface hides work that can be predicted and started early, but a speculative result may become visible only after it and every earlier step have been validated.
  We present TomasuLLM, a runtime that executes agent tool calls out of trajectory order while preserving task-execution correctness. It drafts future actions, runs them in isolated copy-on-write sandboxes, traces their dependencies and effects, and commits results in trajectory order only after validation against committed state. Across three benchmarks spanning sub-second to minutes-long tool calls, TomasuLLM improves the reported benchmark means and scales with tool latency: 1.31x on 100 SWE-bench Verified tasks, 1.35x on 28 Terminal-Bench 2.0 tasks, and 1.27x matched progress on 18 SWE-Marathon sessions. Across 4,010 audited commit-validation records, it produces zero false accepts.

</details>


### [73] [Automatic estimation of verbal fluency index in people with Motor Neuron Disease using ASR alignment and pause modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38203)
*Bahman Mirheidari,Leslie Ing,Daniel Blackburn,Sharon Abrahams,Christopher McDermott,Heidi Christensen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于自动语音分析的系统，用于估计肌萎缩侧索硬化症（MND）患者认知障碍评估中的言语流畅性指数（VFI）。利用独特的MND数据集，结合WhisperX语音识别和Silero语音活动检测技术，并通过精细的时间戳处理，成功预测了VFI并提取了多个临床可解释的指标。该方法在多种回归算法下优于传统声学特征和自监督嵌入模型，尤其临床启发特征表现最佳，在预测P-词和S-词时分别达到R2 0.9、NRMSE 0.05和R2 0.8、NRMSE 0.08，证明了自动化VFI估算的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于MND患者常伴有言语困难，传统认知障碍评估方法难以实施，因此亟需一种无需依赖患者主动配合的自动化评估手段。言语流畅性指数（VFI）是评估认知障碍的关键指标，但其手动评估耗时且易受主观影响，故需要开发更高效、客观的替代方法。

Method: 采用WhisperX进行语音识别，使用Silero进行语音活动检测（VAD），结合精细化时间戳对语音片段进行定位；从语音信号中提取多种特征，包括临床启发特征、传统声学特征和自监督嵌入特征；通过多种回归算法比较不同特征集的表现，最终筛选出最优模型以预测VFI。

Result: 临床启发特征显著优于其他特征集，最佳模型在预测P-词时达到R2=0.9、NRMSE=0.05，预测S-词时达到R2=0.8、NRMSE=0.08，表明该系统在自动化估算VFI方面具有高度准确性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功构建了一种基于语音分析的自动化VFI估算系统，克服了MND患者因言语障碍导致的传统评估难题，为实现快速、客观、可扩展的认知功能监测提供了可行路径，具有重要的临床应用前景。

Abstract: Monitoring cognitive impairment (CI) in motor neuron disease (MND) is essential for timely treatment and care, yet challenging due to co-occurring speech difficulties. The Edinburgh Cognitive and Behavioural ALS Screen (ECAS) provides a robust metric for CI assessment, with the Verbal Fluency Index (VFI) a central element. Building on recent advances in automated speech analysis, this study proposes a system for estimating VFI. It leverages a unique MND dataset and combines ASR (WhisperX) and VAD (Silero) with refined timestamping to predict the VFI and extract several clinically interpretable measures. Our approach outperformed systems based on traditional acoustic features and self-supervised embeddings, evaluated using multiple regression algorithms. Clinically inspired features consistently outperformed the other sets, with the best models achieving strong results (P-words: R2 0.9, NRMSE 0.05; S-words: R2 0.8, NRMSE 0.08), demonstrating the feasibility of automated VFI estimation.

</details>


### [74] [The System Prompt Illusion: How Instruction Preambles Modify Computation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38205)
*Muhammad Usama,Dong Eui Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过中心核对齐（CKA）分析17个指令微调模型在20种系统提示下的层间表征，发现系统提示对模型计算的影响具有层选择性和指令类型依赖性：角色和格式类提示深度重构中间表示，而安全类提示几乎不改变表示，其变化与基线无统计差异。限制性安全提示与明确允许的提示（如“无限制”）使用几乎相同的计算路径（平均CKA相关性0.997），且在70B-72B规模下安全渗透率仍低于10%。线性探测表明模型在每层都编码提示类别，但仅在少数层重构计算，即提示被“感知”但未被“执行”，尤其是安全指令。因果激活修补验证了这些层介导行为变化，表征深度可预测行为效应大小（斯皮尔曼等级相关rho=0.761，p<0.001）。研究揭示了基于系统提示的安全机制的内在脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解系统提示如何影响大语言模型内部计算，特别是为何基于系统提示的安全机制容易被绕过。

Method: 采用中心核对齐（CKA）比较17个不同架构和参数规模（1.5B–72B）的指令微调模型在五类系统提示下的层间表征，结合线性探测、因果激活修补和相关性分析，揭示提示对模型计算的影响机制。

Result: 安全类系统提示对模型中间表示影响极小，与基线无显著差异；限制性安全提示与完全开放提示共享几乎相同的计算路径；仅少数特定层参与行为改变，且表征深度与行为效应大小显著相关。

Conclusion: 系统提示的安全机制存在根本性漏洞：模型虽感知提示内容，但对安全指令不进行深层计算重构，导致易被越狱攻击。这为改进安全控制提供了机制层面的理解。

Abstract: System prompts are the primary lever practitioners use to control language model behavior, yet what they actually do to the computation inside the transformer remains poorly understood. Across 17 instruction-tuned models spanning 8 architecture families and 1.5B to 72B parameters, we use Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) to compare layer-wise representations under 20 system prompts in five functional categories. Effects are layer-selective and instruction-type-dependent: persona and formatting instructions deeply restructure intermediate representations, while safety instructions barely move them, producing changes statistically indistinguishable from a minimal baseline. Restrictive safety instructions and explicitly permissive ones ("you have no restrictions") engage near-identical computational pathways (mean CKA correlation 0.997), and this persists at commercial scale, where safety penetration remains below 10% even at 70B-72B. A linear probing baseline exposes the mechanism: the model encodes prompt category at every layer but restructures its computation only at a small subset, so the prompt is reliably "seen" but, for safety, not deeply "acted upon." Causal activation patching confirms these layers mediate behavioral change, and representational depth predicts behavioral effect size across the full 17-model cohort (Spearman rho = 0.761, p < 0.001). The findings provide a mechanistic explanation for the persistent jailbreak vulnerability of system-prompt-based safety. Code: https://github.com/Usama1002/system-prompt-illusion-cka

</details>


### [75] [TutlAit v1: a crowdsourced Moroccan Tamazight speech dataset with Arabic transcriptions and regional accent labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38219)
*Mohamed-Amine Chadi,Ezzahra Ait El Arbi,Ismail Khayoub,Aymane Fadili,Yassine Ennhili,Jadjigua Bouali,Hanane Inhid,Mohammed Ameksa,Hajar Mousannif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TutlAit 是一个用于摩洛哥柏柏尔语（Tamazight）的语音数据集，通过众包平台收集了13,384段音频，总时长约20.9小时，涵盖Atlas、Souss、Rif和Kabyle等方言，配有阿拉伯语文本和明确的地域标签，可用于语音识别、语音翻译和口音识别研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩洛哥柏柏尔语在语音技术方面资源严重不足，公开标注音频稀缺且质量不一，缺乏地区方言信息，因此需要构建一个高质量、多方言的标注数据集以支持相关研究。

Method: 通过定制的众包网页应用TutlAit，招募母语者参与两种工作流：Text-to-Audio（输入阿拉伯语句子，录制柏柏尔语发音）和Audio-to-Text（播放柏柏尔语片段，转录为阿拉伯语），同时从公开媒体中获取并标注补充数据，所有上传文件经服务器处理为16kHz单声道WAV格式，并通过SHA-256去重、时长检查和管理员审核。

Result: 最终获得13,384个音频文件，总计75,231秒（约20.9小时，约3.01GB），其中Atlas方言占9,956个文件（14.08小时），Souss方言占3,378个文件（6.75小时），小规模包含Rif和Kabyle方言数据。

Conclusion: TutlAit数据集为摩洛哥柏柏尔语的语音技术研究提供了重要资源，适用于语音识别、语音翻译和口音识别任务，有助于推动该语言在数字技术中的发展。

Abstract: Tamazight (Amazigh) is, together with Arabic, one of the two official languages of Morocco, yet it remains severely under-resourced for speech technology: pub licly available labelled audio is scarce, generally lacks information on the regional variety spoken, and is often of uneven transcription quality. This article describes the TutlAit dataset, a corpus of Moroccan Tamazight speech paired with Modern Standard Arabic text and explicit regional accent labels. The data were collected with TutlAit, a purpose-built crowdsourcing web application (React 18 front end, Django 5 / Django REST Framework back-end, PostgreSQL database). Native speakers recruited through targeted LinkedIn and Instagram campaigns created an account, declared their regional variety (Atlas, Souss, Rif or other) and demographic information, and then contributed through two workflows: Text-to Audio, in which an Arabic sentence is displayed and the volunteer records its oral Tamazight rendering in the browser, and Audio-to-Text, in which a Tamazight excerpt is played and the volunteer types its Arabic transcription. A complemen tary set of segments was obtained from freely accessible Tamazight audiovisual media, segmented and annotated with ELAN and imported through a bulk CSV/ZIP pipeline. Every upload is converted server-side to 16kHz mono WAV, hashed with SHA-256 for duplicate rejection, checked for duration bounds and validated by an administrator. The dataset contains 13,384 audio files totalling 75,231 seconds (approximately 20.9 hours, about 3.01GB). The Atlas variety accounts for 9,956 files (14.08h) and the Souss variety for 3,378 files (6.75h); small Rif (22 files) and Kabyle (28 files) subsets are also included. The corpus can be reused for speech recognition, speech translation and accent identification for Moroccan Tamazight.

</details>


### [76] [Conformal Factuality Control for Multi-Hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38222)
*Muhammad Aimal Rehman,Chi-Kuang Yeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多跳检索增强生成（multi-hop RAG）中，基于声明级别的合取保真度控制（conformal factuality control）的有效性。通过应用拆分合取（split-conformal）声明过滤方法，在HotpotQA、Natural Questions和TriviaQA数据集上使用Llama 3.1 8B和GPT-4o-mini进行评估。结果显示，在95%的严格目标下，保留声明的完全支持率从无过滤时的55.60%-76.03%提升至95.80%-97.20%，但仅有少量声明被保留（4.41%-31.09%），且多数响应为空（9.70%-51.40%）。这表明合取保真度可扩展至多跳RAG，但可靠性需结合声明保留率与弃权率综合理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多跳RAG中，由于推理依赖多个阶段的检索与推断，生成内容的可信度面临更大挑战。现有保真度控制方法虽在单跳RAG中有效，但在多跳场景下的适用性尚不明确。因此需要评估声明级合取保真度控制是否仍能有效保障生成内容的事实准确性。

Method: 采用拆分合取（split-conformal）声明过滤方法对多跳RAG系统进行干预，在多个基准数据集（HotpotQA, Natural Questions, TriviaQA）上测试不同模型（Llama 3.1 8B, GPT-4o-mini）的表现，并设置不同严格程度的保真度目标，比较有无过滤条件下的事实支持率、声明保留率及响应非空率。

Result: 在95%保真度目标下，所有六种配置中保留声明的事实支持率均显著提升至95.80%-97.20%，远高于未过滤时的55.60%-76.03%；但同时，仅4.41%-31.09%的生成声明被保留，9.70%-51.40%的响应保持非空，说明保真度提升以牺牲输出覆盖率和完整性为代价。

Conclusion: 声明级合取保真度控制可有效应用于多跳RAG，显著提高生成内容的事实准确性。然而，其实际应用效果受制于较高的弃权率和低保留率，因此在评估系统可靠性时必须同时考虑保真度、声明保留率与响应完整性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can ground large language models in external evidence, but retrieved context does not guarantee that generated claims are factually supported. This problem is especially relevant in multi-hop RAG, where retrieval and reasoning proceed through multiple dependent stages. We study whether claim-level conformal factuality control, previously developed for RAG, remains effective in this setting. We apply split-conformal claim filtering to multi-hop RAG and evaluate it on HotpotQA, Natural Questions, and TriviaQA using Llama 3.1 8B and GPT-4o-mini, together with a single-hop reference experiment. Across all six multi-hop model-dataset configurations, increasingly stringent conformal targets consistently increase the fraction of responses whose retained claims are fully supported. At the 95% target, this rate ranges from 95.80% to 97.20%, compared with 55.60%-76.03% without filtering. However, the improvement is strongly selective: only 4.41%-31.09% of generated claims are retained and 9.70%-51.40% of responses remain non-empty at the 95% target. These results show that conformal factuality extends to multi-hop RAG, while demonstrating that nominal reliability must be interpreted jointly with claim retention and abstention.

</details>


### [77] [Framing the Narrative: Ideological Mimicry in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38256)
*Olivia Macmillan-Scott,Michael Jacobs,Nils Metternich,Mirco Musolesi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨大型语言模型（LLMs）在回答政治敏感问题时是否会因用户的政治信号（如术语选择、前提假设和个人背景）而产生意识形态模仿现象，即模型立场随交互动态变化。通过构建Poli-SHIFT数据集与评估框架，对七个开源大模型在美、英、澳三国十项政治议题上进行测试，发现仅改变术语即可导致模型支持立场反转16.9%；用户政治立场也显著影响模型输出。结果表明，模型的政治立场并非固定属性，而是依赖于具体交互情境，可能加剧信息茧房效应，强化用户既有观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在回答政治争议性问题时通常被视为具有稳定立场，但真实用户会通过术语、假设和背景传递政治信号。若模型据此调整立场，可能造成个性化政治信息环境，加剧社会分歧。因此有必要探究模型是否会出现意识形态模仿行为及其影响。

Method: 构建Poli-SHIFT数据集与评估框架，系统操控争议性术语、政治倾向性前提及用户信息，在多选题和开放文本两种格式下测试七种开源大模型在十项政治议题上的响应，涵盖美国、英国和澳大利亚。

Result: 实验显示，提示语的框架显著影响模型输出的政治立场：仅更改术语即可使模型在16.9%的对比中反转支持方向；用户的明确政治立场也会系统性引导模型向其靠拢。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的政治立场并非固定不变，而是高度依赖于与用户的互动情境。随着模型日益个性化，这种交互依赖性的适应机制可能推动形成强化用户既有观点的政治信息环境，从而加深社会分裂。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to answer questions about politically contentious issues, yet evaluations typically treat a model's stance as a relatively stable property. Real users, however, communicate political signals through their terminology, assumptions, and personal context. We investigate whether such signals produce ideological mimicry: systematic shifts in the political stance expressed by an LLM toward the position conveyed by the interaction. If LLMs adapt their responses to these signals, they risk creating personalised political information environments in which users with opposing views receive systematically different accounts of the same issue, potentially reinforcing existing divisions. We build the Poli-SHIFT dataset and evaluation framework and assess seven open-weight LLMs across ten contentious political topics in the United States, United Kingdom, and Australia, systematically manipulating contested terminology, politically valenced premises, and user information, and eliciting responses in both multiple-choice and open-text formats. Across models, we find robust evidence that prompt framing shapes the political stance of LLM outputs. Changing terminology alone reverses which side of an issue a model supports in 16.9% of matched comparisons. Stated political ideology also systematically shifts responses toward the user's position. These findings show that political stance is not a fixed property of LLMs; the views expressed are conditional on the interaction with the user. As LLMs become increasingly personalised sources of information, such interaction-dependent adaptation could contribute to political information environments that reinforce users' existing perspectives.

</details>


### [78] [ContextAdapt: Evaluating Contextual Adaptation and Value Alignment in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38260)
*Olivia Macmillan-Scott,Mirco Musolesi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨大型语言模型（LLMs）在不同专业领域（医学、法律、金融、国家安全）中是否能恰当适应诚实、自主和保密等价值观的应用，同时在上下文不改变专业规范时保持一致性。研究提出ContextAdapt评估框架，基于专业文献构建价值与领域匹配的测试场景，评估12个LLMs在行为推荐和理由解释方面的表现。结果显示，平均行为适当性达95.6%，但领域特定理由使用率差异显著（25.6%至76.9%）。明确领域或角色对模型行为影响有限，但高风险情境下模型出现局部失效，尤其在感知严重性影响披露决策时表现出不一致。研究强调，评估AI价值对齐需关注其跨情境的适切应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注抽象价值观对齐，但忽视这些价值观在具体专业情境中的适用性差异。本文旨在探究LLMs能否根据专业背景合理调整价值应用，确保在不变的专业规范下保持一致性，并识别其在高风险情境下的潜在偏差。

Method: 提出ContextAdapt评估框架，整合医学、法律、金融、国家安全领域的权威专业与监管文件，构建‘价值×领域’矩阵，设计涵盖默认规则与公认例外的测试场景；对12个LLMs进行行为推荐与理由解释评估，包括主实验与因子实验，分析领域、角色、风险程度等因素的影响。

Result: LLMs在行为推荐上总体适当性高（95.6%），但领域特定理由使用率差异大（25.6%–76.9%）；明确领域或角色对行为影响小；高风险情境下模型出现非理性转变，感知严重性成为披露决策的关键触发因素，导致在义务未变时仍改变响应。

Conclusion: AI价值对齐不仅需遵守抽象原则，更需具备在多元专业语境中准确、一致地应用这些原则的能力。当前模型在推理合理性与情境敏感性方面存在明显短板，亟需改进以实现真正意义上的专业适配。

Abstract: Values such as honesty, autonomy, and confidentiality are often regarded as general principles underpinning AI alignment. However, what it means to act in accordance with these values can depend on the context in which a decision is made. In this paper, we ask whether large language models (LLMs) appropriately adapt the application of a value across professional settings, while remaining consistent when contextual changes do not alter the relevant professional norm. To study this, we introduce ContextAdapt, an evaluation framework covering honesty, autonomy, and confidentiality across medicine, law, finance, and national security. Drawing on primary-source professional and regulatory documents, we construct a value x domain framework and use this to develop scenarios testing both default professional rules and recognised exceptions. We evaluate 12 LLMs on both the actions they recommend and the justifications they provide. In our main experiment, models achieve 95.6% mean appropriateness, although the use of the correct domain-specific justification varies substantially across models, from 25.6% to 76.9%. In a separate factorial experiment, explicitly naming the professional domain and changing the role of the model have limited effect on behaviour. Varying stakes, however, reveals severe but localised failures: in some cases, models alter their responses even though the underlying professional obligation remains unchanged. In particular, perceived severity appears to act as a cue for disclosure across both honesty and confidentiality scenarios. These results show that evaluating value alignment requires us to consider not only whether models follow abstract principles, but whether they apply them appropriately across different contexts.

</details>


### [79] [NinaXander: Feasibility and Limits of Composing Frozen Language Models Across Architecture Families via a Shared Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38261)
*Takanori Kotama,Shun-ichiro Hayashi,Daichi Mukunoki,Tetsuya Hoshino,Takahiro Katagiri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NinaXander提出一种通过共享潜空间适配器连接不同架构语言模型的组合方法，实现跨架构模型的后处理重组。该方法在不重新训练的情况下，可生成多个不同层连接的组合模型。实验使用RWKV和Pythia模型进行测试，结果显示组合模型在减少键值缓存84.4%的同时保持了良好的语法生成能力，但在多项选择任务中未达到原始Pythia模型性能，且在外部语料（WikiText）上的语言建模性能显著下降。尽管在特定条件下（相同深度、隐藏维度与共享分词器）观察到中间表示的对应性，但并未证明模型共享通用语义空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不同架构家族的冻结语言模型能否在训练后通过适配器进行有效重组，以提升效率或性能，同时避免重复训练。

Method: 采用单个训练的共享潜空间适配器，将不同架构的冻结模型（如RWKV与Pythia）在特定层之间连接，形成组合模型；通过中间表示转换实现跨模型信息传递。

Result: 组合模型在减少84.4%的键值缓存方面表现优异，生成文本语法正确；但在多项选择准确率上低于原始模型，且在跨域语料（WikiText）上语言建模性能明显下降；仅在特定条件下观察到中间表示的对应性，但不足以支持共享通用语义空间的结论。

Conclusion: 尽管跨架构组合模型在效率上有显著优势，但其性能受限于任务类型和数据分布，且目前尚无法证明不同架构模型间存在统一的语义表示空间，提示未来需进一步研究表示对齐机制。

Abstract: In this paper we propose NinaXander, a series of composed language models obtained by connecting layers of frozen language models from different architecture families with a single trained shared-latent adapter. A composed model runs the first layers of one model, converts the resulting intermediate representation once with the adapter, and then runs the remaining layers of the other model. Once the adapter is trained, several composed models that connect at different layers are obtained without retraining. Using the recurrent RWKV-4-Raven-7B and the Transformer-based Tulu-Pythia-6.9b, abbreviated as RWKV and Pythia, this study examines whether frozen models from different families can be recombined post hoc. The composed models answered multiple-choice questions, and those whose generations we examined produced syntactically well-formed text. The configuration that combines the first 5 layers of Pythia with the remaining 27 layers of RWKV reduced the Transformer key-value (KV) cache by 84.4% with accuracy not significantly different from that of RWKV alone. In multiple-choice accuracy, however, no composed model matched the parent model Pythia, and language-modeling performance decreased sharply on WikiText, a corpus of Wikipedia articles outside the training domain. The correspondence between intermediate representations was also obtained in one favorable case, with a shared tokenizer, the same depth, and the same hidden width, and does not show that the models share a general semantic space.

</details>


### [80] [Which Models Work Well Together? Measuring Heterogeneity for LLM Team Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38274)
*Liangyu Teng,Hengsong Liu,Juncen Guo,Jingyu Zhang,Yang Liu,Jing Liu,Liang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于异质性的团队选择框架，通过离线分析个体模型能力及两种互补信号（误差模式去相关性与预测行为差异性），以减少共失败并增强策略多样性。将团队选择建模为质量-互补性组合优化问题，并采用高效贪心搜索从候选池中选出小规模最优团队。实验表明，在多个基准测试中，该框架在控制候选池和团队规模下均优于仅考虑质量的基线方法，建立了可复用的多大语言模型系统团队选择原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型团队性能受限于个体能力、成员间错误共振和预测差异；尽管异质性团队在实践中有效，但缺乏可计算、可解释且可优化的互补性度量，导致团队组成依赖启发式方法。

Method: 提出异质性驱动的团队选择框架，进行离线性能剖析，量化个体能力及两类互补信号：误差模式的去相关性（降低共失败）与预测行为的差异性（捕获策略多样性）。将团队选择建模为标准化的质量-互补性组合目标函数，使用贪心搜索算法从候选池中高效选取最优小团队。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，所提框架在固定候选池和团队规模条件下，持续优于仅基于质量的基线方法，验证了其有效性与实用性，为多大语言模型系统提供了可复用的团队构建原则。

Conclusion: 本研究通过引入可计算、可解释的互补性度量，实现了对大语言模型团队的有效组合优化，显著提升了团队整体性能，为构建高性能多模型协作系统提供了新的方法论支持。

Abstract: The performance ceiling of an LLM team is constrained not only by individual model capabilities, but also by inter-member error resonance and predictive differences. Although heterogeneous teaming is often observed to be effective in practice, existing approaches lack complementarity metrics that are computable, interpretable, and optimizable, leaving team composition to rely on heuristics. We propose a heterogeneity-driven team selection framework that performs offline profiling to characterize individual capability along with two complementary signals: one captures decorrelation in error patterns to reduce co-failures, while the other measures divergence in predictive behavior to capture strategy diversity. We formulate team selection as a standardized quality--complementarity combinatorial objective and apply an efficient greedy search to select a small team from a candidate pool. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms quality-only baselines under controlled candidate pools and team sizes, establishing reusable selection principles for multi-LLM systems.

</details>


### [81] [EVOKE: Eliciting World Knowledge in Agents for Transferable Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38334)
*Yuhan Guo,Jinming Liu,Liang Xu,Ziqiang Li,Jianguo Huang,Zhicheng Wang,Hu Zhu,Qiuyu Chen,Yuntao Wei,Xin Jin,Wenjun Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EVOKE 是一种后训练方法，通过在固定状态下的目标多样性来激发大型语言模型（LLM）中内化的世界知识，从而提升多步决策任务中的泛化能力和数据效率。该方法通过在相同状态下对候选动作进行多目标排序，迫使策略依赖深层世界模型而非表面习惯，实现更鲁棒的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统后训练方法缺乏对模型内部世界知识的充分激发，导致模型在未见环境中表现不佳；由于单个目标的监督容易使模型依赖表面情境习惯，因此需要新的机制来促进对预训练中已内化的世界知识的利用。

Method: EVOKE 通过保持环境状态和交互历史不变，对同一组候选动作在不同目标下进行排序，强制策略调整其行动偏好，从而揭示并利用其内在的世界模型。

Result: 在多个任务和三种模型架构上，EVOKE 显著提升了任务性能、未见环境的泛化能力以及数据效率，并通过受控分析验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 通过直接决策监督实现对预训练中内化世界知识的显式激发，是提升 LLM 代理在复杂任务中可迁移性和鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as agents for multi-step decision-making, yet transfer poorly to unseen environments. World-model methods address this by training agents to predict future observations, at the cost of additional training and errors that compound when predictions are used for planning. However, for LLM agents operating in digital environments, much of this world knowledge is already internalized during pretraining, which shifts the problem from acquiring it to eliciting it. We argue that typical post-training provides little pressure for such elicitation, since supervision under a single goal at each visited state inadvertently drives policies to rely on superficial contextual habits. We introduce EVOKE, a post-training method that supplies this pressure through goal diversity at fixed states. Motivated by theory showing that an agent competent across diverse goals must encode a world model recoverable from its action preferences, EVOKE holds the environment state and interaction history fixed and ranks the same candidate actions under alternative goals, forcing action preferences to change, so that a policy relying on contextual habits or single-goal correlations cannot order them correctly. This implicitly elicits the policy's pretrained world knowledge to inform decisions. We evaluate EVOKE across diverse tasks in three backbones, demonstrating improved task performance, unseen environment generalization, and data efficiency. We further conduct controlled analyses to better understand what drives these gains. These findings offer a new perspective on eliciting internalized world knowledge for transferable action through direct decision supervision.

</details>


### [82] [Halluscoring 2026: The first shared task on llms hallucination detection and answer verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38355)
*Aisha Alansari,Abdessalam Bouchekif,Ahmed Hasanaath,Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Malak Alkhorasani,Mohammed-En-Nadhir Zighem,Saad Ezzini,Hichem Telli,Hend Al-Khalifa,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed,Hadid Abdenour,Hamzah Luqman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HalluScoring 2026 is a shared task evaluating hallucination detection and factual verification in Arabic question answering under challenging generalization conditions. It includes four subtasks: binary hallucination detection on unseen questions (1.1) and responses from unseen LLMs (1.2), and answer selection among six candidates for Islamic (2.1) and general knowledge (2.2). Based on HalluScore and HalluTruthQA datasets, 13 teams participated, with 10 submitting system papers. Results show limited performance in hallucination detection under distribution shift (AUC-ROC up to 0.772), while answer selection shows stronger results (up to 0.882).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To evaluate the robustness of hallucination detection and factual verification systems in Arabic question answering under real-world distribution shifts, especially when facing unseen questions and outputs from new LLMs, as well as to assess their ability to identify correct answers in complex domains like Islamic knowledge.

Method: The shared task uses two Arabic datasets—HalluScore and HalluTruthQA—to benchmark systems across four subtasks. Evaluation focuses on binary hallucination detection (Task 1) and multi-choice factual answer identification (Task 2), with systems assessed under both standard and assisted evaluation settings.

Result: For Task 1, top-performing systems achieved AUC-ROC scores of 0.772 (Subtask 1.1) and 0.767 (Subtask 1.2), indicating ongoing challenges in hallucination detection under distribution shift. For Task 2, the best systems reached F1 scores of 0.882 (Islamic knowledge, Subtask 2.1) and 0.857 (general knowledge, Subtask 2.2) under assisted evaluation, showing better performance in answer selection.

Conclusion: HalluScoring 2026 highlights persistent difficulties in detecting hallucinations in Arabic QA under distributional shifts, despite progress in factual answer identification. The results underscore the need for more robust models capable of generalizing across diverse inputs and domains, particularly in culturally and religiously sensitive contexts.

Abstract: We present HalluScoring 2026, a shared task for evaluating hallucination detection and factual verification in Arabic question answering under challenging generalization settings. The shared task is organized into two main tasks, each comprising two subtasks, for a total of four subtasks. Task 1 evaluates binary hallucination detection, considering generalization to unseen questions (Subtask 1.1) and responses generated by unseen LLMs (Subtask 1.2). Task 2 extends the evaluation beyond detection by requiring the systems to additionally identify the correct factual answer from six related candidates, covering Islamic knowledge (Subtask 2.1) and general knowledge (Subtask 2.2). The shared task is based on two Arabic datasets: HalluScore and HalluTruthQA. A total of 13 teams participated in the shared task, 10 of which submitted system description papers. The results of Task 1 demonstrate that hallucination detection remains challenging under distribution shift, with the winning team achieving AUC-ROC test scores of 0.772 and 0.767 for Subtasks 1.1 and 1.2, respectively. For Task 2, the winning team achieved scores of 0.882 and 0.857 in the Islamic and general-knowledge subtasks, respectively, under assisted evaluation.

</details>


### [83] [Evaluating Language Model Safety Across Long Adversarial Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38357)
*Parisa Salmani,Peter R. Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在持续对抗性对话中，语言模型的安全性是否能够保持。结果显示，尽管单轮对话中安全响应率较高（85%-100%），但在多轮对话中（深度达11时下降至38%-61%，深度101时降至15%-44%），安全响应率显著降低。这表明强单轮安全性无法保证长期互动中的安全，强调需要长周期评估和对话级防护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话安全评估多基于单个有害提示，但真实场景中用户会通过多轮、动态交互施加压力。为更贴近实际，需考察模型在持续对抗性对话下的表现。

Method: 选取三个开源指令微调模型，在两个有害提示下进行多轮对话测试；使用另一模型作为持续对抗性用户，通过安全分类器逐轮标注响应安全性，分析不同对话长度与随机种子下的安全表现变化。

Result: 所有模型-提示组合中，首轮安全响应率为85%-100%；深度11时降至38%-61%；深度101时进一步降至15%-44%。安全性能随对话轮次增加显著下降，且该趋势跨模型普遍存在。

Conclusion: 强单轮安全性不能确保长期对抗性交互中的安全表现。当前短周期评估不足以反映真实风险，亟需引入长周期评估框架与对话级风险防控机制。

Abstract: Conversational safety evaluations often test language models with a single harmful prompt, even though real-world systems interact with users through long, adaptive conversations. This study examines whether models continue to respond safely when an adversarial user persists across multiple turns. We evaluate three open-weight, instruction-tuned models on two harmful prompts across different conversation lengths and random seeds. In each setting, a second language model acts as a persistent adversarial user, while a safety classifier labels every response as safe or unsafe. Across all model-prompt combinations, first-turn safe-response rates ranged from 85% to 100%. By depth 11, they dropped to 38-61%, and by depth 101, to 15-44%. This decline appeared across models and continued well beyond the short interactions typically used in multi-turn safety evaluations. These results provide proof-of-concept evidence that strong single-turn safety does not necessarily persist during sustained adversarial interaction. They highlight the need for long-horizon evaluations and conversation-level safeguards that account for risk accumulating across turns.

</details>


### [84] [Doc2LoRA Provides Decodable Representations of Scientific Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38374)
*Chand Sahil Mansuri,Joel Zachariah,Sadamori Kojaku*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出使用Doc-to-LoRA超网络生成的LoRA适配器表示科学论文，将论文嵌入向量空间，实现搜索与生成双重功能。通过混合向量可生成新思想点，每个点对应一个可接受自然语言指令的大语言模型（LLM），从而支持对新概念的探索与描述。实验表明，该方法在子领域命名任务中表现优于五种基线，且在论文摘要生成中能平滑过渡。经可逆变换后，嵌入向量在搜索任务上达到SPECTER2、EmbeddingGemma水平，接近SBERT，同时保持与LLM的映射关系，实现搜索与生成一体化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有论文向量化方法多侧重于搜索，难以支持生成式探索；而生成式方法产生的混合点缺乏实际论文支持，无法解释其含义。本研究旨在构建一个兼具高效搜索与自然语言生成能力的统一向量空间，使任意点（包括混合点）都能被解释和提问，以促进创新性想法的发现与生成。

Method: 采用Doc-to-LoRA超网络为每篇论文生成LoRA适配器，将论文表示为可调用大语言模型的向量点；通过线性插值混合不同论文的向量，在中间点上运行LLM生成摘要或命名子领域；引入小规模可逆变换，使嵌入向量适用于传统检索任务，同时保留与LLM的映射关系。

Result: 在APS数据集上，该方法在子领域命名任务中比五种基线更接近官方名称（基于词重叠和五名LLM评委评估）；在跨论文摘要生成中，生成内容随混合权重平滑过渡；嵌入向量在搜索任务中性能接近SPECTER2、EmbeddingGemma和SBERT，且具备可逆性，支持双向应用。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于LoRA适配器的论文向量化框架实现了搜索与生成的统一，使研究人员能够在向量空间中的任意位置提问并生成新想法，显著提升了科学知识探索的灵活性与创造性。

Abstract: Representing scientific papers as points in a space lets us search for similar papers and inquire about how fields relate to one another and drive innovation. Beyond search, the vector space of papers invites generation: mixing papers through simple vector operations creates new points, mirroring combinatorial novelty, the recombination of existing ideas into new ones. However, a mixed point often represents an idea no paper has yet realized, with no papers nearby to identify the idea. We propose representing each paper by a LoRA adapter generated by the Doc-to-LoRA hypernetwork. Every point in the space, including mixtures, thus represents a large language model (LLM) open to questions and instructions in natural language. On papers from the American Physical Society (APS), we instruct the LLM at the average of each subfield to name the field in a few words and obtain labels closer to the official names than the labels of five baselines, as judged by word overlap and a panel of five LLM judges. We also ask the LLMs at points between two APS papers to write an abstract and obtain descriptions shifting from one paper to the other in step with the mixing weight. While Doc-to-LoRA is trained for generation, a small invertible transform makes the embeddings competitive for search, on par with SPECTER2 and EmbeddingGemma and close to SBERT. Because the transform is invertible, every point in the transformed space still maps back to an LLM. The embeddings thus serve both search and generation, enabling researchers to question the idea at any point in the space as a starting point for generating new ideas.

</details>


### [85] [Evaluating Whether LLMs Can Reliably Connect the DOTs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38406)
*Eftekhar Hossain,John Salvador,Santu Karmaker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含约9.2K个实例的多领域叙事补全基准，涵盖百科文本、常识故事、新闻文章和视觉叙事四种类型，用于评估大语言模型在真实叙事补全任务中的表现。实验评估了20个指令微调的开源模型（1.5B-70B参数），发现模型规模并非决定性能的关键因素：Gemma-2-2B在定性评分中表现最佳（4.02/5），甚至优于多个十倍更大的模型。此外，链式思维推理带来的提升有限（仅+0.6%），而短篇叙事与领域特性比补全位置更能预测任务难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型虽使用文本补全作为预训练目标，但其在真实世界叙事补全任务中的实际表现尚未充分研究，尤其缺乏系统性评估基准与多维度分析。

Method: 构建一个覆盖四类叙事类型的多领域文本补全基准（~9.2K实例），通过掩码一至三句话生成补全任务；评估20个不同规模的指令微调开源LLM，结合自动指标与涵盖五个叙事维度的定性框架进行综合评价。

Result: 模型规模与性能无可靠关联，小模型Gemma-2-2B在定性评分中领先于多个更大模型；链式思维推理带来边际提升；短篇叙事与领域特征是影响任务难度的主要因素，而非补全位置。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在叙事补全任务中表现受多种因素影响，模型规模并非决定性优势，需关注领域特性、文本长度及推理策略设计，未来应构建更具针对性的训练与评估机制。

Abstract: Access to real-world information is often noisy and fragmented. Constructing a coherent narrative from such fragments requires models to reconstruct missing spans within a broader storyline, commonly referred to as text infilling, while preserving consistency with both the local context and the global storyline. Despite using text infilling as a pre-training objective in many Large Language Models (LLMs), their actual performance on real-world narrative infilling remains underexplored. In this paper, we address this gap by introducing a multi-domain benchmark of ~9.2K instances for narrative infilling, constructed by masking one to three sentences across four narrative types: encyclopedic text, commonsense stories, news articles, and visual narratives. Using this benchmark, we evaluate 20 instruction-tuned open-source LLMs ranging from 1.5B to 70B parameters across varying levels of instruction specificity and reasoning guidance. Outputs are assessed using standard automatic metrics and a qualitative framework covering five narrative dimensions. Results show that model scale does not reliably predict infilling quality: Gemma-2-2B achieves the highest qualitative score (4.02/5), outperforming models over ten times larger, including DeepSeek-Qwen-32B (3.77/5, 6.6%) and LLaMA-3.3-70B (3.71/5, 8.3%). We further find that explicit reasoning offers limited benefits as chain-of-thought reasoning yields only a marginal improvement (+0.6%). Additionally, short narratives and domain characteristics emerge as stronger predictors of task difficulty than infill position alone for narrative infilling in current LLMs.

</details>


### [86] [Policy-Conditioned AI-Use Detection: An Evidentiary Framework for Academic Publishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38427)
*Jairo Diaz-Rodriguez,Mumin Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种政策条件的AI使用检测框架，旨在更准确地评估人类-人工智能协作是否符合会议或期刊的既定规则。传统AI检测仅判断文本是否由AI生成，但该方法的目标与实际政策执行需求不一致。新框架将政策作为显式输入，通过推理报告假设、证据、校准机制和不确定性，而非简单判定‘检测到AI’。评估基于可复现的工作流管道构建基准，衡量在预定误报率下的真正报率。在同行评审场景中，即使在合理违规率下，强性能检测器仍可能错误标记更多合规作者。因此，该框架强调必须部署结构化披露、受保护的工具路由及申诉路径。检测器不再是单纯的作者身份分类器，而是一种可审计、误差率可控且可辩护的程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学术会议和期刊对作者、审稿人等角色使用AI制定了详细规则，但现有AI检测工具仅判断文本是否由AI生成，与实际政策执行目标不匹配。这导致检测结果难以用于真实合规性判断，尤其在涉及人类-人工智能协作时存在严重偏差。因此需要一种能与具体政策对齐的检测框架，以支持公正、可审计的合规评估。

Method: 提出政策条件的AI使用检测框架，将政策规则作为输入，结合推理过程报告假设、证据、校准方式和不确定性；建立基于可复现工作流的评估基准，量化真阳性率与预设假阳性率的关系；强调需配置结构化披露、受保护的工具路由及申诉机制，使检测成为可审计的程序而非黑箱判决。

Result: 在同行评审场景中，即使在合理违规率下，现有强性能检测器仍会错误标记多数合规作者，说明单纯依赖文本生成检测不可靠。新框架表明，检测应被设计为具有预设误差率、可辩护的审计程序，并依赖结构化披露与申诉路径来保障公平性。

Conclusion: AI检测不应以‘是否由AI撰写’为目标，而应服务于具体政策的合规评估。政策条件的检测框架提供了一个可解释、可审计、可辩护的评估体系，要求学术出版机构配套建设披露机制、保密工具路由和申诉渠道，才能实现真正有效的AI使用治理。

Abstract: Major venues now publish detailed rules about how authors, reviewers, and area chairs may use AI, and those rules differ by role, by task, and by what must be disclosed. AI detection, the instrument usually proposed to enforce them, estimates something else: whether an AI model wrote the text. We argue that this target is misaligned with the decisions conferences and journals face, and propose policy-conditioned AI-use detection, an evidentiary framework for assessing whether a human--AI workflow complied with a stated rule. Policy makes the governing rule an explicit input. Inference reports hypotheses, evidence, calibration regime, and uncertainty in place of verdicts such as "AI detected". Evaluation builds benchmarks from reproducible pipelines that generate compliant and non-compliant workflows, and reports true positive rate at a false positive rate the venue fixes in advance. We work the framework through peer review, where at plausible violation rates a detector at a strong operating point still flags more compliant authors than violating ones. The framework therefore also names what a venue must instrument: structured disclosure, approved-tool routing that respects reviewer confidentiality, and a path by which a finding can be contested. Under this framing a detector is not an authorship classifier but an auditable procedure with an error rate the venue fixes in advance and can defend.

</details>


### [87] [The Backdrop Exposes What the World Around an Agent Costs It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38469)
*Nusrat Jahan Lia,Shubhashis Roy Dipta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BACKDROP evaluates how well agents perform in dynamic, real-world-like environments by introducing four common hazards: authority (external messages overriding user instructions), injection (malicious text in records), boundary (unauthorized app access), and fault (uncertain write outcomes). Across 3,678 task variants and 16 models, average pass rates drop from 69.5% to 31.3% when all hazards are present, with top models like Claude Fable 5.1 declining from 96.6% to 56.0%. Agents often follow unauthorized human messages (46.4%) and injected text (20.3%), revealing significant vulnerabilities despite strong performance in static environments. The study highlights that clean-world benchmarks overestimate real-world capability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing agent benchmarks evaluate performance in static environments, failing to reflect real-world dynamics where external entities influence agent behavior. This gap limits the reliability of benchmark scores as predictors of real-world performance.

Method: BACKDROP introduces four controlled hazards—authority, injection, boundary, and fault—into a task's execution environment, one at a time and together, while keeping instructions and desired outcomes unchanged. It tests 16 models across 3,678 task variants to measure degradation in performance under realistic conditions.

Result: Average pass rate drops from 69.5% to 31.3% when all four hazards are active. Top models show the largest decline. Even when malicious content reaches the agent, 46.4% of runs follow unauthorized human messages and 20.3% are influenced by injected text. These patterns persist across all models.

Conclusion: Agent performance in static benchmarks serves as an upper bound for real-world effectiveness. BACKDROP reveals critical vulnerabilities in current agents' robustness to real-world perturbations, emphasizing the need for more dynamic and adversarial evaluation frameworks.

Abstract: Agent benchmarks test agents in worlds that stay still. Deployed agents work in worlds that other people also change. Someone texts the agent to send the money elsewhere or an order confirmation asks it to reply with a door code. We present BACKDROP, which asks how much of an agent's capability in a clean world survives in such a world. BACKDROP takes a task along with the agents execution environment, and plants four everyday hazards in its world, one at a time and all together. The instruction and the correct end state stay the same. Each hazard asks one question. Authority: does a message from another person override the user? Injection: does text planted in a record redirect the agent? Boundary: does a request pull it into an app it was not given? Fault: after a write fails without saying whether it landed, does the agent check before it retries? Across 3,678 variants and 16 models, , the average pass rate falls from 69.5% to 31.3% once all four hazards are present; the strongest models fall furthest (Claude Fable 5.1 from 96.6% to 56.0%). Agents have learned to resist injected text but often follow other unauthorized requests of other people. With all four hazards present, and counting only runs where the planted text reached the agent, agents followed another person's message in 46.4% of runs and injected text in 20.3%. The gap is consistent throughout all 16 models. BACKDROP formalizes these gaps and shows how an agent's score in a task's world is a ceiling on real-world performance.

</details>


### [88] [KlinikeBench: Evaluating Language Models Beyond Diagnostic Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38480)
*Xueting Fang,Zehui Li,Yang Yang,Camilla Giovino,Shubh K. Patel,Shailly Prajapati,Vallijah Subasri,Caihua Shan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KlinikeBench 是一个由333个临床医生编写的任务组成的基准，模拟真实的临床交互过程，评估语言模型在与虚拟患者对话、获取病史、请求检查和做出诊断等多步骤中的表现。研究发现，尽管部分模型诊断准确率高达90.7%，但在完整临床评估中成功率不足30%，表明诊断准确率无法反映模型在真实临床交互中的综合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床语言模型评估基准仅基于完整的病例描述进行诊断准确性测试，忽略了临床实践中信息收集、问诊、检查决策和交互过程的重要性，且缺乏专业临床医生的验证。因此需要一个更贴近真实临床流程的评估框架。

Method: 构建KlinikeBench基准，包含333个由超过35名临床医生参与编写和评估的任务，每个任务提供虚拟患者、临床工具和具体成功标准；设定固定对话轮次预算，对模型在问诊、检查请求、诊断记录等各环节的表现分别评分，并综合评估。

Result: 在31个模型和7个模型家族中，最佳模型（如GPT-6-astra和Claude Opus 5）在任务成功率上仍低于30%，即使其诊断准确率达到90.7%；部分模型在对话中表现更好，而另一些则在静态病历上表现优异但互动中表现差，揭示了诊断准确率与交互式临床评估能力之间的显著差距。

Conclusion: KlinikeBench为评估语言模型在完整临床交互中的表现提供了有效测试平台，凸显了当前模型在真实临床评估中存在重大能力短板，亟需改进其交互理解与决策能力。

Abstract: Most clinical benchmarks evaluate language models (LMs) on diagnosis using complete case descriptions. In clinical practice, however, patients present information in different ways, and clinicians must obtain relevant history and determine which examinations are needed before reaching a diagnosis. Diagnostic accuracy alone therefore cannot establish whether an agent gathered essential information or conducted an appropriate clinical assessment. Furthermore, existing benchmarks lack professional clinicians' verification. To address this gap, we introduce KlinikeBench, a benchmark of 333 clinician-authored tasks, each providing an isolated sandbox environment with a virtual patient, clinical tools, and task-specific success criteria. More than 35 clinicians contributed to case authoring and benchmark evaluation. In an empirical study, clinicians gave simulated dialogues higher mean quality ratings than reference conversations, which is adapted from real conversation. In each task, an LM has a fixed budget of turns to communicate with the patient, ask about relevant history, request examinations, follow action constraints, and record a final diagnosis. We score these steps separately as well as together. Across 31 models and seven model families, the best-performing models (e.g., GPT-6-astra and Claude Opus 5) succeed on less than 30% of tasks, even though their diagnosis accuracy reaches 90.7%. Some models benefit from talking with the patient; others diagnose well from a complete chart but perform much worse in conversation. Overall, KlinikeBench provides a testbed for evaluating the full clinical encounter and reveals a substantial gap between diagnostic accuracy and performance in interactive clinical assessment.

</details>


### [89] [Personalized State-Transition-Aware Memory for Clinical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38490)
*Maryam Haghifam,Zahra Rajabi,Yizhou Sun,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STAM 是一种状态转移感知的记忆框架，用于在临床记录中跟踪患者状态变化，通过结合语义检索与类型化临床关系，区分当前活跃信息与已过时或解决的信息，并在查询时通过依赖查询的门控机制选择性地调用历史记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在处理临床记录时，大型语言模型代理需要同时追踪患者状态的变化并保留历史信息以理解治疗过程和临床轨迹，但传统方法要么积累过多记忆导致信息模糊，要么覆盖早期记忆而丢失关键历史证据。

Method: STAM 利用语义检索与类型化的临床关系识别受影响的记忆，将当前信息存储于 Active 模块，将已过时或解决的信息归档至 History 模块，并在读取时使用基于查询的门控机制动态选择性访问历史记忆。

Result: 在四个纵向临床基准测试中，STAM 在下游问答、直接状态维护诊断任务中表现优异，且在近似匹配的上下文长度下优于对比方法。

Conclusion: STAM 有效解决了临床记忆管理中的状态演化与历史保留难题，为大语言模型在医疗场景中的长期推理提供了可靠的记忆机制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents that reason over clinical records must track changes in a patient's state while preserving the history needed to understand them. Simply accumulating memories leaves it unclear which information still applies, whereas overwriting earlier memories can erase evidence needed to reconstruct treatment history and clinical trajectories. We introduce STAM, a state-transition-aware memory framework that records state changes as new clinical entries arrive. STAM combines semantic retrieval with typed clinical relations to identify affected memories, maintaining current information in Active and superseded or resolved information in History. At read time, a query-dependent gate selectively serves historical memory. Across four longitudinal clinical benchmarks, we evaluate STAM with downstream question answering, direct state-maintenance diagnostics, and comparisons at approximately matched context lengths.

</details>


### [90] [DEdit: Iterative Draft Editing for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38510)
*Longxuan Yu,Bingsen Chen,Peng Shi,Dongkyu Lee,Yi Xiang,Hideo Kobayashi,Sheng Zhang,Shuaichen Chang,Xing Niu,Zhuoyan Xu,Greg Ver Steeg,Jiarong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DEdit提出一种基于扩散的轻量级草案模型，通过迭代编辑机制修复早期错误并扩展可接受前缀，显著提升自回归大模型的生成效率。其核心创新在于引入双向上下文支持的逐标记修正能力，并结合ProposalMix训练策略以减少有害编辑。在多个基准测试中，DEdit在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上分别实现了5.72×和5.97×的平均加速，且接受率随编辑次数和草案窗口扩大而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散基草案模型因独立预测导致单个早期错误即丢弃整个草案，限制了效率提升；需引入纠错与修正机制，利用后续预测作为双向上下文修复错误并延长有效输出前缀。

Method: 提出DEdit框架，结合扩散模型进行并行草案生成，并引入迭代编辑模块，通过token-to-token预测实现对已有草案的逐步修正；设计ProposalMix训练方法，在训练中根据首次预测置信度混合草案与真实标签，引导模型学习保留正确部分、修正错误部分的能力。

Result: 在七个基准任务上，DEdit在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上分别获得5.72×和5.97×的宏平均加速，显著优于其他草案模型；接受率随编辑轮次和草案宽度增加而提升；ProposalMix使有害编辑减少50%；禁用未来上下文（仅因果注意力）会降低接受率，尤其在高可预测输出中，说明未来信息对纠错至关重要。

Conclusion: DEdit通过引入迭代编辑机制与双向上下文感知的修正能力，有效缓解了传统扩散草案中因早期错误导致的前缀丢弃问题，大幅提升了草案接受率与推理速度。ProposalMix训练策略保障了修正过程的稳定性，验证了未来上下文在纠错中的关键作用，为高效自回归生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive LLMs by having a lightweight drafter propose tokens that the target model verifies in parallel. Diffusion-based drafters further reduce drafting latency by proposing multiple tokens at once. However, these tokens are predicted independently, so a single early error causes prefix verification to discard the rest of the draft, even when it contains useful downstream predictions. We introduce DEdit, a diffusion-based drafter that can not only draft by conventional parallel unmasking but also iteratively edit its draft through token-to-token predictions. Through editing, later predictions can serve as bidirectional context for repairing earlier errors and extending the accepted prefix. To teach the model to repair errors while preserving correct predictions, we propose ProposalMix, a training scheme that mixes draft predictions with ground-truth tokens based on first-pass confidence during training. Across seven benchmarks on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, DEdit achieves the highest macro-average token acceptance and speedup among the evaluated drafters, reaching macro-average speedups of $5.72\times$ and $5.97\times$ over autoregressive generation under greedy decoding, respectively. Further analysis shows that acceptance improves with more editing passes and wider drafting windows, and that ProposalMix halves harmful edits that shorten the accepted prefix. Moreover, restricting the editor to causal attention lowers acceptance, especially on highly predictable outputs, indicating that future context is a key source of these gains.

</details>


### [91] [Shifting Mechanisms: How Positional Encoding Choice Shapes In-Context Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38530)
*Eric Enouen,Sainyam Galhotra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同位置编码（PE）策略对语言模型在上下文内检索行为的影响。通过分析22个开源模型，发现使用标准RoPE的模型主要依赖位置检索，而混合PE架构（如SWA NoPE）则转向语义检索。进一步实验表明，将位置编码限制在局部层会引发这种语义转变，并削弱位置信息表示。此外，尽管混合PE架构在多目标检索和问答任务中表现更好，但在区分竞争性键时性能下降，揭示出一种检索机制的权衡，而非普遍的长上下文优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同位置编码策略如何影响语言模型在上下文中的检索机制，尤其是随着注意力跨度和编码方式在各层变化时的行为差异。

Method: 采用机制视角，系统分析22个开放权重模型（来自8个模型家族）在不同位置编码设置下的内部检索机制；通过控制预训练消融实验验证位置编码分布对检索策略的影响；并评估多种任务上的表现以揭示潜在的检索权衡。

Result: 标准RoPE模型依赖位置检索，而混合PE模型（如SWA NoPE）更倾向于语义检索；将位置编码局限于局部层可诱发该机制转变，但削弱位置表示能力；混合架构虽在多目标检索和问答中表现更优，但在区分相似关键信息时性能下降，说明其提升并非全局性，而是机制转移的结果。

Conclusion: 位置编码的设计深刻影响模型的检索机制：从位置到语义的转变是系统性的，且伴随着性能上的权衡。因此，长上下文性能的提升不应被简单视为整体改进，而应理解为检索策略的根本变化。

Abstract: Language models increasingly use architectures that vary attention span and positional encoding across layers, such as applying RoPE with sliding-window attention and NoPE with global attention (SWA NoPE). However, how these choices shape in-context retrieval remains unclear. To study this question, we take a mechanistic view, tracing how positional encoding (PE) choice shapes the internal mechanisms models use for in-context retrieval. Across 22 open-weight models spanning eight families, we find that standard RoPE models rely primarily on positional retrieval, while PE hybrids shift toward semantic retrieval. We further show on a controlled pre-training ablation that confining positional encoding to local layers produces this semantic shift, degrading representations of positional information. Finally, we show that the reported long-context gains of PE hybrids mask a retrieval trade-off: SWA NoPE improves over RoPE on multiple-target retrieval and QA, but degrades when distinguishing competing keys. We show that these behavioral differences better track the mechanism shift from positional toward semantic mechanisms than a uniform improvement in long-context retrieval.

</details>


### [92] [MedKIT: Evaluating Knowledge Integration and Generalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38543)
*Lukas Thede,Yash Kumar Atri,David Chen,Danielle Bitterman,Matthias Bethge,Tom Hartvigsen,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedKIT 是一个用于评估医学知识整合与迁移的基准测试，旨在衡量模型在面对临床证据更新时，新知识是否真正可用。研究发现，尽管大多数方法在事实回忆和词汇变化下表现良好，但在关系泛化、组合推理和实际应用方面表现不佳，揭示了知识整合中召回率与实际可用性之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学领域知识不断更新，过时的知识可能带来安全风险。现有评估方法仅关注事实召回，无法判断新知识是否真正可用，因此需要更细致的评估框架来检验知识的实际应用能力。

Method: 构建 MedKIT 基准，包含基于临床证据的事实更新实例，并设计多种探测任务（如词汇变异、关系变换、组合推理、开放操作化及局部性测试），用于评估模型在不同情境下的知识整合与迁移能力。对 12 种知识整合策略在 5 种模型上进行大规模实证研究。

Result: 多数方法在原始任务和词汇变化下表现良好，但在关系泛化、组合推理和实际操作任务中提升有限，无一方法在复杂任务中取得显著成效，表明当前知识整合方法在实用性上存在根本性局限。

Conclusion: 知识整合不仅需提升召回率，更需确保新知识在多样化任务和上下文中可被有效使用。MedKIT 为开发更具实用性的知识整合方法提供了重要测试平台。

Abstract: Constantly evolving real-world knowledge necessitates models to be updated continuously. Especially in medicine, as clinical evidence changes over time, outdated knowledge can pose safety risks. Existing evaluations of knowledge integration focus on factual recall, offering limited insight into whether newly integrated knowledge is actually usable. Our benchmark MedKIT (Medical Knowledge Integration and Transfer) provides a granular evaluation of how models integrate and apply knowledge under realistic sequences of clinical updates. Each instance corresponds to a factual update derived from clinical evidence, paired with targeted probes that assess transfer across lexical variation, relational transformations, compositional reasoning, and open-ended operationalization, as well as locality tests for knowledge preservation. Using MedKIT, we conduct a large-scale empirical study of 12 knowledge integration strategies across 5 diverse models, including both general-purpose and medical LLMs. Our results reveal a consistent gap between recall and usable knowledge: while most methods achieve strong gains on the original update task and under lexical variation, relational generalization is limited, and no method yields meaningful improvements on compositional or operational tasks. These findings highlight a fundamental challenge in knowledge integration and position MedKIT as a testbed for developing methods that make newly integrated knowledge more consistently usable across tasks and contexts.

</details>


### [93] [Prompt2Skill: Unsupervised Skill Optimization From Natural Language Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38593)
*Bo Ni,Li Li,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt,Tyler Derr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Prompt2Skill 是一种仅基于自然语言任务描述构建技能的框架，通过从提示中推导任务规范、发现或合成数据集，并在反射式编辑的闭环中优化技能。该方法无需依赖预设的训练数据集，在多个领域（如问答、阅读理解、电子表格操作和数学推理）中均显著优于直接提示基线，平均提升10.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专家编写的技能成本高且不针对特定模型优化，新任务常超出已有技能库范围，而现有自动化技能优化方法依赖于有标注的分布内训练数据，用户难以获取。因此需要一种无需额外训练数据、能从自然语言任务描述自动生成有效技能的方法。

Method: Prompt2Skill 通过分析自然语言任务描述，自动推导任务规范；利用检索或生成技术发现或合成相关数据集；并通过多轮反射式编辑不断优化技能，形成闭环改进流程。

Result: 在四个不同领域（问答、阅读理解、电子表格操作、数学推理）的实验中，Prompt2Skill 在开源和前沿大模型上均显著优于直接提示基线，平均性能提升达10.8点。

Conclusion: Prompt2Skill 实现了从自然语言任务描述到高质量技能的自动化构建，克服了传统技能制作成本高、适配性差及依赖标注数据的局限，为 LLM 在动态任务场景中的高效应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Skills are external artifacts that Large Language Models (LLMs) consume at inference time to improve their performance on specialized domains by incorporating relevant procedural and domain knowledge. Expert-authored skills are expensive to produce, and the resulting artifacts are not optimized for the specific model that consumes them, whose failure modes can vary with version, scale and training. In addition, emerging tasks may fall outside the scope of existing skill libraries, creating a need to develop new skills before curated training data become available. Recent works have explored automated skill optimization through reflection, but they require a curated, in-distribution training set, which users might not always have. To address these limitations, we present Prompt2Skill, a framework that builds skills from natural-language task description alone. From the prompt, the system derives a task specification, discovers or synthesizes datasets, and refines the skill in a closed loop of reflective editing. Across four domains spanning question answering, reading comprehension, spreadsheet manipulation, and mathematical reasoning, Prompt2Skill consistently outperforms the direct prompting baseline, achieving an average improvement of 10.8 across open-source and frontier models.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond Oracle Communication: Benchmarking Interactive Intent Alignment Under Miscommunication and Evolving User Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38604)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Mengyuan Chao,Ke Xiao,Ziyi Chen,Daoan Zhang,Yan Zhang,Yanfang Ye,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出交互意图对齐（Interactive Intent Alignment）的新任务设定，针对用户沟通不完整、目标变化及耐心有限等现实问题。为此构建了Drift-Bench++基准测试框架，支持可控的意图漂移与可执行任务验证，并设计了GRIP评估协议，涵盖任务理解、用户真实性、提问有效性及意图适应性。实验表明，尽管更强的交互能力有助于提升表现，但仍远未达到理想情况下的性能；真实部署数据也证实此类失败普遍存在且影响重大。该工作为评估和改进智能体在真实交互场景中的表现提供了统一、可执行的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型代理评估基准假设用户始终准确充分地传达固定意图，但现实中用户常出现误述、目标变更或失去耐心，导致当前评估体系无法反映真实交互挑战。因此亟需一种能模拟不完美沟通与动态意图的评估框架，以推动智能体在复杂交互中更稳健地理解并适应用户意图。

Method: 提出Drift-Bench++，一个系统化的基准构建流程，用于生成具有可控意图漂移和可执行性的任务；设计包含有限耐心、多样化模拟用户及静默条件下意图突变的交互协议；开发GRIP评估协议，全面衡量模型在任务对齐、用户仿真、提问效率和意图适应方面的表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，具备更强交互能力的模型虽优于基础版本，但距离理想情况仍有显著差距；真实生产环境中的代理会话验证表明，所建模的失败模式在实际应用中广泛存在且具有严重后果。

Conclusion: Drift-Bench++为交互式意图对齐提供了一个统一、可执行的基准，能够有效评估和促进智能体在非理想通信条件与动态意图下的性能提升，是迈向更鲁棒人机协作的重要一步。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents increasingly tackle complex tasks through interactive, long-horizon exchanges with users, while existing benchmarks generally assume that users always accurately and sufficiently communicate a fixed intent. However, this oracle communication assumption rarely holds in practice: users may miscommunicate, change their goals, and run out of patience. We define this task setting as Interactive Intent Alignment, where agents must recover and continuously track the user's current intent despite imperfect communication and evolving goals. To study this setting, we introduce Drift-Bench++, a principled benchmark construction pipeline for verified executable tasks with controlled misalignment and intent shifts, along with an interaction protocol featuring finite patience, diverse simulated users, and silent interaction-conditioned shifts. We further develop GRIP, a comprehensive evaluation protocol covering task grounding, user realism, inquiry effectiveness, and adaptation to evolving intent. Across diverse environments, models, and interaction conditions, stronger interaction consistently helps but remains far from oracle performance; Validation on deployed ProdAgent sessions further shows that the modeled failures are prevalent and consequential in deployment. By providing a unified, executable benchmark for interactive intent alignment, Drift-Bench++ offers a foundation for evaluating and advancing agents under realistic communication and evolving intent.

</details>


### [95] [StreamDecisionBench: Evaluating Decisions in Force on Evolving Language Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38612)
*Jhen-Ke Lin,Chung Chun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出StreamDecisionBench（SDB）评估语言模型在程序中作为决策组件时的实时表现，关注决策在流式证据下的准确性与延迟问题。通过四类应用场景和可执行代码生成参考决策，以对数时间轴上的归一化曲线下面积衡量在力准确率，揭示判断力与延迟之间的权衡。结果显示，推理虽提升判断力，但显著增加延迟成本；更快但判断力较弱的组件仍可获得相近综合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当自然语言驱动的应用中证据随时间变化时，语言模型的旧决策可能在状态已过期后仍生效，导致错误，而传统离线评估无法捕捉此类问题。因此需要一种能评估决策在每一时刻有效性、区分判断与延迟误差的新方法。

Method: 构建StreamDecisionBench（SDB），在四类应用场景中持续输入流式证据，使用可执行代码生成参考决策，评估每个时间点的在力决策，并基于对数时间轴的归一化面积（AUC）量化在力准确率，同时分离判断与延迟影响。

Result: SDB得分与场景级乘积（离线准确率 × 时序最优得分）相差不超过2.9分；引入推理虽提升判断力，但显著增加延迟，使Luna和Terra模型分别落后38.8和42.8分；更快但判断力弱的组件可达到与无推理的Terra相似的综合表现。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了语言模型在程序中作为实时决策组件时，判断力与延迟之间存在显著权衡。SDB提供了一种有效且敏感的评估框架，能够暴露时间尺度依赖性，为优化模型设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: As natural language drives more applications, language models increasingly run inside programs as decision components: the program sends them the current state and acts on the returned decision until a newer one arrives. When evidence changes during inference, a decision correct for its own state can stay in force after that state has passed, as when a call recorder keeps running after a customer starts reading out a card number; untimed (offline) accuracy counts such an error as correct. We introduce StreamDecisionBench (SDB), which evaluates the decision in force at every instant and attributes every erroneous instant to judgment, latency or both. Its scenarios stream evidence in four application families, with reference decisions computed from public rules by executable code. We summarize in-force accuracy across update intervals of 1-5 s by its normalized area under the curve on a logarithmic time axis, giving equal weight to equal multiplicative ranges. Across six settings of four hosted models, this score stays within 2.9 points of the scenario-wise product of untimed accuracy and an oracle's integrated timing score. Reasoning improves judgment, but at low effort latency costs Luna and Terra, two GPT models we also evaluate without reasoning, 38.8 and 42.8 points relative to untimed accuracy; a faster component with weaker judgment attains a similar integrated score to Terra without reasoning. The aggregate and family curves show where these tradeoffs change, making the evaluation's time-scale dependence visible.

</details>


### [96] [Marking Contour Tones in Yorùbá](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38627)
*Kólá Túbòsún*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了约鲁巴语中声调拼写难题，尤其针对个人姓名和词汇中因省略音节长度而造成的声调信息缺失问题。当前拼写方式常导致意义反转，提出采用锐音符（caron）和抑扬符（circumflex）标记来准确表示升调与降调，这些符号在语言学研究中有历史依据，并通过WriteYoruba键盘和TTSYoruba语音合成器实现，支持标准键盘输入与计算处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约鲁巴语是声调语言，现有拼写系统无法准确表达轮廓声调，尤其在人名和词汇中常因省略音节长度而丢失或颠倒声调信息，造成语义错误。

Method: 引入并推广使用锐音符（caron）和抑扬符（circumflex）作为单个元音上的声调标记，以表示升调和降调，并结合WriteYoruba键盘与TTSYoruba语音合成器进行技术实现与验证。

Result: 成功实现基于标准键盘输入的声调精确标注，并通过语音合成系统验证其可读性与实用性，为约鲁巴语数字化拼写提供可行解决方案。

Conclusion: 采纳锐音符与抑扬符作为约鲁巴语声调拼写的标准化符号，能够有效解决现有拼写系统的缺陷，提升语言表达的准确性与计算处理能力。

Abstract: Yorùbá is a tonal language in which contour tones pose persistent orthographic challenges. These are especially notable for personal names and lexical items whose conventional spellings avoid vowel lengthening that would otherwise provide a host syllable for the second tone. A particular concern is a class of names in which the conventional spelling does not just omit tonal information but inverts the meaning of said name, sometimes asserting the opposite of what the name intends. This paper describes the problem, illustrates the inadequacy of current solutions, and proposes the adoption of the caron and circumflex marks. These are symbols with precedent in Yorùbá phonological scholarship since Olmsted (1951), used as orthographic conventions on single vowels to encode rising and falling contour tones, making them accessible for the first time through standard keyboard input and computational text processing. The proposal is supported by an implementation in the WriteYoruba keyboard and the TTSYoruba speech synthesizer, whose architecture and listener evaluation are reported separately (Tubosun et al., 2026).

</details>


### [97] [Strong Multilingual Privacy Tagging at Encoder Speed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38630)
*Jonathan Graehl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种支持多种去标识化策略的多语言命名实体标注器，通过在35种语言的前沿模型标注数据上微调多语言编码器，并采用覆盖感知掩码和子词边界修复技术，显著提升去标识化效果。在7种语言的1,283个真人标注测试片段上，最佳红移F1达到88.8，远超现有方法。添加约5万条标注训练句并改进人类标注重播机制后，精确类型跨度F1从74.5提升至76.3。该模型在保持高效推理的同时，支持细粒度实体区分，且代码、提示和训练方案均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私去标识化需在去除个人信息的同时保留文本中的关系表达，现有方法在多语言支持、细粒度实体识别及训练效率方面存在不足，亟需更高效、灵活且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 在35种语言的前沿模型标注数据上微调多语言编码器，使用覆盖感知掩码处理未标注类型，避免其被误判为负样本；通过学习的±1字符调整修复子词边界；结合人类黄金标准数据重播与增量训练，提升模型对罕见或未见实体类型的识别能力。

Result: 在7种语言的1,283个真人标注测试段上，去标识化F1达88.8，优于GLiNER2（69.1）、GLiNER2适配版（67.8）、Microsoft Presidio（57.3）和OpenAI隐私过滤器（35.8）。在Ont3数据集上，精确类型跨度F1从74.5提升至76.3。边界修复带来1.7点提升，而映射黄金重播使人类标注F1提升10点。本地LLM性能较差，推理速度慢且成本高。编码器架构实现4.9倍于GLiNER2的CPU吞吐量。

Conclusion: 本研究构建的多语言命名实体标注器在去标识化任务中表现优异，具备良好的泛化性、效率与可扩展性，适用于多样化隐私保护场景，相关代码与资源已公开，便于后续研究与应用。

Abstract: Privacy redaction must remove personal information while preserving relationships expressed in text. We develop a multilingual named-entity tagger with fine-grained distinctions supporting varied redaction policies and methods for cheaply learning additional distinctions. We fine-tune a multilingual encoder with an affine span-tagging head on frontier-model annotations in 35 languages, replay mapped human gold with coverage-aware masking so unannotated types are not treated as negatives, and repair subword boundaries with a learned +/-1-character adjustment. On 1,283 human-gold test segments in seven languages, best measured redaction F1 is 88.8, against 69.1 for published GLiNER2 with 11 unrepresentable types excluded from its task (68.8 without that exemption), 67.8 for GLiNER2 adapted to the new training data, 57.3 for Microsoft Presidio and 35.8 for the best published OpenAI Privacy Filter fine-tune. Adding about 50,000 annotated training sentences and increasing human-gold replay improves exact typed-span F1 from 74.5 to 76.3 on Ont3, our 31-type frontier-annotated NER evaluation of 1,201 development segments. Mapped-gold replay alone raises human-gold F1 by ten points without loss on frontier-annotated text; boundary adjustment adds 1.7 exact typed-span F1 points on Ont3. Local LLMs fitting on a single 96-GB GPU underperformed as prompted annotators and frozen encoders, with encoding 30-95 times slower than XLM-R inference and prompted annotation roughly 180-1,100 times slower in the evaluated configurations. The encoder architecture delivers 4.9 times GLiNER2's CPU throughput. We release code, prompts and training recipes, with data-acquisition scripts and source links.

</details>


### [98] [Breaking Babel: A Self-Evolving Multi-Agent System for Long-Form Subtitle Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38660)
*Haibo Jin,Xinjie Li,Najmeh Sadoughi,Yang Liu,Yibo Wang,Zhu Liu,Yuzong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SMART is a self-evolving multi-agent system for long-form subtitle translation that uses dynamic routing, persistent memory, and a judge-refiner loop to adaptively improve translation quality across episodes and genres. It outperforms existing methods on both automated (Subtitle Arena, SubMQM) and human evaluation (MuSC benchmark), achieving state-of-the-art results in consistency, cultural adaptation, and error reduction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing subtitle translation methods are limited to sentence-level processing or use static workflows that fail to adapt to complex discourse and cultural context across long-form series. There is a need for systems that maintain consistency in terminology and style while dynamically responding to scene complexity and production context.

Method: SMART employs a dynamic router and Mixture-of-Agents layer with tools for terminology verification, constraint validation, and contextual retrieval. It builds a persistent series-level memory and uses a judge-refiner loop to iteratively update agent prompts and routing policies via textual critiques—without retraining the base LLMs—during test-time training. During inference, the evolved configuration translates the remaining content.

Result: SMART achieves the best overall MQM score across all 15 target language directions in Subtitle Arena, reducing average penalty by 6.9% compared to the strongest baseline. On MuSC, it attains the highest model and human evaluation scores (4.50/5), demonstrating superior performance in fluency, accuracy, consistency, and cultural appropriateness.

Conclusion: SMART advances long-form subtitle translation through adaptive, self-evolving multi-agent reasoning with persistent memory and dynamic policy refinement. The proposed Subtitle Arena and SubMQM framework provide comprehensive benchmarks for evaluating cross-series subtitle quality, establishing new standards for future research.

Abstract: Long-form subtitle translation requires reasoning over discourse and cultural context spanning episodes or entire series, while maintaining consistent terminology and style. Existing single-LLM methods are largely sentence-level, and multi-agent systems often use static workflows that do not adapt to scene complexity or production context. We propose SMART, a Self-evolving Multi-Agent system for long-foRm subtitle Translation. During test-time training, SMART builds persistent series-level memory and translates a subset of sentences through a dynamic router and Mixture-of-Agents layer with tools for terminology verification, subtitle constraint validation, and contextual retrieval. A judge-refiner loop scores candidates and uses textual critiques to update agent prompts and routing policies without retraining the underlying LLMs. During test-time inference, the evolved configuration translates the remaining series. We also introduce Subtitle Arena, covering 14 genres, 2--198 episodes per series, production years 1959--2023, and 15 target locales, together with SubMQM, a subtitle-adapted MQM framework with seven dimensions and 19 error categories. SMART achieves the best overall MQM score in all 15 Subtitle Arena directions, reducing average penalty by 6.9% over the strongest competing agent system. On the public MuSC benchmark, SMART obtains the best model result across all four language pairs and also achieves the best human-evaluation result, with an overall score of 4.50/5.

</details>


### [99] [MetaSteer: Context-Conditioned, nonlinear Steering via Attention-Projection Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38718)
*Mehdi Jafari,Hao Xue,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MetaSteer提出一种非线性、上下文依赖的干预方法，通过作用于注意力投影矩阵实现动态激活变化，无需线性假设，在零样本场景下可泛化至未见概念和分布外上下文。该方法基于偏好优化训练，能有效保留隐藏状态轨迹的局部动态特性（如速度与曲率），在文本生成和智能体任务中表现优于或匹配现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大模型引导方法依赖线性、上下文无关的激活空间干预，存在信息瓶颈问题，难以同时表达多种行为差异；需要更灵活、上下文感知的干预机制以提升泛化性和可控性。

Method: MetaSteer采用偏好驱动的优化框架，学习非线性低秩适配器，对注意力投影矩阵施加上下文相关的动态干预，使激活效应随输入上下文变化而变化，不依赖固定概念几何结构。

Result: MetaSteer在多个文本生成基准和智能体任务中表现出色，零样本性能优于或等同于特定任务的强基线；其干预能产生结构化的上下文依赖变化，部分保留隐藏状态轨迹的动力学特征；更强的文本生成控制能力与更强的智能体控制能力相关联。

Conclusion: MetaSteer提供了一种可迁移、上下文敏感且高效的大模型引导方法，具备良好的泛化能力与动力学保真度，为可控生成与安全干预提供了新思路，同时揭示了轨迹几何、能力保持与安全性之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Steering large language models typically relies on linear, context-independent interventions in activation space, an assumption that recent work has challenged and that can induce an information bottleneck when a fixed representation must encode many behavioral distinctions. We introduce MetaSteer, a method that learns nonlinear interventions with context-dependent effects and applies them to attention projection matrices, producing activation effects that vary with the input context by construction and requiring no linear concept-geometry assumption. Framed as preference-based optimization, MetaSteer is trained once on a pooled preference corpus and transferred zero-shot to unseen concepts and out-of-distribution contexts.
