We test this benchmark across sixteen different open and closed-source models and report 4AFC performance, a measure which is also used to grade human participants. We observe that models identify a place from the studied viewpoint but lose it once the camera moves, dropping below the 25% chance level at 135° where a human observer scores 85%. Frontier models (Gemini 3.8 Flash, GPT-5.6) answer only 39% and 31% of rotated trials correctly, recovering to 85% and 55% only when distractors are moved more than 30 meters apart. Our benchmark demonstrates that while current VLMs possess rudimentary cognitive maps, their spatial resolution remains fundamentally too coarse to maintain a stable, 3D understanding of the world once the viewpoint changes.

</details>


### [133] [Concept Subspaces Compute Beyond the Logit Lens: A Weights-Only Test for Locating Representations Upstream of Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39263)
*Aojie Yuan,Zhiyuan Julian Su,Haiyue Zhang,Zijian Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双侧几何诊断方法，用于衡量提取的特征子空间与解码矩阵主右奇异方向的重叠程度，评估其在输出读出中的作用。基于模型权重，该方法无需额外训练即可评估概念子空间的有效性。实验以FARS（格式无关推理子空间）为例，发现激活衍生的概念估计器仅在顶层读出跨度中保留0.38–0.80%的能量，而最终层PCA表现更优（3.56%），且远超FARS。同层下一个词控制组能量是FARS的13倍，所有25个模型均显著分离。跨格式检索结果显示62–100%的泛化能力，表明提取方法可迁移而非固定基。干预研究显示模型依赖性的影响较弱，远未达到全向量替换效果。整体结果区分了概念结构与主导读出方向，并限制了对因果充分性的过度主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以判断概念子空间是否真正影响模型输出读出，尤其无法区分概念结构与主导读出方向之间的关系。需要一种能同时评估子空间与输出相关性的客观诊断工具，以避免误判概念表示的因果作用。

Method: 提出双侧几何诊断：测量提取子空间与解码矩阵主右奇异方向的重叠度；使用输出导向正样本作为对照；仅需模型权重，无需额外训练；在九种秩匹配估计器和二十六个模型上进行评估；结合跨格式检索与多模型干预实验验证泛化性和影响强度。

Result: 激活衍生的概念估计器仅携带极低能量（0.38–0.80%）于顶层读出跨度；最终层PCA表现优于FARS（3.56%）；同层下一个词控制组能量为FARS的约13倍，且所有模型中均显著分离；跨格式检索成功率62–100%，表明提取过程具有可迁移性；干预实验显示源向量影响有限，模型依赖性强，未达全替换水平。

Conclusion: 所提几何诊断有效区分了概念结构与主导读出方向；当前概念子空间（如FARS）虽具结构性，但不足以独立驱动输出；其对模型行为的影响有限，不能被视作因果充分因素；研究强调需谨慎解读概念解释的因果效力。

Abstract: A concept subspace's effect on model behavior does not establish how it relates to the output readout. We introduce a two-sided geometric diagnostic that measures an extracted subspace's overlap with the dominant right-singular directions of the unembedding matrix, evaluated against output-oriented positive controls. Given an extracted basis, the raw diagnostic requires only model weights. Our testbed is the Format-Agnostic Reasoning Subspace (FARS), a ten-dimensional basis extracted from eighteen reasoning concepts expressed in six surface forms. Across nine rank-matched estimators and twenty-six models, four activation-derived concept estimators carry only 0.38--0.80% mean energy in the top-ten readout span. Final-layer PCA carries 3.56%, exceeding FARS in 25 of 26 models. A same-layer next-token control, evaluated using a fitted linear translator for depth matching, carries approximately thirteen times more energy than FARS, with separation in all 25 tested models. Re-extracting FARS on ten disjoint concepts yields 62--100% cross-format retrieval across twenty-four generative models, demonstrating transfer of the extraction procedure rather than a fixed basis. A complementary four-model, three-seed intervention study finds model-dependent source-directed effects that remain well below full-vector replacement. Together, the geometry and intervention controls distinguish concept structure from dominant readout directions while limiting claims of causal sufficiency.

</details>


### [134] [Offline Guidance, Online Reasoning: Reusing LLM Feedback for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39346)
*Bohan Zhang,Linan Yue,Weibo Gao,Pengyu Chen,Hong Guo,Yanqi Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为可复用隐式校正（Reusable Latent Correction, RLC）的新方法，旨在在仅离线访问大语言模型（LLM）且小语言模型（SLM）参数固定的前提下，提升SLM的推理能力。RLC将LLM生成的一次性自然语言指导转化为SLM隐藏空间中的持久化修正经验，并存入外部记忆库，根据SLM当前推理状态进行检索与重用，从而在不依赖在线LLM调用的情况下实现推理性能提升。该方法无需参数更新或额外训练，已在多个推理基准和不同规模的SLM上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-SLM协作方法存在两大局限：一是知识蒸馏需频繁更新参数和额外训练；二是在线协作虽有效但依赖重复调用LLM，且生成的指导无法跨任务复用。为克服这些限制，本文探索一种更受限场景——仅允许离线使用LLM、SLM参数固定、推理全程由SLM独立完成，目标是实现高效、可复用的推理增强。

Method: 提出可复用隐式校正（RLC）框架：首先通过离线方式利用黑盒LLM生成自然语言指导；然后将这些指导映射为隐空间中的校正向量，并存储于外部记忆银行中；在推理过程中，SLM根据当前推理状态动态检索相关校正经验，注入自身推理流程以修正错误路径，实现无在线调用的持续改进。

Result: 在多个主流推理基准（如GSM8K、MATH、AQuA）及不同规模的小语言模型上，RLC均显著提升推理准确率，且优于现有离线与在线协作方法。更重要的是，它在不更新模型参数、不依赖实时LLM调用的前提下实现了跨问题的经验复用，验证了其高效性与实用性。

Conclusion: RLC成功构建了一种基于离线预训练、无需参数更新、可复用的推理增强机制，有效弥合了大模型能力与小模型部署优势之间的鸿沟，为轻量级模型的高性能推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer strong reasoning capabilities but are often costly to access through commercial APIs, while small language models (SLMs) are easier to deploy locally yet remain weaker in reasoning. This capability-deployment gap has motivated LLM-SLM collaboration, which aims to improve SLM reasoning using LLM capabilities while preserving the deployment advantages of SLMs. Existing approaches mainly follow two paradigms. Knowledge distillation uses LLM-generated answers and reasoning trajectories to train SLMs offline, but requires parameter updates and additional training. Alternatively, online collaboration routes difficult problems to an LLM or leverages LLM-generated guidance and corrections when an SLM encounters difficulties. Although effective, online collaboration requires repeated LLM access. Moreover, the guidance produced for a particular problem is discarded after inference and cannot benefit subsequent problems involving similar reasoning states. In the paper, we focus on a more constrained setting in which the LLM is accessed only offline, the SLM parameters remain fixed, and online inference is performed solely by the SLM. To this end, we propose Reusable Latent Correction (RLC), which converts one-off natural-language guidance from a black-box LLM into persistent corrective experiences in the hidden space of an SLM. RLC stores these experiences in an external bank and retrieves them according to the SLM's current reasoning state, enabling the SLM to reuse LLM-derived corrections during inference without any online LLM calls. Experiments across multiple reasoning benchmarks and SLM scales show that RLC consistently improves SLM reasoning without parameter updates or online LLM calls. Code is available at https://github.com/ZBH031/reusable-latent-correction.

</details>


### [135] [Working Around the Compute Ceiling: Byte-Exact Memory in Galahad Makes LLM Reading a One-Time Cost LLM Reading a One-Time Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39358)
*Sietse Schelpe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Galad, 一个用于vLLM、SGLang和llama.cpp的内存层，通过缓存模型的键值（KV）状态或文档内容，减少重复计算，显著提升大语言模型在处理长文本时的效率与能耗表现。实验表明，使用Taliesin和Blaise后，模型可访问整个97,000-token语料库并准确回答100道问题，推理时间缩短至0.6秒以内，能耗降低至213焦耳，且恢复状态为比特级一致。系统具备失败闭合机制，兼容30个模型，推动了从无状态到有状态推理的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于Transformer的语言模型在每个令牌上执行有限计算，限制了其处理和验证复杂任务的能力。此外，服务端对每次请求都是无状态的，导致重复计算已读文本的注意力状态，造成资源浪费。本文旨在解决这一效率瓶颈，提升长文本处理能力并降低能耗。

Method: 提出Galahad内存层，包含Taliesin（缓存文本块的键值状态）和Blaise（存储完整文档，仅传递所需段落），实现一次读取、多次复用；结合失败闭合机制保障可靠性，并在多个框架和模型上进行测试验证。

Result: 在97,000令牌语料库上的召回测试中，使用Taliesin和Blaise后，模型成功回答100/100问题，平均响应时间0.59–0.64秒，能耗200–213焦耳；相比基线（仅10/100，9.3秒，2754焦耳），性能大幅提升。系统状态恢复为比特级一致，能源回收周期仅需13个查询。

Conclusion: Galahad将大语言模型服务从无状态推理转变为有状态推理，有效解决了重复计算问题，显著提升长文本处理效率与能效，具有广泛兼容性和可靠性，是大模型实际部署的关键进展。

Abstract: A transformer language model performs a bounded amount of computation per token, and recent work by Vishal Sikka, former CEO of Infosys, argues that this bound limits which tasks a model can carry out or verify (arXiv:2507.07505). We ask how much of the budget beneath that ceiling is spent on work the model has already done. Serving is stateless across requests: a model that answers a second question about a document recomputes the document's attention state from the first token. On seven real-world datasets, 98.7% of prompt tokens were text the model had already read. We present Galahad, a memory layer for vLLM, SGLang and llama.cpp that makes this reading a one-time cost. Taliesin saves the model's key-value (KV) state for a block of text and loads it on the next request that contains the same bytes, instead of recomputing it. Blaise keeps the documents themselves and passes the model only the section a question needs. On a recall test with 100 facts hidden in a 97,000-token corpus (Gemma 4 31B), Taliesin alone let the model attend to the whole corpus and answered 98 of 100 on llama.cpp at 3.0 s and 572 J per question, against 10 of 100, 9.3 s and 2,754 J for the same model without Galahad, which could hold only the last 12,000 tokens. With Blaise added, the model read about 668 tokens per question and answered 100 of 100 on all three runtimes at 0.59-0.64 s and 200-213 J; a tuned RAGFlow pipeline answered 77. Storing the corpus is a one-time cost of about 100 s and 28 kJ, whose energy is recovered after 13 questions. Restored state is bit-identical: all 262,144 output logits matched after restart, rehydration and hot-load. Galahad worked with all 30 models we tested under vLLM, and it fails closed: any load that does not pass its checks is recomputed. Together these results move LLM serving from stateless to stateful inference.

</details>


### [136] [Ready2Blend: From Natural-Language Instructions to Composable Alignment Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39365)
*Jeesu Jung,Hwan Chang,Juseon Do,Jeonghwan Choi,Jinho Choo,Sungwoo Nam,S. K. Hong,Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Ready2Blend combines natural language flexibility with learned alignment via a modular prompt bank and composability regularization, enabling effective continual alignment with frozen backbones. It achieves near-post-training performance using minimal prompts and training time, supports dynamic blending and personalization, and requires no retraining.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing methods for continual alignment either lack flexibility (post-training) or offer indirect control (natural language instructions). There is a need for a method that combines the strengths of both while preserving previously learned knowledge.

Method: Ready2Blend uses AlignFormer to map requirements to fixed-length alignment prompts stored in a modular prompt bank. The backbone and prior prompts remain frozen. Composability regularization transfers semantic geometry from text to prompt space, enabling inference-time blending and reweighting.

Result: Ready2Blend matches post-training-based methods in performance (93.1–98.5% of joint-training reference), with competitive retention, only a few prompt tokens, and up to 4.3× less training time. It supports weighted personalization and order-free composition without retraining.

Conclusion: Ready2Blend provides a highly efficient, flexible, and scalable solution for continual alignment by leveraging modular prompts and composability regularization, outperforming existing frozen-backbone approaches while maintaining strong retention and adaptability.

Abstract: Continual alignment requires LLMs to adapt to new requirements without forgetting previously acquired behaviors. Natural-language instructions are flexible and composable but offer only indirect control, whereas post-training provides stronger adaptation at the cost of repeated parameter updates. We introduce Ready2Blend, which combines the flexibility of natural language with learned alignment. AlignFormer maps each requirement to a fixed-length alignment prompt stored in a modular prompt bank, while the backbone and prior prompts remain frozen. Composability regularization transfers the semantic geometry of textual requirements into prompt space, enabling inference-time blending and reweighting. Across two practical continual alignment settings, Ready2Blend is the only frozen-backbone method that matches post-training-based alignment methods, reaching $93.1$-$98.5\%$ of a joint-training reference with competitive retention, while requiring only a few prompt tokens and up to $4.3\times$ less training time. Its modular design further enables weighted personalization and order-free composition without retraining. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [137] [Making Grid Beam Search Less Greedy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39368)
*Sean Papay,Roman Klinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了自回归文本生成模型中的词汇约束解码方法，指出网格束搜索（grid beam search）存在对较易满足的约束优先处理的偏差，而确定性有限自动机约束束搜索（DFA-constrained beam search）无此偏差。为此提出公平网格束搜索（fair grid beam search），在保持线性前向传播次数的同时消除偏差，并实验证明其能生成更高概率的序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网格束搜索虽然速度快，但会优先满足容易的约束，导致难约束被忽略或延迟处理，影响生成质量；需要一种既能高效又能公平处理所有约束的方法。

Method: 提出公平网格束搜索，通过调整搜索过程中的约束处理顺序，确保所有约束被平等对待，同时维持线性复杂度的前向计算量。

Result: 实验表明，传统网格束搜索确实存在约束优先级偏差，而公平网格束搜索有效纠正该问题，并在两个约束生成任务中生成了概率更高的文本序列。

Conclusion: 公平网格束搜索在保持高效性的同时克服了原有方法的不公平性，显著提升了约束生成的质量与可靠性。

Abstract: A common formalism for constraining the output of autoregressive text generation models involves lexical constraints, words or phrases which are required to occur in the generated text. DFA-constrained beam search and grid beam search are two widely used paradigms for decoding from autoregressive models while enforcing lexical constraints. As the former approach requires a number of forward passes exponential in the number of constraint tokens, it is often dispreferred to the latter, which requires only linearly many forward calls. However, while grid beam search achieves an exponential speedup, it does so in a manner which does not treat all of the constraints equally. In this paper, we demonstrate that grid beam search is biased to incorporate easier-to-satisfy constraints first, leaving harder constraints to the end of the sequence. This contrasts with DFA-constrained beam search, which exhibits no such bias. To address this shortcoming, we propose fair grid beam search, a modification to grid beam search which avoids this bias while still requiring only linearly many forward passes. Experimentally, we confirm grid beam search's bias on two constrained generation tasks, finding significant differences in how it orders constraint tokens as compared to DFA-constrained beam search and fair grid beam search. Furthermore, we find that fair grid beam search not only fixes grid beam search's bias, but finds higher-probability strings in the process.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Travel Time Prediction in Supply Chain Management Using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38190)
*Balaji Venkateswaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究旨在利用机器学习和深度学习技术，基于大量历史数据构建精准的运输时间预测模型，以提升供应链中物流的一致性与效率，优化计划、需求预测、交货期管理及装配规划，并提高客户满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应链系统复杂，依赖原材料和成品的运输物流，准确的旅行时间估计对提升物流性能、改善计划与客户满意度至关重要。

Method: 利用大规模历史数据，结合机器学习与深度学习方法，构建旅行时间预测模型。

Result: 成功建立了一个高精度的旅行时间预测模型，能够有效支持供应链中的各项决策活动。

Conclusion: 该模型显著提升了供应链物流的可预测性与效率，为供应链管理提供了强有力的技术支持。

Abstract: The purpose of this research is to find data and methods using machine learning and deep learning to correctly predict the estimated travel time for transportation and logistics in a supply chain system. The supply chain ecosystem is very complex and heavily relies on the transportation and logistics of raw materials and finished goods. Accurate travel time estimation is critical because it helps supply chain members to improve logistics consistency and performance. This helps in planning, demand forecasting, lead time management and assembly planning. The logistics on the delivery side of the customer also plays a crucial role in customer satisfaction and voice of customer. With the collection of huge historical data and using novel techniques, the research builds an accurate model to predict travel time of inventory.

</details>


### [139] [EHR2Trace: Auditable EHR Data Infrastructure for Patient World Models and Clinical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38193)
*Xinye Yang,Yuli Wang,Cheng Ting Lin,Harrison Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EHR2Trace converts heterogeneous EHRs into traceable, standardized patient events with clear temporal and source tracking, enabling reliable training and evaluation of patient world models and clinical agents.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Electronic health records (EHRs) are essential for modeling patient trajectories but suffer from inconsistent recording practices, making them hard to use reliably across different systems. This inconsistency hampers the development of accurate patient world models and clinical decision-support agents.

Method: EHR2Trace standardizes EHR data by linking events to source records, separating event time from information availability, and distinguishing between medication order, dispensing, and administration. It uses a unified event representation compatible with OMOP and MEDS formats, supports automated validation, and enables reproducible builds.

Result: EHR2Trace processed 846.4 million events across three datasets; all validation checks passed except one unit-consistency issue in MIMIC-IV. All 28 injected faults were detected. A controlled experiment revealed that using later diagnoses at admission time artificially inflated model performance, and models trained on such data lost accuracy when evaluated with availability-aware histories.

Conclusion: EHR2Trace provides a robust, transparent, and reusable foundation for building and evaluating patient world models and clinical agents, improving data integrity, reproducibility, and model reliability in real-world clinical settings.

Abstract: Patient world models and clinical agents aim to predict changes in patients' health and support clinical work. Developing these systems requires reliable histories of patient conditions, treatments, and the information available at each decision. Electronic health records (EHRs) contain these histories, but differences in how events are recorded make them difficult to use consistently. We present EHR2Trace, a system that converts EHRs from different sources into traceable patient events for model training and evaluation. It links events to source records, separates event time from information availability, and distinguishes medication orders, dispensing, and administration. A shared event representation supports both OMOP and MEDS exports, with automated validation and reproducible builds. Across three clinical datasets, EHR2Trace converted 846.4 million events, with every applicable check passing except one unit-consistency check on MIMIC-IV, and detected all 28 injected faults. A controlled prediction experiment showed that assigning later diagnoses to admission time substantially inflated measured performance, and that a model trained on such data lost accuracy when deployed on histories filtered by availability. EHR2Trace provides a reusable data foundation for patient world models and clinical agents, helping researchers inspect patient histories, check conversion decisions, and evaluate models with explicit data rules.

</details>


### [140] [A Moving-Horizon Approximate Branch-and-Reduce Method for Deep Classification Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38194)
*Chenxuanyin Zou,Jiayang Ren,Qiangqiang Mao,Jing Liu,Marcus Lai,Yankai Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于移动窗口的近似分支-缩减方法，用于在大规模连续特征数据集上训练接近最优的深层分类决策树。该方法采用分层根-子树优化框架，在根节点使用分支-缩减求解，对子树问题则通过贪心启发式近似。虽然整体框架理论上可保证全局最优性，但近似策略（类似强化学习中的前瞻回溯）显著提升了深层结构的计算效率。进一步采用低成本的移动窗口策略迭代优化模型精度。大量实验表明，该方法在测试准确率上优于现有启发式基线，且在数据集规模和树深方面展现出远超全局最优求解器的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树虽具高可解释性，但在大规模数据和深层结构下面临严重可扩展性挑战。现有全局最优方法受限于二元特征选择和浅层深度，而传统启发式方法常以牺牲预测精度为代价。因此亟需一种既能保持高精度又能高效处理大规模、深层决策树的新方法。

Method: 提出一种移动窗口近似分支-缩减方法，基于分层根-子树优化框架：在根节点使用分支-缩减精确求解，对子树问题采用贪心启发式近似；通过低开销的移动窗口策略迭代优化模型精度，实现对深层结构的高效求解。

Result: 所提方法在测试准确率上超越现有启发式基线，在数据集规模和树深方面均显著优于全局最优求解器，展现出卓越的可扩展性与实用性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功设计了一种兼具高精度与强可扩展性的深层决策树训练方法，为大规模复杂数据场景下的可解释机器学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Despite the importance for interpretability, decision trees face severe scalability challenges. Existing global optimal methods are often limited by binary feature selection and shallow tree depths, whereas traditional heuristic approaches frequently sacrifice predictive accuracy. To overcome these limitations, this paper proposes a moving-horizon approximate branch-and-reduce method to train near-optimal deep classification trees on large-scale datasets with continuous features. Built on a hierarchical root-subtree optimization framework, the method solves the root-level problem via branch-and-reduce while approximating the induced subtree problem using greedy heuristics. Although the underlying framework is capable of guaranteeing global optimality, the approximation, which functions as a lookahead rollout in a reinforcement learning context, significantly boosts efficiency for deeper structures. A low-cost moving-horizon strategy is then employed to iteratively refine model accuracy. Extensive numerical results demonstrate that our method exceeds the testing accuracy of existing heuristic baselines while offering significantly greater scalability, in terms of both dataset size and tree depth, than global optimal solvers.

</details>


### [141] [A Data-Free Physics-Informed Neural Operator for Level-Set Interface Advection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38195)
*Muhammad Akbar Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需数据的物理信息神经算子，用于水平集界面平流问题，仅依赖传输残差和几何约束进行训练，不使用参考解。采用时空傅里叶骨干网络一次性输出整个时空轨迹，初始条件通过构造方式施加，避免了锚定项与残差之间的竞争。在反向单涡旋和刚体旋转测试中，该方法在无监督情况下表现优于监督基线，尤其在几何约束有效区域表现出更好的面积守恒能力。混合模型（使用8个参考解）性能超过监督模型（使用16个参考解）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经算子依赖大量参考解进行训练，但获取高质量参考解成本高且受限。本文旨在开发一种不依赖任何参考解的数据自由物理信息神经算子，以降低对标注数据的依赖，同时保持高精度和物理一致性。

Method: 采用时空傅里叶骨干网络构建神经算子，通过传输残差和梯度模长为1的eikonal几何约束进行训练；初始条件通过网络结构设计直接满足，而非通过惩罚项引入；在无监督和混合训练框架下对比监督基线。

Result: 在反向单涡旋场景中，数据自由算子相对L2误差为1.614%±0.067%，是监督基线（0.369%±0.035%）的4.4倍；在刚体旋转中分别为3.804%±1.075%和2.576%±0.159%，差距缩小至1.5倍。尽管场误差较大，但在满足eikonal约束的区域，其面积守恒性能比监督基线好2.7倍；使用8个参考解的混合模型优于使用16个参考解的监督模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的无数据物理信息神经算子在缺乏参考解的情况下仍能实现良好性能，尤其在物理一致性方面显著优于传统监督方法。其成功关键在于精确的几何约束建模与初始条件的构造性施加，表明物理先验可有效替代数据驱动学习。

Abstract: Operators for interfacial problems are trained on reference solutions produced by the solver they are intended to replace. This work develops a data-free physics-informed neural operator for level-set interface advection, in which the interface is the equation's unknown and the operator maps an initial interface to the full spatiotemporal trajectory under a prescribed flow. Training uses only the transport residual and a geometric constraint; no reference solution enters the objective at any point. A spacetime Fourier backbone emits the entire trajectory in one pass, and the initial condition is imposed by construction rather than by penalty, which removes the competition between the anchoring term and the residual that otherwise arises when no solution data are available. Supervised and hybrid operators are trained under an identical architecture, family, budget and test set, and are reported throughout as baselines that quantify what refusing labels costs. On a reversed single vortex the data-free operator reaches 1.614 +/- 0.067% relative L2 error on 100 held-out initial interfaces against 0.369 +/- 0.035% for the supervised baseline, a factor of 4.4; on solid-body rotation the corresponding figures are 3.804 +/- 1.075% and 2.576 +/- 0.159%, a factor of 1.5. The two benchmarks rank the arms differently, and the difference is attributable to the eikonal constraint: the exact solution violates |grad phi| = 1 over 0.3% of the domain under rotation and 86.9% under the vortex. Where the constraint is valid the physics-trained operator conserves enclosed area 2.7 times better than the supervised baseline despite a larger field error, and a hybrid arm using eight reference solutions outperforms a supervised arm using sixteen.

</details>


### [142] [Conformal Adversarial Generative Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38196)
*Ahmad Shahi,Mamehgol Yousefi,Brendon J. Woodford,Farhaan Mirza,Tapabrata Chakraborti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAGE是一种结合生成建模、对抗判别和置信预测的新型时间序列预测框架，通过动态调整模型权重减少极端预测的影响，显著提升预测的准确性和可靠性，尤其在处理异常值和噪声数据时表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集成方法容易受到极端预测的过度影响，导致预测结果不可靠，因此需要一种能够增强预测可信度并减少异常值干扰的新方法。

Method: CAGE利用多个生成模型生成初始预测，通过判别组件结合置信预测技术评估非一致性得分，并基于p值动态调整模型权重，确保仅最可信的预测参与最终集成输出。

Result: 在新西兰乳制品收集数据和全球健康数据（owid-monkeypox）上的实验表明，CAGE在处理异常值和噪声数据方面优于传统集成方法，提供了更准确且统计严谨的预测结果。

Conclusion: CAGE是一种通用性强的时间序列预测方法，适用于金融、气象、供应链管理等多个领域，能有效提升预测的可靠性与准确性。

Abstract: Accurate time series forecasting is critical across various domains, yet traditional ensemble methods often suffer from the disproportionate influence of extreme forecasts. We introduce the Conformal Adversarial Generative Ensemble (CAGE), a novel framework that combines generative modeling, adversarial discrimination, and conformal prediction to enhance forecast reliability and accuracy. CAGE employs multiple generative models to produce initial forecasts, which are then evaluated by a discriminative component using conformal prediction techniques. P-values derived from nonconformity scores help dynamically adjust model weights, minimizing the impact of unreliable forecasts. This approach ensures that only the most credible predictions contribute to the final ensemble output. Our empirical and statistical analyses of time series data from New Zealand's milk collection and the global health data from the public owid-monkeypox dataset show that the CAGE outperforms traditional ensemble methods, especially in handling outliers and noisy data. By incorporating conformal prediction, CAGE delivers accurate and statistically rigorous forecasts, enhancing decision-making. We have demonstrated performance on two different datasets deliberately to showcase that the proposed method offers a versatile solution potentially applicable across finance, weather, and supply chain management.

</details>


### [143] [DualCast: A Dual-Path Language Model for Bimodal Financial Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38197)
*Wentao Zhao,Hongqiang Wu,Shanghang Liu,Zhaochen Zan,Yu Zhang,Biqing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualCast 是一种双路径金融时间序列预测框架，通过冻结的语言模型结合离散金融词汇表，利用总结令牌和残差形状令牌表示价格波动，实现跨资产共享模式。采用自适应频率均衡的残差向量量化以优化代码本使用，并设计快速路径（仅训练新嵌入）与可选的慢速路径（基于新闻和快速预测进行修正），后者通过监督微调和返回空间分组相对策略优化进一步提升性能。在多市场、多时间尺度的零样本评估中，慢速路径在8/12设置中表现最优，尤其在长周期预测中优势明显，新闻信息带来额外增益但效果因市场而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融时间序列预测难以同时捕捉跨资产的价格动态并有效融合实时新闻信息。现有方法在建模复杂波动性和利用非结构化文本方面存在局限，亟需一种既能高效处理数值数据又可灵活接入新闻输入的统一框架。

Method: DualCast 采用双路径架构：快速路径基于冻结的 Qwen3-8B 模型，仅训练金融令牌嵌入与输出头；慢速路径引入可切换的 LoRA 适配器，结合快速预测结果与预测时刻的新闻信息，生成修正预测。其核心创新包括：学习到的总结令牌与三类残差形状令牌用于保留局部漂移与波动特征；自适应频率均衡的残差向量量化提升代码本利用率；使用返回空间分组相对策略优化目标，激励对快速预测的改进。

Result: 在五档时间分辨率（5分钟、日、周）下对股票与能源价格的零样本测试中，慢速路径在12个设定中的8个达到最低平均绝对百分比误差，且在所有最长预测周期设置中表现最佳。新闻消融实验显示，在多数市场中加入新闻信息能带来额外性能提升，尽管增益程度因市场而异。

Conclusion: DualCast 成功将高效的数值预测与可选的新闻条件修订机制相结合，实现了快速、准确且可扩展的跨资产金融时间序列预测，尤其适用于需要长期预测与外部信息融合的场景。

Abstract: Financial time-series forecasting must capture price dynamics across heterogeneous assets while incorporating news available at prediction time. We introduce DualCast, a dual-path framework that extends a frozen language model with a discrete financial vocabulary. Each log-return patch is represented by a learned summary token and three residual shape tokens, preserving local drift and volatility while allowing shape patterns to be shared across assets. To improve codebook utilization, we develop adaptive frequency-equalizing residual vector quantization, which rebalances overloaded codewords without compromising reconstruction accuracy. The fast path trains only the new financial-token embeddings and output heads on a frozen Qwen3-8B backbone. A toggleable LoRA adapter enables a slow path that conditions on the fast forecast and news available at the forecast origin to produce a revised prediction. The reviser is initialized by supervised fine-tuning and further optimized with a return-space group relative policy optimization objective that rewards improvements over the fast forecast. In zero-shot evaluations covering equities and energy prices at five-minute, daily, and weekly resolutions, the slow path achieves the lowest mean absolute percentage error among the compared methods in 8 of 12 dataset-horizon settings, including every longest-horizon setting. News ablations indicate additional gains in most tested settings, although their magnitude varies across markets. DualCast thus combines a fast numerical forecaster with an optional text-conditioned revision mechanism.

</details>


### [144] [FlashDiffusion: Fused Tiled Kernel Spectral Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38198)
*Julio Candanedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashDiffusion 是一种无矩阵方法，通过融合 GPU 块评估密集高斯核块，并结合经验 β-流选择有限样本分辨率尺度，利用样本量和带宽的连续性从较粗糙分辨率热启动越来越昂贵的谱求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统扩散映射和核方法在小带宽情况下因高秩矩阵导致的 $O(N^2)$ 内存需求问题，提升大规模数据下的几何学习效率与可扩展性。

Method: 采用矩阵自由框架，利用融合的 GPU 块计算密集高斯核，并结合经验 $\beta$-流动态选择分辨率尺度；通过样本量和带宽的连续性进行热启动，逐步优化谱求解过程。

Result: 显著降低内存占用，实现高效的大规模扩散映射计算，同时保持良好的精度与可扩展性。

Conclusion: FlashDiffusion 有效解决了传统核方法在大规模数据下的存储与计算瓶颈，为几何学习提供了可扩展且高效的谱表示工具。

Abstract: Diffusion maps, and kernel methods more generally, provide an interpretable nonlinear spectral representation basis for geometric learning. In the geometric limit, small bandwidth, these matrices tend to be high rank and thus require materializing dense Gaussian kernels requires $O(N^2)$ memory. We introduce FlashDiffusion, a matrix-free method that evaluates dense Gaussian kernel blocks in fused GPU tiles and couples the eigensolver to an empirical $β$-flow that selects the finite-sample resolution scale. A continuation over sample size and bandwidth warm-starts increasingly expensive spectral solves from coarser resolutions.

</details>


### [145] [Kinematic signatures of impairment: Detecting alcohol intoxication in e-scooter riders using sensor data and machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38276)
*Rahul Rajendra Pai,Marco Dozza,Alexander Rasch,Ali Mohammadi,Marco Capuccini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过在受控实验中让25名参与者在清醒及血醇浓度分别为0.05%和0.08%的状态下骑行改装的电动滑板车，利用六轴惯性测量单元（IMU）和油门/刹车传感器采集数据，分析了酒精对骑行者运动控制的影响。研究发现，随着醉酒程度增加，信号的归一化排列熵下降（p < 0.001），标准差上升（p < 0.01），表明骑行者从持续、小幅度的微调转向较少、大幅度的反应式修正。基于熵的多类逻辑回归分类器在留一人外交叉验证中达到85%的整体准确率，加权一 vs 余集的AUC为0.94，清醒与高浓度状态间的AUC达1.00。其中转向速率和横向加速度是最重要的预测特征，说明酒精导致骑行时横向平衡显著崩溃。结果表明，车载运动传感结合熵分析可可靠区分清醒与醉酒骑行，为自动酒精检测系统提供基础，同时保障清醒骑行者的出行便利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前针对电动滑板车骑手酒驾的防控措施（如时间限制或认知筛查）无法实时连续评估其身体运动控制能力或受损状态，亟需一种能实时监测并识别酒后驾驶的自动化技术。

Method: 在受控实验中，25名参与者在清醒及0.05%和0.08%血醇浓度下骑行装有六轴IMU和油门/刹车传感器的电动滑板车，采样频率为100 Hz。提取两个互补信号特征：归一化排列熵（衡量时间复杂性）和标准差（衡量信号幅值）。使用重复测量相关性分析识别关键运动学特征，并构建基于熵的多类逻辑回归分类器，采用留一人外交叉验证评估性能。

Result: 7个运动学特征（全部来自IMU和油门信号）的熵随醉酒程度升高而显著下降（p < 0.001），标准差显著上升（p < 0.01）。基于熵的分类器在留一人外交叉验证中实现85%整体准确率，加权一 vs 余集AUC为0.94，清醒与高浓度状态间AUC达1.00。转向速率和横向加速度为最重要预测特征，揭示酒精导致横向平衡崩溃。

Conclusion: 车载运动传感结合熵分析可有效、可靠地区分清醒与醉酒电动滑板车骑行行为，为开发实时自动酒精检测系统奠定技术基础，既提升道路安全，又保障清醒骑手的出行自由。

Abstract: Alcohol intoxication is a leading contributor to fatal and severe-injured e-scooterist crashes. Current countermeasures, such as temporal restrictions or pre-ride cognitive screening, cannot continuously assess an e-scooterist's physical motor control or impairment in real time. We conducted a controlled experiment in which 25 participants rode an instrumented e-scooter through a test track while sober and at two targeted blood alcohol concentration levels (0.05% and 0.08%). The e-scooter was instrumented with a six-axis inertial measurement unit (IMU), and throttle and brake lever position sensors, all sampled at 100 Hz. Two complementary signal features were computed: normalised permutation entropy, which quantifies temporal complexity, and standard deviation, which quantifies signal amplitude. Repeated measures correlation identified seven kinematic features (all IMU and throttle signals) whose entropy decreased (p < 0.001) while standard deviation increased (p < 0.01) with increasing intoxication, indicating that intoxicated riders shift from continuous, low-amplitude micro-corrections to fewer, high-amplitude reactive corrections. An entropy based multi-class logistic regression classifier, evaluated through leave-one-participant-out cross-validation, achieved 85% overall accuracy and a weighted one-vs-rest area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AuROC) of 0.94, with a sober-vs-high AuROC of 1.00. Steering rate and lateral acceleration were the most important predictive features, indicating that alcohol induces a distinct collapse in lateral equilibrium during riding. Ultimately, these results demonstrate that onboard kinematic sensing combined with entropy-based signal analysis can reliably distinguish sober from intoxicated e-scooter riding, providing a foundation for automatic intoxication detection systems that preserve mobility for sober riders.

</details>


### [146] [Hermes: Learning Contextual Reasoning Unlocks Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38332)
*Xinyu Li,Mononito Goswami,Hao Liu,Nikos Kanakaris,Langlin Huang,Prithwish Jana,Patrick Blöbaum,Purak Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为Hermes的可配置框架，通过在推理时动态分配上下文并学习如何在不同上下文窗口间传递信息，提升模型性能。引入Hermes-Learn训练框架，使模型学会自适应地进行上下文推理，显著提升了小规模开源模型的表现，并在多个基准测试中表现出良好的泛化能力与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在推理时使用额外计算资源时，依赖预设策略来决定如何分配新上下文和保留信息，缺乏灵活性；本文旨在让模型自主学习这些决策机制，以更高效利用推理时计算资源。

Method: 提出Hermes框架，支持逐步增强模型对上下文分配与复用的控制权；设计Hermes-Learn两阶段训练框架，使模型学习适应性上下文推理策略。

Result: 具备能力的模型能有效利用额外推理计算，实现性能提升；小模型经训练后显著缩小与大模型差距，且性能提升在不同任务、模型和计算量下均具有泛化性和可迁移性。

Conclusion: 通过让模型自主学习上下文推理策略，可以更高效地利用测试时扩展计算，显著提升模型表现，尤其适用于资源受限的小型开源模型。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves model performance by allocating additional compute during inference. Using this compute effectively across multiple context windows requires deciding how to allocate fresh contexts and what information to carry between them. We call a model's ability to make these decisions contextual reasoning. Existing approaches largely prescribe these decisions through their harness; we instead shift them to the model. We introduce 1) Hermes, a family of simple, configurable harnesses that progressively varies model control over context allocation and reuse, and 2) Hermes-Learn, a two-stage framework for learning these capabilities. We find that capable models can exploit this flexibility to scale with additional inference-time compute, while smaller open-source models initially struggle to do so. Training with Hermes-Learn closes this gap, inducing adaptive contextual reasoning strategies that vary with both the problem and the progress of reasoning. These gains generalize across benchmarks and models, extrapolate beyond the inference-time compute seen during training, and transfer to complementary test-time scaling methods beyond Hermes.

</details>


### [147] [Activation-Conditioned Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38342)
*Zhexi Lu,Subhajit Chaudhury,Tejaswini Pedapati,Keerthiram Murugesan,Lei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ACSD通过对比正确与错误生成轨迹的激活值，提取导向向量以实现自蒸馏，无需参考文本或教师参数更新，在多个数学基准上超越现有方法，提升推理稳定性与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏方法依赖参考解或特权信息，但难以提供全程有效的逐标记监督；如何在不依赖外部参考的情况下实现高效、稳定的自监督训练是关键挑战。

Method: 提出激活条件自蒸馏（ACSD），利用验证正确的生成轨迹与其余轨迹的激活差异构建方向向量，冻结教师模型将该向量应用于每个预测位置，学生模型基于自身前缀学习下一词分布。

Result: 在五个模型上均取得最高平均准确率；在DeepSeek-R1-0528-Qwen3-8B上，数学准确率达71.9%，LiveCodeBench v6 pass@12达70.9%，优于基线69.0%和66.3%；且方向可跨数据集复用，后期logit更新更稳定。

Conclusion: ACSD有效实现了无需参考文本与教师更新的高质量自蒸馏，显著提升长序列推理性能与稳定性，具备良好泛化能力。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation uses a model as its own teacher to provide dense supervision for reasoning, often through reference-solution conditioning. Providing privileged information does not by itself ensure effective token-level supervision throughout long responses. We introduce Activation-Conditioned Self-Distillation (ACSD), which extracts a steering vector by contrasting activations of self-generated trajectories that reach verified correct answers within a generation budget with those of all remaining trajectories. A frozen copy of the base model applies this vector at each prediction position, and the student learns from its next-token distributions on student-generated prefixes. Outcome verification is used for direction construction and calibration; distillation requires neither problem-specific reference text nor teacher parameter updates. The distilled student is used alone at inference. On each of five models, ACSD achieves the highest mean accuracy over four mathematical benchmarks among the evaluated methods. On DeepSeek-R1-0528-Qwen3-8B, mean mathematical accuracy reaches 71.9\% and LiveCodeBench v6 pass@12 reaches 70.9\%, compared with 69.0\% and 66.3\% for the reference-conditioned OPSD baseline. Contrasts among correct trajectories also support distillation, and extracted directions can be reused across mathematical training datasets. On fixed student trajectories, ACSD maintains more stable late-position logit-update magnitudes than OPSD.

</details>


### [148] [Function-Space Transformer with Adaptive Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38348)
*Guorui Sang,Pedram Rooshenas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Function-Space Transformer (FST)，通过空间自适应的连续潜在表示学习函数，其锚点位置由输入观测预测并递归优化，实现对输入的自适应空间组织，支持高分辨率与有限维输出。在PDE求解（Burgers、Darcy）和ImageNet-1K图像分类任务中，FST显著优于Perceiver IO，性能媲美Fourier Neural Operator，且参数更少，验证了函数空间更新与递归精炼的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理连续域函数时受限于离散采样网格的固定结构，难以灵活适应局部变化；传统神经算子虽能映射函数，但缺乏显式空间组织。因此需要一种能自适应空间布局、兼具高效计算与高精度表示的框架。

Method: FST采用空间自适应的连续潜在表示，将特征存储于可动态预测的锚点上，并通过函数空间内的交互递归更新锚点特征，实现对输入数据的自适应空间组织。

Result: 在PDE求解任务中，FST显著超越Perceiver IO，接近Fourier Neural Operator性能；在ImageNet-1K分类任务中，准确率高于Vision Transformer，且参数量更少。消融实验证明函数空间更新与递归精炼带来关键提升。

Conclusion: FST展示了自适应连续表示在科学预测与视觉识别中的强大潜力，为函数型数据建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Many forms of data, including physical fields, geometric shapes, and visual signals, are naturally described by functions over continuous domains but are observed through discrete samples. Representing these functions on fixed uniform grids imposes a trade-off between resolving localized variation and increasing computation across the domain. Neural operators address this mismatch by learning mappings between functions, while latent-attention architectures provide flexible processing of sampled observations. We introduce the Function-Space Transformer (FST), a framework for learning from functions through a spatially adaptive continuous latent representation. FST stores features at anchors whose locations are predicted from the input observations and recursively refines these anchor features through function-space interactions. This allows the representation to adapt its spatial organization to each input rather than inherit that of the observation grid, while supporting both spatially resolved and finite-dimensional outputs. On PDE solution prediction using PDEBench Burgers and Darcy flow, FST substantially outperforms the Perceiver IO baseline, whose latent representation lacks explicit spatial organization, and is highly competitive with the Fourier Neural Operator. On ImageNet-1K, FST achieves higher classification accuracy than the Vision Transformer baseline, with fewer parameters across these comparisons. Ablations further support the benefits of function-space updates and recursive refinement. Together, these results highlight the potential of adaptive continuous representations for both scientific prediction and visual recognition.

</details>


### [149] [MILO: Automated Harness Discovery via Orchestrated Multi-Agent Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38349)
*Prithwish Jana,Mononito Goswami,Hao Liu,Xinyu Li,Langlin Huang,Zhehui Huang,Zhishen Huang,Patrick Blöbaum,Anoop Deoras,Purak Jain,Nikos Kanakaris,Sahika Genc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MILO是一种元进化岛屿编排框架，通过协同演化智能体的控制架构和发现策略，解决传统方法在长时程任务中因架构设计复杂、搜索空间庞大而需大量人工干预的问题。它利用岛屿树结构的记忆机制、基于全局历史与父代反馈的变异代理，以及动态调整搜索策略的协调器，实现了更高效、更具适应性的自动化架构探索。在多个基准测试中，MILO显著优于现有先进方法，在性能提升、资源消耗减少和问题求解边界突破方面表现卓越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化的智能体架构搜索方法受限于狭窄的优化范围或固定且易被利用的搜索策略，难以有效应对组合爆炸的搜索空间，尤其在模型迭代时需重复大量人力工作。因此亟需一种能够自适应演化搜索策略并协同优化架构本身的新框架。

Method: MILO采用岛式进化架构，构建层次化谱系记忆系统，将被拒绝的变异作为负样本；每个岛上配备可重写完整架构的变异代理，结合全局搜索历史和父代反馈进行改进；并通过协调器实现谱系嫁接、物种分化、变异代理再分配及课程修订等机制，动态调整搜索过程。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.1、PaperBench和DeepSWE等多个基准上，MILO发现的架构超越8种先进架构和6种搜索方法，使用Opus 4.8模型时性能提升分别为+12.0%、+28.3%、+10.3%，远超此前最佳结果（+4.5%、+18.3%、0%）。在Terminal-Bench 2.1上达到86.1±2.0%，超过官方排行榜最高分（83.8±2.3%），同时减少26%的token消耗。在EinsteinArena难题中，刷新了关于Erdős最小重叠及自相关不等式的最优上界。

Conclusion: MILO成功实现了对智能体架构及其发现策略的联合演化，显著提升了长时程任务中的性能表现，降低了人工干预需求，并在多个复杂挑战中取得突破性成果，为下一代自主智能系统的设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern agentic systems combine an AI model with a harness that controls execution and environmental interactions. Harness design strongly affects long-horizon performance, yet its combinatorial search space demands substantial human effort that must be repeated as models change. Existing automated methods explore this space narrowly, optimizing only components such as prompts or skills or becoming trapped by fixed, exploitative search strategies. We introduce MILO (Meta-evolutionary Island Orchestration), a framework that co-evolves agent harnesses and the strategy used to discover them. MILO combines: (i) hierarchical lineage memory over island-based trees, using rejected mutations as negative evidence; (ii) per-island mutator agents that rewrite complete harnesses using global search history and parent-specific feedback; and (iii) an orchestrator that adapts search through lineage grafting and speciation, mutator reassignment and curriculum revision. Across Terminal-Bench 2.1, PaperBench, and DeepSWE, MILO-discovered harnesses outperform eight state-of-the-art harnesses and six search methods using frontier (Opus 4.8) and open-weight (gpt-oss-120b) models. With Opus 4.8, MILO improves resolution over its initial harness by $+12.0\%$, $+28.3\%$, and $+10.3\%$, respectively, compared with best prior-search gains of $+4.5\%$, $+18.3\%$, and $0\%$. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, it achieves $86.1 \pm 2.0\%$, exceeding the official leaderboard's top entry ($83.8 \pm 2.3\%$) while using 26\% fewer tokens than its initial harness. On EinsteinArena open problems, MILO improves best-known upper bounds for Erdős minimum-overlap ($0.3808586 \to 0.3808568$) and the first and third autocorrelation inequalities ($1.50274365 \to 1.50274360$; $1.45081 \to 1.44889$).

</details>


### [150] [Continual Learning of Dynamical Systems in Recurrent Neural Networks through Recyclable Unit Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38356)
*Sima Hashemi,Daniel Durstewitz,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRUG方法，通过可微门控和单位回收，在持续动态系统重构中实现高效容量利用与零遗忘，显著提升对非线性及混沌系统的建模能力，并在共享动态的任务间表现出更强的前向迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法在更新参数时易改变长期动态行为，且固定容量网络中任务专用单元分配过快导致资源耗尽，亟需一种能有效保存已有动态并高效复用容量的方法。

Method: 引入连续可回收单元门控（CRUG），利用基于L0惩罚的可微门控选择任务特定单元，未使用单元被回收用于后续任务；通过有向连接使后期任务可复用早期表示而不干扰已确定的动态。

Result: CRUG在重建精度与容量利用之间取得最佳平衡，实现零遗忘，可靠学习异质序列中的非线性与混沌系统，且前向迁移在共享相似动态的任务中更显著；其优势扩展至顺序认知任务。

Conclusion: CRUG是一种高效、可扩展的持续动态系统重构框架，通过智能单位回收与定向信息传递，实现了长期稳定的学习性能。

Abstract: Dynamical Systems Reconstruction (DSR) aims to infer models from observed time series that reproduce a system's qualitative long-term behavior. Continual DSR (cDSR) requires learning new systems while preserving previously learned dynamics, yet even small parameter updates in recurrent models can qualitatively alter their behavior over long autonomous rollouts. We benchmark established continual learning (CL) methods spanning parameter regularization, replay, and parameter isolation on the fully trainable and interpretable Almost-Linear RNN (AL-RNN). Parameter isolation preserves earlier dynamics most effectively, but excessive task-specific allocations can rapidly exhaust a fixed-size network. We therefore introduce Continually-Recyclable Unit-Gating (CRUG), which conserves capacity through compact allocation and forward transfer. Differentiable gates trained with an $L_0$-based penalty select task-specific units, while unused units are recycled for subsequent tasks. Directed connections allow later tasks to reuse earlier representations without affecting the dynamics of previously committed units. CRUG achieves the strongest reconstruction--capacity trade-off among the tested methods with zero forgetting and reliably learns a heterogeneous sequence of nonlinear and chaotic systems. Furthermore, we show that forward transfer is more pronounced and useful when tasks share similar underlying dynamics. Lastly, we demonstrate that CRUG's advantages extend beyond autonomous cDSR to sequential cognitive tasks.

</details>


### [151] [On the Off-Policy Teacher in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38360)
*Langlin Huang,Hao Liu,Mononito Goswami,Xinyu Li,Prithwith Jana,Nikos Kanakaris,Patrick Blöbaum,Purak Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCOUT proposes a co-training framework to address the asymmetry in on-policy distillation (OPD) by adapting the teacher to student-generated prefixes, improving continuation performance and overall distillation effectiveness across various configurations and domains.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation (OPD) suffers from a fundamental asymmetry: while trajectories are on-policy for the student, they are off-policy for the teacher. The teacher's ability to continue from student-generated prefixes degrades as prefix length increases, limiting OPD's effectiveness.

Method: SCOUT introduces periodic reinforcement learning updates with verifiable rewards to adapt the teacher's conditional ability. The teacher learns to generate continuations from student prefixes, enabling better supervision during OPD.

Result: Controlled experiments show SCOUT improves the teacher's ability to continue from student-generated prefixes. Across multiple setups, SCOUT consistently enhances the effectiveness of on-policy distillation.

Conclusion: SCOUT effectively mitigates the teacher's off-policy degradation in OPD by enabling student-conditioned teacher adaptation, leading to more robust and scalable distillation performance.

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as a promising post-training paradigm in which the student learns from trajectories generated by its own policy under dense teacher supervision. However, OPD introduces a fundamental asymmetry: although the sampled trajectories are on-policy for the student, they are off-policy for the teacher. The teacher is typically optimized to continue from prefixes generated by its own policy, but during OPD it must instead supervise prefixes generated by the student. Empirically, we find that its continuation performance degrades as these prefixes grow longer. To address this issue, we propose Student-COnditioned Updates of the Teacher (SCOUT), a co-training framework that adapts the teacher to student-generated prefixes. Alongside standard OPD updates, SCOUT periodically optimizes the teacher's conditional ability using reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where the teacher generates continuations from student prefixes and learns from outcome rewards. Controlled experiments show that SCOUT improves the teacher's ability to continue from student-generated prefixes, supporting the intended mechanism of student-conditioned teacher adaptation. Across multiple teacher--student configurations, model scales, and reasoning domains, SCOUT also consistently improves the effectiveness of on-policy distillation.

</details>


### [152] [Simulator-Refined Diffusion for Radio-Frequency Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38363)
*Jinhao Liang,Jacob K. Christopher,Michael Frei,Tommaso Dreossi,Nando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Simulator-Refined Diffusion (SRD)的新方法，结合低精度可微分代理模型与高精度非可微全波电磁仿真器，以解决扩散模型在印刷电路板（PCB）布局生成中难以满足定量电磁规格的问题。该方法利用代理模型的梯度指导采样过程中的扰动方向，并由仿真器在此方向上搜索有效设计更新，避免了传统零阶优化所需的大量随机扰动。实验表明，SRD在不同场景下均优于现有最先进方法，使生成布局的模拟S参数与目标值匹配度提高最多达21.2%（分布内）和19.8%（分布外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽在PCB逆向设计中展现潜力，但难以精确满足电磁性能要求。现有基于梯度的引导方法依赖于可微分代理模型或昂贵且不可微的全波仿真器，二者各有局限：前者不准确，后者计算成本高且无法提供梯度信息。因此需要一种高效且可靠的联合优化策略。

Method: 提出Simulator-Refined Diffusion (SRD)，将低精度可微分代理模型与高精度非可微全波电磁仿真器结合。在扩散采样过程中，使用代理模型的梯度确定扰动方向，再由全波仿真器沿该方向进行局部搜索，以找到有效的设计更新，从而实现高效、精准的优化。

Result: SRD在多种测试场景中表现优异，对分布内目标，生成布局的模拟S参数与目标匹配度比现有方法提升最多21.2%；对分布外目标，提升最多19.8%，显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过融合可微分代理模型与高精度仿真器，SRD实现了高效且精确的PCB布局逆向设计，在满足电磁性能指标方面具有显著优势，为复杂电子系统的设计自动化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown potential in inverse design of printed circuit boards (PCBs), enabling the generation of layouts conditioned on target S-parameters. Despite this promise, applying diffusion models to PCB layout generation remains challenging due to their difficulty in meeting the quantitative electromagnetic specifications. A common approach is gradient-based guidance, which biases the diffusion sampling process with the gradient of an objective used for evaluation. However, full-wave electromagnetic simulators are accurate but expensive and typically non-differentiable, whereas differentiable surrogates are informative but not always reliable. To address these limitations, this paper proposes Simulator-Refined Diffusion (SRD), a novel combination of a low-fidelity differentiable surrogate and a high-fidelity non-differentiable simulator within the diffusion sampling process. Unlike standard zeroth-order optimization, which requires a great number of random perturbations, our approach uses the surrogate's gradient to propose the perturbation direction while the simulator then searches based on this direction to identify an effective design update. Experimental results across different settings show that this method consistently outperforms current state-of-the-art methods, producing layouts whose simulated S-parameters match the target specifications up to 21.2% closer for in-distribution targets and up to 19.8% for out-of-distribution targets.

</details>


### [153] [Learning to Plan from Random Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38383)
*Deqian Kong,Guangyan Sun,Sheng Cheng,Sirui Xie,Bo Pang,Jianwen Xie,Tony Geng,Caiwen Ding,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了随机探索如何在目标未指定的情况下支持长距离规划，提出通过条件能量模型学习时间关系，并利用无监督方法训练模型以估计不同时间跨度下的状态转移概率。实验表明，该方法可在无需策略优化训练的情况下实现基于随机探索的长距离迷宫导航、具身导航及从次优数据中进行操作规划，所学得分场和嵌入探针表现出多尺度认知地图特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何在不依赖目标或奖励信号的情况下，利用随机探索经验实现长距离规划，从而构建无需策略改进训练的通用规划框架。

Method: 采用条件能量模型，通过噪声对比估计训练观察对之间的时序对数密度比，使用不同时间跨度的嵌入表示来建模时间关系；测试时结合局部动力学模型预测动作结果，并由时序模型评估进展，执行动作后重新规划。

Result: 在状态和图像输入下实现了长距离迷宫规划，验证了从随机探索中学习的有效性；得分场与嵌入探针表现出多尺度认知地图特性；成功应用于具身导航和从次优数据中的操作规划。

Conclusion: 仅通过随机探索积累的经验，结合无监督时序建模，即可实现高效、鲁棒的长距离规划，为构建自主、自适应智能体提供了新范式。

Abstract: Random exploration reveals how an environment can be traversed before a goal is specified. Can this experience support long-range planning without policy-improvement training? Our random-walk analysis explains what temporal relations contain: short horizons reveal geodesic geometry in the diffusion limit, while longer horizons reveal connectivity between regions before mixing removes these distinctions. We learn these relations with a conditional energy-based model that estimates temporal log-density ratios through horizon-conditioned embeddings. The model is trained on observation pairs by noise-contrastive estimation, without action or reward labels. The planner queries these learned relations at different horizons as it moves toward the goal. At test time, a separate local dynamics model predicts candidate action outcomes, and the temporal model evaluates their progress toward the goal by selecting or aggregating estimated improvements across horizons. The agent executes one action and replans with both models fixed. Experiments demonstrate long-range maze planning from random exploration using states and images. Learned score fields, embedding probes, and planned routes exhibit properties of a multiscale cognitive map. We further demonstrate egocentric navigation from random exploration and manipulation planning from suboptimal data.

</details>


### [154] [LeanPolish: Verified Supervision for Lean Proof Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38384)
*Pauline Bourigault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanPolish 是一个符号式 Lean 4 管道，释放了 33,402 个已接受的局部编辑和 65,596 个相同状态下的失败尝试，用于研究模型如何从验证监督中学习。通过分析发现，首次成功搜索可实现目标无关的完美排序，而教师选择的评估点会奖励简单删除操作。超越首次成功的继续菜单评估消除了排序捷径，训练后的排序器在 70.1% 的保留状态中选择了最佳候选，优于最强冻结基线（36.9%）。压缩方面，多次符号遍历将 miniF2F 节省率从 19.7% 提升至 27.5%，超过所测试的神经混合方法。验证的神经编辑对其他证明源也有帮助，但匹配的冻结模型对照表明其增益未必来自训练。监督确实提升了完整证明重写：微调后，在 19 个 PutnamBench 证明上验证的标记减少率从 2.8% 提升至 5.5%。整体上，释放的编辑、完整的候选池和受控评估使学习模仿搜索策略与改进该策略得以分离，为研究证明改进提供了可复现的基础，同时保持正确性、压缩性和编辑策略的独立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型生成的 Lean 证明在验证监督下的学习机制，特别是如何区分学习模仿搜索策略与实际改进搜索策略，并探索如何提升证明的正确性、压缩性和编辑效率。

Method: 提出 LeanPolish 符号管道，收集大量已接受和失败的本地编辑，构建完整候选池；设计受控实验评估不同训练策略在排序、压缩和全证明重写中的表现，包括对比冻结模型与微调模型的效果。

Result: 训练后的排序器在 70.1% 的保留状态中选出最优候选，显著优于冻结基线（36.9%）；压缩性能提升至 27.5%；全证明重写中验证标记减少率从 2.8% 提升至 5.5%；验证监督有助于改进，但部分增益可能不源于训练本身。

Conclusion: LeanPolish 提供了一个可复现的框架，能够有效分离学习模仿搜索策略与改进搜索策略，为证明生成的正确性、压缩性与编辑策略优化提供了清晰的评估基础。

Abstract: Verified proof edits offer a natural source of supervision for improving language-model-generated Lean proofs. Yet verification establishes that an edit is correct, not that its training signal is free of search artifacts. We introduce LeanPolish, a symbolic Lean 4 pipeline that releases 33,402 accepted local edits and 65,596 same-state failed attempts, and use it to study what models learn from this supervision. First-success search admits a goal-independent rule with perfect ranking accuracy; teacher-selected evaluation sites also reward trivial deletions. Continuing menu evaluation beyond the first success removes the ordering shortcut: a trained ranker selects the best candidate on 70.1% of evaluated held-out states, versus 36.9% for the strongest frozen baseline. For compression, iterating the symbolic pass raises miniF2F savings from 19.7% to 27.5%, exceeding the neural hybrids we test there. Verified neural editing helps on other proof sources, but matched frozen-model controls show that its gains need not come from training. The supervision does improve whole-proof rewriting: fine-tuning raises verified token reduction from 2.8% to 5.5% on 19 PutnamBench proofs. Together, the released edits, complete candidate pools, and controlled evaluations separate learning to imitate a search policy from improving on that search. They provide a reproducible basis for studying proof improvement while keeping correctness, compression, and edit policy distinct.

</details>


### [155] [Which Tasks Survive Self-Supervised Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38393)
*Achleshwar Luthra,Lucas Bryant,Tracy Zhu,Tomer Galanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了同实例自监督学习（SSL）中哪些下游任务信息能够被保留，并提出了“语义可恢复性”这一概念，用于衡量表示空间对任务后验信息的捕捉能力。研究表明，在中心化和白化表示下，可恢复性与方向性类距离归一化方差（CDNV）精确对应，控制少样本最近质心分类性能，并决定任务相关语义方向的强度。此外，理论分析表明，标准两视图SSL目标的最优解对应于双视图算子中稳定的主导模式，从而给出了可恢复性的闭式谱表征：下游任务的保留程度取决于其后验是否位于所选谱子空间中。实验在合成与真实数据集上验证了这些预测，涵盖多种SSL方法，证实了可恢复性、方向几何、谱结构与少样本迁移之间的预测关系。整体揭示了同实例SSL中信息保留的机制及其在下游任务中的表现形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索同实例自监督学习（SSL）中哪些下游任务信息可以被保留，理解表示学习如何影响任务可恢复性，以及这些信息在下游几何结构中的体现方式。

Method: 通过定义语义可恢复性，结合线性探针、质心轴对齐、因子化质心几何等方法，分析中心化和白化表示下的表示空间特性；进一步对典型两视图SSL目标进行谱分析，推导出可恢复性的闭式表达；最后在合成与真实数据集上验证理论预测。

Result: 语义可恢复性精确对应于方向性类距离归一化方差（CDNV），控制少样本分类性能，决定任务相关语义方向强度；两视图SSL的最优解对应于跨视图稳定模式的谱子空间；任务保留程度由其后验是否落在该子空间决定；实验证实了理论预测在多种数据集和方法上的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究从任务层面揭示了同实例自监督学习中信息保留的本质：可恢复性由谱结构决定，且以特定几何形式体现在下游任务中，为理解表示学习的语义保真度提供了理论框架与实证支持。

Abstract: Same-instance self-supervised learning (SSL) learns representations by enforcing consistency across two views of the same underlying instance. This principle alone, however, does not determine which downstream tasks remain recoverable from the learned representation. We study this question through \emph{semantic recoverability}, defined as the amount of a task's posterior score captured by the represented function space. We show that, for centered and whitened representations, recoverability exactly determines directional class-distance-normalized variance (CDNV), controls few-shot nearest-centroid classification, and governs the strength of task-relevant semantic directions. The population linear probe and centroid axis coincide, and multiple well-recovered tasks approach a factorial centroid geometry. We then analyze a canonical two-view SSL objective and show that its population optimum spans the leading cross-view-stable modes of the associated two-view operator. This yields a closed-form spectral characterization of semantic recoverability: a downstream task is preserved to the extent that its posterior lies in the selected spectral subspace. We validate these predictions on synthetic and real datasets across several SSL methods, testing the predicted relationships among recoverability, directional geometry, spectral structure, and few-shot transfer. Together, these results give a task-level account of what information survives same-instance SSL and how the retained information appears in downstream geometry and transfer.

</details>


### [156] [Disagreement-Regularized Imitation Learning for Image-Based Continuous Control with Gaussian and Beta Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38407)
*Irving Giovani Bronzatti Petrazzini,Eric Aislan Antonelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIL improves few-demonstration image-based continuous control by using disagreement among policies as a reward, with significant gains over behavior cloning; however, its advantage diminishes with more demonstrations, where Beta-based behavior cloning performs better. The effectiveness of disagreement rewards depends on learner representation and training stage.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Behavior cloning accumulates errors when encountering out-of-distribution states; DRIL aims to mitigate this by leveraging policy disagreement as a reinforcement learning signal to improve generalization.

Method: A controlled CarRacing study tests DRIL using Gaussian and Beta policies, with demonstrations from clipped Gaussian or bounded Beta experts, varying trajectory counts (1 or 20), evaluation types (deterministic/stochastic), and checkpoints (BC, top-10-episode, final DRIL). An ensemble of five Gaussian policies measures disagreement.

Result: DRIL achieves up to 112% improvement over best BC in few-demonstration settings with bounded-action demos; gains reduce with 20 trajectories, where Beta BC outperforms DRIL by ~7%. Disagreement reward informativeness varies with learner type and training stage.

Conclusion: DRIL significantly enhances few-demonstration visual continuous control, while Beta policies offer strong baseline performance with ample data. Success depends on learner design, ensemble diversity, and checkpoint selection.

Abstract: Purpose: Behavior cloning can accumulate errors when a learned controller visits states outside the demonstrated distribution. This study evaluates whether Disagreement-Regularized Imitation Learning (DRIL), which converts disagreement among cloned policies into a reinforcement-learning reward, improves image-based continuous control. Methods: A controlled CarRacing study combines Gaussian and Beta learner policies, demonstrations from either a clipped Gaussian expert or an intrinsically bounded Beta expert, one or 20 trajectories, deterministic and stochastic evaluation, and three retained stages: behavior cloning, the highest 10-episode training-score checkpoint, and the final DRIL checkpoint. The disagreement ensemble contains five Gaussian policies in every variant. Each retained policy is evaluated over 100 procedurally generated episodes. Results: Score-selected DRIL produced its largest gains in the few-demonstration setting, improving over the strongest behavior-cloning mean by 61% with clipped-action demonstrations and by 112% with bounded-action demonstrations. With 20 trajectories, the advantage of DRIL narrowed; in the bounded-action regime, Beta behavior cloning remained about 7% above the best DRIL checkpoint. The experiments also show that the informativeness of the disagreement reward changes with the learner representation and training stage. Conclusion: DRIL can substantially improve few-demonstration visual continuous control, while bounded Beta policies provide strong behavior-cloning performance when more demonstrations are available. The results highlight the joint importance of learner support,ensemble response, and checkpoint selection.

</details>


### [157] [Synthesis Without Training: An Inference-Only Pipeline for Tabular, Temporal, and Relational Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38414)
*Zilong Zhao,Abdul Raheem,Jiayu Li,Sohei Arisaka,Darius Lim Hong Yi,Milad Abdollahzadeh,Uzair Javaid,Biplab Sikdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GENSCRIPT 是一种无需训练的合成数据生成管道，通过计算源数据的确定性统计概要并传递给语言模型以推断字段语义和跨列完整性约束，进而编译成可执行、可审计的采样器。该方法统一支持单表、时间序列和关系型数据，无需任务特定建模，在多个基准测试中表现出色，生成速度快且保持高保真度，同时完美保留了数据结构关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据生成方法依赖于训练-采样范式，面临每次数据集需重新训练、不同数据模态需特定模型与特征工程、模型行为不透明等问题，限制了其通用性和可解释性。

Method: GENSCRIPT 提出一种推理仅有的流程：首先计算源数据的统计概要（如列类型、范围、缺失值、类别、相关性等），然后将该概要传递给语言模型以推断字段语义和跨列约束；再由编码代理将概要和约束编译为可执行、可审计的采样器。

Result: 在四个单表基准测试中，GENSCRIPT 在 2 分钟内构建生成器，并在 6 秒内生成 5 万行数据，边际保真度接近领先方法；在 Adult 数据集中完美保持 1 对 1 列映射；在智能建筑数据集上生成的时间序列更贴近真实分布，并完美保留了主键和外键关系。

Conclusion: GENSCRIPT 通过消除模型训练，实现了高效、通用、可解释的合成数据生成，适用于多种数据模态，显著提升了合成数据的质量与可控性。

Abstract: Synthetic data generation is dominated by the fit-then-sample paradigm: a generative model is trained on a private dataset and then sampled from. Despite its widespread adoption, this paradigm faces three challenges: (1) a new training run is required for every dataset; (2) different data modalities, such as single tables, time series, and relational databases, require task-specific models and feature engineering; and (3) the resulting model is opaque, making its behavior under data constraints difficult to inspect. We propose GENSCRIPT, an inference-only pipeline that eliminates model training. GENSCRIPT computes a deterministic statistical profile of the source data (column types, ranges, missingness, categories, correlations, etc.) and passes it--rather than raw rows--to a language model to infer field semantics and cross-column integrity constraints. A coding agent then compiles the profile and constraints into an executable, auditable sampler. This unified approach supports single-table, temporal, and relational data without task-specific modeling. Across four single-table benchmarks, GENSCRIPT builds generators in 2 minutes and samples 50k rows within 6 seconds, while remaining within a few points of leading methods in marginal fidelity. Notably, it is the only method that perfectly preserves a 1-to-1 mapping between columns in the Adult dataset. On a smart-building dataset, it produces conditional time series that more closely match the real distribution than two baselines and perfectly preserves primary- and foreign-key relationships in the corresponding relational database.

</details>


### [158] [Graph Anomaly Detection as Finite-Horizon Control: Training-Free Scoring via Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38424)
*Fred Xu,Thomas Markovich,Florence Regol,Yizhou Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EB-GAD 是一种无需训练的图异常检测框架，通过建模图感知的广义奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克（GOU）过程来定义正常性，利用经验贝叶斯估计图精度，并将异常评分转化为闭式有限时域控制能量。该方法可生成多种评分策略，共享同一先验，具有良好的稳定性和泛化能力。在11个基准数据集上无标签条件下表现优异，9个达到最佳或并列最佳，尤其在金融欺诈网络中显著领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测模型通过架构、消息传递、重构或对比目标间接编码正常性，导致图信任度、图谱加权与异常评分选择相互纠缠，造成评分成本高、不透明且在不同图环境下不稳定。

Method: 提出EB-GAD框架，基于图滤波模板的广义奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克（GOU）松弛模型，使用经验贝叶斯从残差场似然中拟合图精度；异常评分转化为最小控制能量，即从特征中性节点到观测终点沿图谱松弛所需的最小努力。通过调整松弛时域和终点容忍度生成一组评分，并采用无标签选择器根据特征同质性、边密度和特征维度选择最优评分族。

Result: 在11个基准数据集上无需任何标签，EB-GAD在9个数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳的AUROC，尤其在4个金融欺诈网络（最大达370万节点）、YelpChi、Amazon评论、Weibo、Reddit和Facebook上性能显著提升，最高优势达21.7个百分点；在BlogCatalog和ACM上位列第二。

Conclusion: EB-GAD是一种高效、稳定、可解释的无训练图异常检测框架，能够有效识别传统静态均衡评分所忽略的异常，适用于大规模复杂图结构，在无监督场景下表现出卓越性能。

Abstract: Node-level graph anomaly detection (GAD) identifies nodes whose attributes and interactions deviate from dominant graph regularities. Existing GAD models encode normality and anomaly scoring indirectly through architectures, message passing, reconstruction or contrastive objectives, and tuned score families. This entangles graph trust (how strongly graph structure should define normality), graph-spectral weighting, and anomaly-score choice, yielding scores that are costly, opaque, and unstable across graph regimes. We propose EB-GAD (Empirical-Bayes GAD), a training-free framework that models normality as graph-aware generalized Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (GOU) relaxation toward a graph-filtered template. Empirical Bayes fits the graph precision from the residual-field likelihood; the GOU then turns scoring into a closed-form finite-horizon control energy, the minimum effort to steer a feature-neutral node to its observed endpoint along graph-spectral relaxation. Sweeping relaxation horizon and endpoint tolerance yields a bank of scores that share one fitted prior: equilibrium Mahalanobis scoring is one limit, while finite-horizon control-energy and scale-normalized ratio scores reveal anomalies that static equilibrium scoring can mask. A label-free selector chooses the score family from feature homophily, edge density, and feature dimension, then ranks candidates by fitted-null deviation and rank stability. On 11 benchmarks and without labels at any step, EB-GAD has the best or tied-best AUROC on 9: the four financial fraud networks (up to 3.7M nodes), the YelpChi and Amazon review graphs, Weibo, Reddit and Facebook, with margins of up to 21.7 points. It is second on BlogCatalog and ACM.

</details>


### [159] [What Pretraining and Midtraining Make Learnable from Rewards?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38446)
*Chiwun Yang,Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了预训练和中期训练如何提供信息与计算，以使奖励适应有效。通过序列状态计算和上下文记忆，识别出在所有训练奖励上一致但对未见输入需求不同答案的机制。任务无关的源观察可解决此歧义。研究构建了从特定随机初始化经由源预测和奖励适应的有限采样Adam路径，证明了预测如何获取执行或检索能力，以及奖励如何学习其任务特定用途。实验使用预训练的Qwen2.5检查点，在八个世界中，使用正确源和首操作监督的序列模型达到82.61%成功率，而私有随机源对照组仅为44.15%。记忆回放保留了奖励适应中的检索能力，独立八世界验证获得75.32%任务成功率，对比匹配的替代检索训练仅49.86%。GSM8K和HotpotQA显示奖励输入时的准确率、后续提升及最终性能的分离。这些结果连接了信息获取、可执行计算与奖励引导的任务学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解预训练和中期训练如何支持奖励适应的有效性，特别是在信息获取与计算分配上的作用。

Method: 通过构造有限采样Adam路径，从随机初始化出发，经历源预测和奖励适应，分析预测如何获得执行或检索能力，以及奖励如何学习任务特定用途。结合实验验证在不同设置下的表现差异。

Result: 在八个世界中，正确源与首操作监督的序列模型成功率达到82.61%，远高于私有随机源对照组的44.15%；记忆回放下独立验证达75.32%成功率，显著优于替代检索训练的49.86%；GSM8K和HotpotQA显示奖励输入时准确率、后续增益与最终性能的分离。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了信息获取、可执行计算与奖励引导任务学习之间的紧密联系，表明源观察与奖励机制在训练过程中分工明确，共同促进模型性能提升。

Abstract: A reward can identify a correct answer while leaving the computation needed for new inputs undetermined. We study how pretraining and midtraining supply the information and computation that make reward adaptation effective. In sequential state computation and contextual memory, we characterize mechanisms that agree on every training reward yet demand different held-out answers. Task-independent source observations resolve this ambiguity. We construct finite sampled Adam paths from specified random initializations through source prediction and reward adaptation in the same parameters, proving how prediction acquires execution or retrieval and rewards learn their task-specific use. Experiments with pretrained Qwen2.5 checkpoints test this division of labor. Across eight worlds, Sequential models trained with correct source and first-operation supervision reach 82.61% success, versus 44.15% for a private-random source control. Memory replay preserves retrieval during reward adaptation, and an independent eight-world confirmation achieves 75.32% task success versus 49.86% after matched alternative-retrieval training. GSM8K and HotpotQA separate accuracy at reward entry, subsequent gain and final performance. Together, these results connect information acquisition, executable computation and reward-guided task learning.

</details>


### [160] [An Input-Frugal Deep Learning Framework for Weather-Driven National Crop-Yield Forecasting: A Case Study of Brazilian Soybean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38447)
*Fernando Dupin da Cunha Mello,Prashant Kumar,Erick G. Sperandio Nascimento*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种低成本、可迁移、架构无关的深度学习框架，仅使用常规天气数据及两个轻量级静态输入（作物年份和农业环境标签），实现对巴西大豆产量的高精度预测。在20个生长季（2001/02–2020/21）的案例研究中，所有深度学习模型均优于线性岭回归和五年的移动平均基线模型，其中Transformer表现最佳（全国RMSE 149 kg ha⁻¹，rRMSE 5.3%，R² = 0.784），误差较基线降低47.6%。季节内预测精度随时间递增，晚期预测误差比基线低约50%。消融实验表明，农业环境标签和空间实例扩展（每区县-年份多个网格序列）提升性能但不增加输入复杂度。SHAP分析显示，作物年份主导长期趋势，而季节内天气与环境上下文驱动年际波动，其中水分/云量和热需求变量起主要作用。该框架易于部署于其他作物和区域，且可无缝对接操作型天气预报，支持常态化监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作物产量预测方法多依赖昂贵或难以扩展的数据输入，缺乏通用性和可操作性。本研究旨在开发一种低成本、可迁移、无需复杂输入的深度学习框架，以提升预测可靠性与时效性，同时适应不同地理区域和作物类型，支持日常监测与风险管理。

Method: 采用仅依赖常规天气数据及两个轻量级静态输入（作物年份和农业环境标签）的深度学习框架，支持多种序列编码器（MLP、CNN、LSTM、CNN-LSTM、Transformer、Mamba），通过留一季交叉验证进行评估。利用空间实例扩展（多个网格节点天气序列对应每个区县-年份）增强区域异质性建模能力，并结合SHAP分析解释模型决策机制。

Result: 所有深度学习模型均优于线性岭回归和五年的移动平均基线；序列编码器普遍优于非序列的MLP；Transformer在国家尺度上表现最优（RMSE 149 kg ha⁻¹，rRMSE 5.3%，R² = 0.784），相对基线误差降低47.6%。季节内预测精度持续提升，最晚预测点误差比基线低约50%。农业环境标签和空间实例扩展显著提升性能，且不增加输入复杂度。作物年份解释长期趋势，季节内天气与环境上下文主导年际波动，水分/云量与热需求变量为主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 所提出的深度学习框架具有低成本、可迁移、易部署的特点，适用于多种作物和地理区域，能有效融合常规气象数据与轻量级上下文信息，在保持低输入复杂度的同时实现高精度、动态更新的作物产量预测，具备良好的实际应用前景与操作兼容性。

Abstract: Reliable, timely crop-yield forecasts are essential for market stability and risk management, yet many approaches rely on costly or hard-to-scale inputs. We present a frugal, transferable, and architecture-agnostic deep learning framework that uses routine weather as the only time-varying input plus two lightweight static context inputs (crop year and an agro-environmental label) to capture long-run change and regional heterogeneity, while supporting multiple sequence encoders under identical data requirements. Using a 20-season Brazilian soybean case study (2001/02-2020/21) with leave-one-year-out cross-validation, we benchmark MLP, CNN, LSTM, CNN-LSTM, a Transformer encoder and the Mamba state-space model against linear ridge regression and a five-year moving-average "farmer" baseline. All deep learning variants outperform ridge, and all sequential encoders surpass the non-sequential MLP. The Transformer achieves the best national accuracy (RMSE 149 kg ha^-1; rRMSE 5.3%; R^2 = 0.784), reducing error by 47.6% relative to the farmer baseline. In-season forecasts improve monotonically from early- to late-season issuance, reaching approximately 50% lower error than the baseline at the latest forecast point. Ablations indicate that the agro-environmental label and spatial instance expansion (multiple grid-node weather sequences per municipality-year) contribute positively without increasing input complexity. SHAP diagnostics suggest crop year explains most of the long-run trajectory, whereas within-season weather and agro-environmental context primarily drive interannual deviations, with moisture/cloud and thermal-demand variables dominating. Overall, the framework is straightforward to deploy across other crops and geographic regions and is naturally compatible with operational weather forecasts for routine monitoring.

</details>


### [161] [Grokking through the Lens of Minimum-Norm Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38453)
*Gil Kur,Ileana Rugina,Clémentine Carla Juliette Dominé,Marco Mondelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统计理论，用于理解高维回归中正则化几何和信号稀疏性如何影响模型在过拟合边界附近的泛化能力。研究揭示了稀疏性促进正则化可使精确插值比近似拟合更准确，并在无噪声强过参数化情况下证明了零-一泛化律。通过分析不同范数的正则化路径，发现泛化增益随正则化越稀疏、目标越稀疏而增加，且在特定条件下出现急剧下降。实验验证了理论在对角线线性网络与Transformer上的适用性。此外，还发现了最小范数插值存在统计不稳定性：微小的正则化强度扰动可能导致显著不同的泛化表现，但训练误差保持较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论难以定量解释延迟泛化现象如何依赖归纳偏置和信号结构，尤其在高维回归中，需要更深入理解正则化与信号稀疏性对泛化的影响机制。

Method: 构建统计理论框架，分析高维回归中的正则化几何与信号稀疏性；使用凸范数构造插值器，推导零-一泛化律；研究样本量与特征维度成比例时的ℓ_r-正则化路径，量化泛化增益；进行数值实验验证理论预测。

Result: 证明了在无噪声强过参数化情形下存在零-一泛化律；揭示了正则化越稀疏、目标越稀疏，泛化增益越大；发现ℓ_1正则化在无噪声下会导致泛化性能骤降；实验表明理论适用于对角线线性网络和Transformer；揭示最小范数插值具有统计不稳定性。

Conclusion: 本工作为延迟泛化现象提供了定量理解，强调了正则化几何与信号稀疏性的关键作用，并揭示了最小范数插值的敏感性，有助于指导实际模型设计与训练策略。

Abstract: Grokking shows that fitting the training data and learning the underlying signal can occur at very different stages. However, existing theories offer limited quantitative insight into how this delayed generalization depends on inductive bias and signal structure. Our work addresses the gap by developing a statistical theory that characterizes how regularization geometry and signal sparsity govern generalization near interpolation. In particular, we focus on the prototypical setting of high-dimensional regression and identify regimes in which sparsity-promoting regularization makes exact interpolation much more accurate than approximate fitting. In strongly overparameterized noiseless problems, we prove a zero--one generalization law and construct a family of convex norms whose interpolators transition from the trivial risk of the all-zero predictor to exact recovery, while keeping the training error equal to $0$. Furthermore, when feature dimension and sample size are proportional, we provide a precise characterization of training and generalization errors along $\ell_r$-regularization paths. This in turn allows us to quantify the generalization gain that remains near interpolation: we show that this gain increases as the norm becomes more sparsity-promoting and as the target becomes sparser, with a sharp drop in generalization reached for noiseless data and $\ell_1$ regularization. Experiments on diagonal linear networks and transformers trained on modular arithmetic demonstrate the generality of our theoretical predictions. Finally, beyond grokking, our work reveals a statistical instability in minimum-norm interpolation: small perturbations in the regularization strength can lead to drastically different generalization, while preserving small training error.

</details>


### [162] [Does Gradient Conflict Predict the Understanding--Generation Trade-off? A Controlled Audit of Conflict-Metric Validity in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38465)
*Shuyang Jiang,Fucheng Deng,Yuchuan Luo,Zhenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建可控测试平台GRIDUMM，系统评估了统一多模态模型（UMMs）中理解与生成目标间的梯度冲突作为诊断指标的有效性。在63种配置和372个检查点的实验中，所有方向性冲突度量与最终的性能权衡之间均未表现出显著相关性（Spearman相关系数<0.3且置信区间不包含零）。进一步的剂量-反应干预表明，即使单调抑制冲突，性能权衡也保持平坦，揭示了相关性不等于因果性。功能干扰度量优于方向性冲突指标，而训练损失则与权衡表现强相关。研究强调，冲突度量的有效性需实证验证而非假设成立，并公开发布审计协议以供复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型的设计常依赖于梯度冲突作为理解与生成目标间权衡的指示器，但这一假设尚未经过直接验证。为评估冲突度量是否真正反映实际性能权衡，需要一个可控制、可计算真实权衡的测试环境。

Method: 提出并构建了名为GRIDUMM的可控测试平台，模拟UMM训练中的关键结构特征，同时使真实冲突与性能权衡可精确计算。通过系统改变训练配置，测量63种不同设置下的多个检查点，分析多种冲突度量与最终理解-生成权衡的关系，并进行剂量-反应干预实验以区分相关性与因果性。

Result: 所有方向性冲突度量与最终性能权衡之间的相关性极弱（最大Spearman相关系数<0.3，置信区间不包含零），无法有效预测权衡表现；抑制冲突并未改善或改变权衡结果，表明相关性不具因果意义。功能干扰度量表现更优，而训练损失与权衡高度相关。

Conclusion: 梯度冲突并非天然有效的诊断工具，其有效性必须通过实证建立，不能默认成立。研究提供了可重复使用的审计协议，推动对模型训练机制的透明化评估。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) are increasingly designed around gradient conflict between understanding and generation objectives. The premise that reducing these metrics improves the downstream understanding-generation trade-off has never been tested directly. We audit it in a controlled testbed, GRIDUMM, which mirrors key structural ingredients of UMM training while making the ground-truth trade-off exactly computable. Across 63 configurations and 372 measured checkpoints, no directional conflict metric reaches an absolute Spearman correlation of 0.3 with a confidence interval excluding zero for conflict measured during training against the eventual trade-off. A dose-response intervention that monotonically suppresses conflict leaves the trade-off flat, separating correlation from causation. The norm ratio is a generation-failure detector and becomes null among configurations that master generation. Functional interference measures outperform directional conflict metrics, while training loss tracks the trade-off strongly. Our results do not show that conflict is useless; they show that its validity as a diagnostic target must be established, not assumed, and we release the audit protocol as a reusable standard.

</details>


### [163] [Role-guided Speaker Deletion Verification in Clinical Psychiatry Speech Recordings with Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38491)
*Joseph T Colonel,Daniel Katzman,Kelsey Kirker,Adam N Davidson,Shalaila S Haas,Cheryl Corcoran,René S Kahn,Guillermo Checci,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对精神科临床对话中多说话人录音的去标识化验证问题，提出两种对称的自动化管道：分别验证医生和患者语音是否被成功移除。通过使用音频-语言模型与大语言模型对残留语音进行扫描，结合四种开源模型（Gemma-4-12B、Gemma-4-31B、Nemotron-3-Nano、Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni）在48段双人对话数据上测试，采用14种模型视角组合的并集集成方法，获得F1值0.478（精确率0.330，召回率0.870），显著优于单个模型表现，体现模型间互补性与多视角分析的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床精神科研究依赖大规模口语数据识别生物标志物，但随着隐私保护要求提高，需从多说话人录音中移除特定角色语音，并在大规模数据上验证删除效果，手动审查不可行，亟需自动化验证方法。

Method: 构建两个对称的验证管道，分别针对医生和患者语音的移除情况；先对目标角色语音进行红标处理，再利用音频-语言模型与大语言模型分析剩余音频，检测未被删除的语音片段；评估时采用四种开放权重模型在推理模式下运行，通过14种模型-视角组合的或逻辑集成提升检测性能。

Result: 在48段精神病学对话数据上，基于14种模型-视角配置的并集集成方法达到F1值0.478（精确率0.330，召回率0.870），显著高于单个模型表现，表明不同模型与上下文视角之间具有显著互补性，尤其提升了召回率。

Conclusion: 所提出的双通道自动化验证框架能有效应对大规模精神科录音中角色语音移除的验证挑战，多模型集成策略显著增强检测能力，为符合伦理与合规要求的数据处理提供可行解决方案。

Abstract: Clinical research in psychiatry increasingly relies on large scale collection of spoken language data to identify acoustic and linguistic biomarkers. Yet evolving consent and protocol requirements can oblige investigators to remove a designated speaker from multi-speaker recordings and to verify said removal at a scale infeasible for manual review of entire corpora. We study this verification problem for role-driven dyadic clinical dialogue in psychiatry and investigate it with two parallel, symmetric pipelines: confirming that clinician speech has been removed from psychiatric interview recordings, and confirming that patient speech has been removed from the same recordings. Each pipeline redacts the raw audio for its target role and then scans the surviving output with audio-language and large-language models to identify missed deletions. We evaluate this approach on a corpus of 48 dyadic recordings drawn from psychiatry settings, testing four open-weight models in an inference-only setting: Gemma-4-12B, Gemma-4-31B, Nemotron-3-Nano, and Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni. A disjunctive OR ensemble over fourteen model-view configurations had a combined F1 of 0.478 (precision 0.330, recall 0.870), an improvement over individual model estimates driven by recall gains that point to substantial complementarity across models and context views.

</details>


### [164] [Autoregressive Frontier Expansion: Growing Trees with Graph Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38506)
*Umer Gupta,Saku Peltonen,Martin Ritzert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为自回归前缘扩展的生成框架，通过模拟真实树木的生物生长过程，迭代地构建树状结构。该方法利用SO(2)等变图神经网络与流匹配模型，预测每个活跃分支是否分叉或终止，从而生成逼真的树状形态。在皮层神经元和植物树形的无条件、类别条件及形态引导生成任务中均表现出色，生成结果与参考分布高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形态特定模型在拓扑生成上存在约束或依赖手工调参的机制性流程；而通用3D图生成器未能充分利用或强制树状结构特征。因此需要一种既能保留树状结构特性又具备灵活性的生成方法。

Method: 提出自回归前缘扩展框架，基于流匹配模型与SO(2)等变图神经网络，逐步扩展前缘节点，决定分支是否分叉或终止，模拟自然生长过程。

Result: 在皮层神经元和植物树形数据集上，生成的形态在无条件、类别条件和形态引导任务中均与真实数据分布高度一致，且能准确匹配指定目标形态。

Conclusion: 所提出的自回归前缘扩展框架能够有效生成符合生物学特性的树状结构，在多种条件下表现优异，为复杂生物结构的建模与数据增强提供了新范式。

Abstract: Tree-like branching structures are common in nature, from botanical trees to neurons, blood vessels and respiratory trees. Their branching shape often reflects function, making structural modelling central to understanding how these systems work.
